**The determination to please the Lord through the performance of one’s natural duties is a secondary path of devotion for one engaged in work (karma).**

## The indirect devotional path of work for the ritualist

*karmī varṇāśrame thāki sādhu-saṅga kari \|*  
*karma mājhe bhakti kare gauṇa-patha dhari \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That karmi who remains within Varnashram and associates with devotees, engaging in bhakti while performing prescribed duties, takes the indirect route.**

*tāra kṛta karma saba hṛdaya śodhiyā \|*  
*tirohita haya śraddhā bīje sthāna diyā \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All these activities purify his heart and then disappear, leaving a place there for the seed of faith to be planted.**

## The indirect devotional path for the philosopher

*jñānī sukṛtira bale bhaktera kṛpāya \|*  
*ananya bhaktite śraddhā bīje sthāna diyā \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The philosopher, through his own pious deeds and the mercy of a devotee, spontaneously develops faith in the path of exclusive devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

The secondary or indirect path of devotion for the philosopher takes place in association with saintly devotees. In His conversation with Ramananda Raya, Lord Chaitanya Mahaprabhu rejected both these secondary paths as “superficial.”

*tumi bala—mora dāsa māyāra vipāke \|*  
*cāhe anya tuccha phala chāḍiyā āmāke \|\|65\|\|*  
*āmi jāni tāra yāte haya sumaṅgala \|*  
*bhukti mukti chāḍāiyā di-i bhakti phala \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! You say, “Due to entanglement in illusion, My servant has turned away from Me and now desires other, unimportant things. I know what is truly good for him; therefore I will make him give up sense enjoyment and liberation and give him devotion.**

## The way the indirect paths work

*tāra kāma anusāre cālāñā tāhāre*  
*gauṇa-patha bhakti-mārge śraddhā di-i tāre \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I engage him on the indirect path, in accordance with his desires, gradually bestowing on him faith in the path of direct devotional service.”**

*e tomāra kṛpā prabhu tumi kṛpāmaya \|*  
*kṛpā nā karile kise jīva śuddha haya \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, all this is Your mercy. You are truly all-compassionate! If You withheld Your mercy, how could we jivas ever be purified?**

## The deterioration of the indirect paths in the Age of Kali

*satya-yuge dhyāna-yoge kata ṛṣi-gaṇe \|*  
*śuddha kari’ dile prabhu nija-bhakti-dhane \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the Age of Truth, O Lord, You purified so many seers in their discipline of meditation, giving them the treasure of devotion.**

*tretā-yuge yajña-karme aneka śodhile \|*  
*dvāpare arcana-mārge bhakti bilāile \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the second age, You purified many through the performance of sacrifice, and in the third, You distributed devotion through temple worship.**

*kali āgamane nātha jīvera durdaśā \|*  
*dekhi jñāna karma yoga chāḍila bharasā \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Master! With the arrival of the age of Kali, You saw the jivas’ misfortune and abandoned all hope in the paths of works, knowledge or yoga.**

*alpa āyu, bahu pīḍā, bala-buddhi-hrāsa \|*  
*ei saba upadrava jīve kaila grāsa \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this age, we lead short lives of great misery, are weak and less intelligent, and are swallowed up by so many disturbances and disruptions.**

*varṇāśrama dharma āra sāṅkhya yoga jñāna \|*  
*kali-jīve uddhārite nahe balavān \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Varnashram dharma, Sankhya, yoga practice, and the cultivation of knowledge are incapable of delivering the jivas of this age from their bondage.**

*jñāna-karma-gata ye bhaktira gauṇa-patha \|*  
*kaṇṭake saṅkīrṇa hañā haila vipatha \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The indirect paths of devotion through works and knowledge are so full of traps and thorns that they have become no path at all.**

**Комментарий:**

The two indirect paths leading to the Temple of Devotion, through the cultivation of knowledge and associating with genuine devotees on the one hand and through discipline and selfless action offered to the Supreme Lord on the other, have become contaminated in the Age of Kali. Genuine devotees are in short supply, while hypocrisy is rife. People engaged in works seek only sense gratification and have no interest in self-purification. As a result, there is no benefit in engaging in the indirect paths to perfection. Even the path of temple worship, which was recommended in the Dvapara Age, has also been almost completely polluted by wicked practices.

*pṛthak upāya dhari upeya sādhane \|*  
*vighna bahutara haila jīvera jīvane \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking up means other than bhakti to reach the supreme end of life fills the lives of the jivas with more and more obstacles.**

**Комментарий:**

The words used here are *upāya* (“means”) and *upeya* (“ends”). Synonyms for these words are *sādhanā* (“spiritual practices”) and *sādhya* (“goal of spiritual practice”)*.* The spiritual practices used are the means, while for every living being the goal of these practices is the mercy of the Supreme Lord, Bhagavan Sri Krishna. Karma and jnana are not the primary means for attaining this end. This is because as soon as one approaches this goal of Devotion, they both disappear completely. This is not, however, true for the chanting of the Holy Name, which is in no way different from the Supreme Lord. This means that the Holy Name is present both as the means and end of spiritual life. It is both the spiritual practice and the goal of that practice. Only the most fortunate are capable of fully understanding this.

## The principal path is that of cultivating the Holy Name

*prabhu tumi jīvera maṅgala cintā kari’ \|*  
*kali yuge nāma saṅge svayaṁ avatari’ \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! You always have the jiva’s welfare in mind; thus in this Age of Kali, You descended into this world, bringing Your holy name with You.**

*yuga dharma pracārile nāma saṅkīrtana \|*  
*mukhya pathe jīva pāya kṛṣṇa prema dhana \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You preached the chanting of the Holy Name as the religious duty for this age. By following this as the main means, one attains the treasure of love for Krishna.**

*nāmera smaraṇe āra nāma-saṅkīrtana \|*  
*ei mātra dharma jīva karibe pālana \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You told everyone that the only religious duty the jiva must respect is to remember the Holy Name and to chant the Holy Name.**

## The Holy Name’s preeminence comes from being both the means and end, both the practice and goal of the practice

*yei ta sādhana sei sādhya yabe haila \|*  
*upāya upeya madhye bheda nā rahila \|\|79\|\|*  
*sādhyera sādhane āra nāhi antarāya \|*  
*anāyāse tare jīva tomāra kṛpāya \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the spiritual practice is itself the goal of that practice, when there is no difference between the means and the end, when nothing separates the spiritual practice from its goal, then, by Your mercy, the jiva easily crosses over samsara.**

*āmi ta adhama ati majiyā viṣaye \|*  
*nā bhajinu nāma tava ati mūḍha haye \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am most lowly and I am always absorbed in mundane thoughts; I am such a fool that I have never worshiped Your holy name.**

*dara dara dhārā cakṣe brahma-haridāsa \|*  
*paḍila prabhura pade chāḍiyā niḥśvāsa \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he spoke these words, tears began to trickle down Brahma Hari Das’ cheek, and with a long sigh, he fell down at Lord Chaitanya’s feet.**

*hari bhakta bhakti mātre vinoda yāhāra \|*  
*harināma cintāmaṇi jīvana tāhāra \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name, this divine, wish-fulfilling jewel, is the life of Bhaktivinoda, whose only pleasure lies in the Supreme Lord, His devotees and devotional service.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the first chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*,“Introducing the glories of the Holy Name.”

## 2 - Taking up the Holy Name

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā jīvana*  
*sītādvaita jaya śrīvāsādi-bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to Sita Devi and Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to Srivasa Pandit and all the devotees!**

*mahā-preme hari-dāsa karena rodana \|*  
*preme tāre gauracandra dilā āliṅgana \|\|2\|\|*  
*balena tomāra sama bhakta kothā āra \|*  
*sarva-tattva-jñātā tumi sadā māyā-pāra \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overcome by divine loving ecstasy, Hari Das wept and the golden Lord embraced him with the same ecstatic love. He said, “There is no devotee like you anywhere. You know all the truths of spiritual life and are beyond illusion.**

## Single-minded, exclusive bhajan is the best

*nica-kule avatari dekhāle sakale \|*  
*dhane māne kule śīle kṛṣṇa nāhi mile \|\|4\|\|*  
*ananya bhajane yāṅra śraddhā atiśaya \|*  
*devatā apekṣā śreṣṭha sei mahāśaya \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You took birth in a lowly family to show us all that neither wealth, nor prestige, nor noble blood can win Krishna’s heart. One who has strong faith in exclusive bhajan of the Lord is a great person, better than even the gods.**

## Hari Das, the teacher of the Holy Name

*nāma-tattva sarva-sāra tomāra vidita \|*  
*ācāre ācārya tumi pracāre paṇḍita \|\|6\|\|*  
*bala haridāsa nāma-mahimā apāra \|*  
*śuniyā tomāra mukhe ānanda āmāra \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You possess the essence of all spiritual truth, an understanding of the Holy Name; In your practice, you are exemplary; in your preaching, a scholar. So tell me more, O Hari Das, of the limitless glories of the Holy Name, for it gives Me great joy to hear these things from you.**

## Vaishnava characteristics

*eka nāma yāra mukhe vaiṣṇava se haya \|*  
*tāre gṛhī yatna kari mānibe niścaya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“One who utters the Holy Name but once is called a Vaishnava. A householder should take care to give a proper welcome to such a person.**

**Комментарий:**

Mahaprabhu explained the division of Vaishnavas to the residents of Kulina Gram. This has been quoted by Krishna Das in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*: The Lord said, “Serve Krishna, serve the Vaishnavas and constantly chant the Holy Name.” (CC 2.15.104) “If I hear someone chant Krishna’s holy name just once, I consider him to be worshipable and the best of all humankind.” (CC 2.15.106) “A person who is always chanting the holy name of the Lord is to be considered a first class Vaishnava. Worship his lotus feet.” (CC 2.16.72) “One whose very sight brings the name of Krishna to your lips should be recognized as the very best of all Vaishnavas.” (CC 2.16.74)

## The characteristics of a more advanced Vaishnava

*nirantara yāra mukhe śuni kṛṣṇa-nāma \|*  
*sei se vaiṣṇavatara sarva-guṇa-dhāma \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“When I constantly hear Krishna’s name on someone’s lips, I know that he is a more advanced Vaishnava, the abode of all virtue.**

## The most advanced Vaishnava’s characteristics

*vaiṣṇava uttama sei yāhāre dekhile \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma āse mukhe kṛṣṇa-bhakti mile \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The best Vaishnava is one upon seeing whom the Holy Name spontaneously forms on one’s tongue and devotion awakens in the heart.**

*hena kṛṣṇa-nāma jīva ki rūpe karibe \|*  
*tāhāra vidhāna tumi āmāre balibe \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Now please tell me what steps the ordinary living entity needs to follow in order to begin chanting Krishna’s glorious name.”**

*kara yuḍi haridāsa balena vacana \|*  
*prema gada-gada svara sajala nayana \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das folded his hands respectfully and began to speak, his voice trembling with emotion, his eyes brimming with tears.**

## The true form of the Holy Name

*kṛṣṇa-nāma cintāmaṇi anādi cinmaya \|*  
*yei kṛṣṇa sei nāma eka-tattva haya \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s name is a touchstone, a divine, beginningless spiritual jewel. Krishna** ***is*** **His name; He and His name form a single substance.**

**Комментарий:**

The word *cintāmaṇi* is translated as touchstone. This fabulous jewel can give one whatever one wishes. Krishna’s name is *cintāmaṇi* in the same way, because it can bestow the four goals of life on one beset with material desires, as well as the ultimate treasure, pure love for Krishna, on the desireless devotee.

*caitanya vigraha nāma nitya mukta tattva \|*  
*nāma nāmī bhinna naya nitya śuddha sattva \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is a conscious form, an eternally liberated entity; the Name and the Named are not different, but eternal, pure being.**

*e jaḍa jagate tāṅra akṣara ākāra \|*  
*rasa-rūpe rasikete sattva avatāra \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this material world, the Name appears in the form of sounds and syllables, an incarnation of the Supreme Soul appearing joyfully on the tongue.**

*kṛṣṇa vastu haya cāri dharme paricita \|*  
*nāma rūpa guṇa karma anādi vihita \|\|16\|*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme entity known as Krishna has four distinguishing features: His name, His form, His virtues and His acts—eternally known to the wise.**

**Комментарий:**

Every substance is classified according to four properties—its name, form, characteristics and functions. Krishna is the one Supreme Substance. Even so, He is also characterized by these four properties.

## The Holy Name is eternally perfect

*nitya vastu rasa-rūpa kṛṣṇa se advaya \|*  
*sei cāri paricaye vastu siddha haya \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna is an eternal verity; He is pure relish, a non-dual substance. The nature of a thing is established by the abovementioned four properties.**

*sandhinī śaktite tāṅra cāri paricaya \|*  
*nitya-siddha rūpe khyāta sarvadā cinmaya \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These four aspects of the Lord, each of which is totally spiritual, are manifested by the sandhini shakti, revealing His eternal nature.**

*kṛṣṇa ākarṣaye sarva viśva-gata jana \|*  
*sei nitya dharma gata kṛṣṇa nāma dhana \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna attracts all creatures in the universe, as does the Holy Name, the divine treasure that possesses the same eternal properties.**

## Krishna’s form is eternal

*kṛṣṇa-rūpa kṛṣṇa haite sarvadā abheda \|*  
*nāma rūpa eka vastu nāhika prabheda \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s form is eternally non-different from Him. \[So too\] there is no distinction between Krishna’s name and form: They are one substance.**

*śrī-nāma smarile rūpa āise saṅge saṅge \|*  
*rūpa nāma bhinna naya nāce nānā raṅge \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one remembers the Holy Name, the Lord’s form immediately appears; The form and name are not different; both dance on the devotee’s tongue.**

## Krishna’s personal characteristics are eternal

*kṛṣṇa-guṇa catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi ananta apāra \|*  
*yāṅra nija aṁśa-rūpe saba avatāra \|\|22\|\|*  
*yāṅra guṇa aṁśe brahmā śivādi īśvara \|*  
*yāṅra guṇe nārāyaṇa ṣaṣṭhi guṇeśvara \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna has sixty-four qualities, which are infinite and unlimited. All Krishna’s incarnations or partial expansions possess these qualities in part, whereas Brahma, Shiva and other gods have them in smaller proportions. Narayan possesses sixty of these characteristics in full.**

**Комментарий:**

These matters are discussed in more detail in the fifth chapter. Only Krishna has the sixty-four qualities in full. His full expansions like Lord Narayan and avatars like Lord Ramachandra manifest only sixty of these qualities. Demigods up to the level of Siva possess fifty-five of them, but only in limited measure. Ordinary jivas have just fifty, present in much smaller proportions. Even among the different forms of Godhead, Krishna alone possesses four exceptional characteristics, demonstrating His position as the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

*sei saba nitya-guṇe nitya nāma tāṅra \|*  
*ananta saṅkhyāya vyāpta vaikuṇṭha vyāpāra \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From every one of these eternal qualities, the Lord has an eternal name, Limitless numbers of these qualities fill the spiritual world of Vaikuntha.**

## Krishna’s pastimes are eternal

*sei guṇa taraṅgete līlāra vistāra \|*  
*goloke vaikuṇṭha vraja saba cid-ākāre \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s pastimes arise out of the waves of His qualities. Whether in Goloka, Vaikuntha or Vraja, they are all spiritual.**

## Just as Krishna is spiritual substance, so too are His name, form, attributes and activities, thus not distinct from Him.

*nāma rūpa guṇa līlā abhinna udaya \|*  
*acit-samparke baddha jīve bhinna haya \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s name, form, qualities and pastimes are non-different from one another, whereas for a conditioned soul, due to contact with material energy, they are distinct.**

**Комментарий:**

Krishna is the supreme conscious being. Therefore, His name, form, qualities and pastimes are integral to His very identity. The jiva is a spark of consciousness; therefore, in the pure state as an atomic conscious entity, his name, form, qualities and activities are factually not different from his true self. The conditioned soul in contact with the material world takes gross and subtle bodies that are distinct from his true identity. Associated with this body are names, forms, characteristics and activities, all of which cause the jiva’s bewilderment. For one who is liberated by Krishna’s mercy, these extrinsic identities cease to exist.

*śuddha jīve nāma rūpa guṇa kriyā eka \|*  
*jaḍāśrita dehe bheda ei se viveka \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The pure jiva’s name, form, qualities and activities are one, but when one takes a material body, distinctions arise between them.**

*kṛṣṇe nāhi jaḍa-gandha ata eva tāṅya \|*  
*nāma rūpa guṇa līlā eka-tattva bhāya \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is not a whiff of inert matter in Krishna, His name, form, qualities and lila therefore form a single, homogenous truth.**

## Krishna’s name is the basis of all things

*ei cāri paricaya madhye nāma tāṅra \|*  
*sakalera ādi se pratīti sabākāra \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Of these four distinguishing features, Krishna’s name comes first, as is evident to everyone.**

*ata eva nāma mātra vaiṣṇavera dharma \|*  
*nāme prasphuṭita haya rūpa guṇa karma \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore serving Krishna’s names alone is the Vaishnava’s duty; for through the Holy Name, one can realize Krishna’s form, qualities and deeds.**

*kṛṣṇera samagra līlā nāme vidyamāna \|*  
*nāme se parama tattva tomāra vidhāna \|\|31\|*

**Перевод:**

**All of Krishna’s pastimes are present in His name, for You have decided to place that supreme truth in Your name.**

## The distinction between a Vaishnava and an apparent Vaishnava

*sei nāma baddha jīva śraddhā sahakāre \|*  
*śuddha rūpe laile vaiṣṇava bali tāre \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When a conditioned soul utters the Holy Name with faith, and does so purely, I identify him as a Vaishnava.**

*nāmābhāsa yāra haya se vaiṣṇava-prāya \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-kṛpā bale krame śuddha bhāva pāya \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But one who chants the mere semblance of the Name is an apparent Vaishnava; should he get Krishna’s mercy, he comes to experience the Pure Name.**

## In this world, only the Name and the individual soul are spiritual

*nāma sama vastu nāi e bhava saṁsāre \|*  
*nāme se parama dhana kṛṣṇera bhāṇḍāre \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this entire creation, there is nothing like the Holy Name. The Holy Name is the greatest treasure in Krishna’s storehouse.**

*jīva nije cid-vyāpāre kṛṣṇa-nāma āra \|*  
*āra saba prāpañcika jagat saṁsāra \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva himself is a spiritual entity, as is Krishna’s name; other than this, all in creation is a product of the material elements.**

**Комментарий:**

In this material world, everything is illusory and lifeless. The jiva has become entangled in the material world by Krishna’s desire. The jiva is the only spiritual entity within the material world. Krishna descends into the world in the form of His holy name and, on so doing, becomes the second spiritual entity within matter. As such, there are only two spiritual entities in this world—the conscious living entity and the Lord’s holy name. The gods like Brahma are separated portions of the Supreme Lord and are thus counted among the jivas.

## Two kinds of Name: primary and secondary

*mukhya o gauṇa bhede kṛṣṇa-nāma dvi-prakāra \|*  
*mukhya-nāmāśraye jīva pāya sarva sāra \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s name is of two kinds: primary and secondary; By taking shelter of the primary Name, the jiva realizes the supreme essence.**

*cil-līlā āśraya kari yata kṛṣṇa nāma \|*  
*sei sei mukhya nāma sarva guṇa dhāma \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All names of Krishna associated with His transcendental pastimes are His primary names and possess all His transcendental virtues.**

## Primary names

*govinda gopāla rāma śrī-nanda-nandana \|*  
*rādhānātha hari yaśomatī prāṇa-dhana \|\|38\|\|*  
*madana-mohana śyāmasundara mādhava \|*  
*gopīnātha vraja-gopa rākhāla yādava \|\|39\|\|*  
*ei rūpa nitya līlā prakāśaka nāma \|*  
*e saba kīrtane jīva pāya kṛṣṇa dhāma \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda, Gopal, Rama, Nandanandan, Radhanath, Hari, Mother Yashoda’s darling. Madan Mohan, Shyamasundar, Madhava, Gopinath, Vrajagopa, Rakhal, Yadava— All these names of Krishna reveal His eternal pastimes; thus, whoever chants them attains His abode.**

## Secondary names, their symptoms

*jaḍā prakṛtira paricaye nāma yata \|*  
*prakṛtira guṇe gauṇa vedera sammata \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All names of the Lord that describe His relation to the material creation or material qualities are considered to be His secondary names.**

*sṛṣṭi-kartā paramātmā brahma sthiti-kara \|*  
*jagat-saṁhartā pātā yajñeśvara hara \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some of these are: Creator, Supersoul, God, Brahma, the Maintainer, Destroyer, Protector, the Lord of Sacrifice or Hara.**

## Differing results from primary and secondary Names

*ei-rūpa nāma karma-jñāna-kāṇḍa-gata \|*  
*puṇya mokṣa dāna kare śāstrera sammata \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s secondary names are favored by those on the paths of works and knowledge. According to the scriptures, they are the source of pious merit and even liberation.**

*nāmera ye mukhya phala kṛṣṇa prema dhana \|*  
*tāra mukhya nāme mātra labhe sādhu gaṇa \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The primary result of chanting the Holy Name is pure love for Krishna, but this is only achieved by pious persons who chant the primary Names.**

**Комментарий:**

By chanting Krishna’s secondary names, one attains either merit (*puṇya*) or liberation (*mokṣa*). Only Krishna’s primary names are able to bestow Krishna prema.

## Differing results from the Pure Name and the Semblance of the Name

*eka kṛṣṇa-nāma yadi mukhe bāhirāya \|*  
*athavā śravaṇa-pathe antarete yāya \|\|45\|\|*  
*śuddha varṇa haya vā aśuddha varṇa haya \|*  
*tāte jīva tare ei śāstrera nirṇaya \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Should someone invoke Krishna’s name even once, or if it should enter his heart after being heard,**  
**whether its syllables are clearly pronounced or not, It delivers him from the material condition, so say the Shastras.**

*kintu eka kathā ithe āche suniścita \|\|*  
*nāmābhāsa haile vilambe haya hita \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Still, there is one thing that needs to be clearly stated here: If one chants but a semblance of the Name, it takes longer.**

*nāmābhāsa haile-o anya śubha haya \|*  
*prema-dhana kevala vilambe upajaya \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is true that the semblance of the Name bestows other auspicious results, but it takes longer to bestow ecstatic love for Krishna.**

*nāmābhāse pāpa-kṣaye śuddha nāma haya \|*  
*takhana-i śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema labhaye niścaya \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Once the semblance of the Name destroys one’s sins, one can chant the Pure Name; then it is certain that one will get find the treasure of Krishna prema.**

**Комментарий:**

The semblance of the Holy Name (*nāmābhāsa*) destroys all one’s sinful reactions. When sins and sinful desire have been eliminated by such chanting, then the Pure Name appears on the devotee’s tongue. Then the Pure Name bestows love of Krishna on the chanter.

## Faults arise from discontinuity

*kintu vyavahita hale haya aparādha \|*  
*sei aparādhe haya prema-lābha bādha \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If there is discontinuity, however, one commits an offense. Such offenses are an obstacle to attaining Krishna prema.**

*nāma nāmī bheda-buddhi vyavadhāna haya \|*  
*vyavahita thākile kadāpi prema naya \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Discontinuity means means making a distinction between the Name and the Named; such discontinuity means one will never attain love for Krishna.**

## Two kinds of discontinuity

*varṇa vyavadhāna āra tattva vyavadhāna \|*  
*vyavadhāna dvi-prakāra vedera vidhāna \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are two kinds of discontinuity say the Vedas: Either in pronouncing the Name’s syllables or in understanding the Name.**

**Комментарий:**

Discontinuity or disjunction of syllables (*varṇa-vyavadhāna*) takes the following form: In the word *haṭhikari,* the first and last syllables make up Krishna’s name, Hari. However, the word *ṭhika* separates the two syllables that make up the Name and thus its full benefits are not attained. In the word *hārāma*, however, there is no such discontinuity. Thus even when used as an expletive or an adjective for something else, uttering the word *hārāma* brings liberation. The discontinuity of understanding (*tattva-vyavadhāna*) is much more serious. In truth, there is no disjunction or discontinuity between Krishna’s name and His person. Those who accept the Mayavada doctrine say that Krishna’s name “Kṛṣṇa” is somehow distinct from the Supreme Brahman. This is called the discontinuity of understanding. Then they commit the offense of separation of identities. That offense destroys everything.

## Mayavada causes doctrinal discontinuity of the Name

*tattva-vyavadhāna māyāvāda duṣṭa mata \|*  
*kalira jañjāla ei śāstra asammata \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Discontinuity in understanding means being contaminated by impersonalism. This is the bane of the Age of Kali and disapproved of by the scriptures.**

## The Pure Name is free from discontinuities

*ata eva śuddha kṛṣṇa nāma yāṅra mukhe \|*  
*tāṅhāke vaiṣṇava jāni sadā sevi sukhe \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore whoever chants the pure name of Krishna is a true Vaishnava; I serve such a devotee with joy.**

**Комментарий:**

The more we are free from contaminants, the more we experience the pure, spiritual Name rather than Its mere semblance

*nāmābhāsa bhedi’ śuddha-nāma labhibāre \|*  
*sad-guru sevibe jīva yatna sahakāre \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To overcome the semblance of the Name and attain the Pure Name, the jiva should take care to serve an authentic spiritual teacher.**

*bhajane anartha nāśa yei kṣaṇe pāya \|*  
*cit-svarūpa nāme nāce bhaktera jihvāya \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The very moment that the obstacles to worship are destroyed, the devotee finds the Name, in all its divine splendor, dancing on his tongue.**

*nāma se amṛta dhārā nāhi chāḍe āra \|*  
*nāma rase matta jīva nāce anivāra \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From that moment on, he cannot stop drinking the streams of its nectar, and dances in unbounded ecstasy from the divine intoxication of the Name.**

*nāma nāce jīva nāce nāce prema dhana \|*  
*jagat nācāya māyā kare palāyana \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name dances, the jiva dances and so dances the treasure of love; Together they make the whole world dance, and Illusion takes flight.**

## Faith in the Name makes one eligible to chant

*nāme adhikāra nara-mātre kaile dāna \|*  
*sarva-śakti nāme prabhu karile vidhāna \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You have given every human being the right to chant Your names, and invested all Your infinite potencies into the Holy Name.**

*yāra śraddhā haya nāme sei adhikārī \|*  
*yāra mukhe kṛṣṇa-nāma sei se ācārī \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever has faith in the Holy Name has the right to chant It; Whoever’s tongue utters the Name sets an example for all humanity.**

## There are no ritual preconditions to chanting

*deśa kāla aśaucādi niyama sakala \|*  
*śrī-nāma-grahaṇe nāi nāma se prabala \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No rules of proper time and place, or states of ritual purity apply to the chanting, for the Name’s power overcomes all obstacles.**

## In the Age of Kali, belief in the Holy Name grants eligibility

*dāne yajñe snāne jape āche ta vicāra \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtane mātra śraddhā adhikāra \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are preconditions for charity, sacrifice, ritual bathing, and reciting mantras; for chanting the Holy Name, the only precondition is faith.**

**Комментарий:**

To give in charity or to engage in any of these other religious activities, one must take account of the time, place and personal qualifications of the candidates. Furthermore, purity and impurity play an important role in such other pious actions. In the chanting of the Holy Name, however, none of these things is relevant. Faith alone is the deciding factor in determining the degree of success one will have in chanting.

*yuga-dharma hari-nāma ananya śraddhāya \|*  
*ye kare āśraya tāra sarva-lābha haya \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The religious practice for the Age of Kali must be done with complete faith; one who takes exclusive shelter of that practice attains all benefits.**

*kali jīva niṣkapaṭe kṛṣṇera saṁsāre \|*  
*avasthita haye kṛṣṇa-nāma sadā kare \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this age, one should sincerely live in the world with Krishna in the center, and faithful to one’s customary duties, always chant Krishna’s names.**

## One should take what is favorable to chanting and reject what is unfavorable

*bhajanera anukūla sarva kārya kari’ \|*  
*bhajanera pratikūla saba parihari’ \|\|65\|\|*  
*kṛṣṇera saṁsāre thāki’ kāṭāye jīvana \|*  
*nirantara hari-nāma karena smaraṇa \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Doing everything to favor the worship of the Lord and rejecting everything that impedes such worship, one should live one’s life in the home putting the Lord at the center, and constantly chant and meditate on the Holy Name.**

## One should chant with complete dedication

*āra kona dharma karma kabhu nā karibe \|*  
*svatantra īśvara-jñāne anye nā pūjibe \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no other religious duty, no other work that one need do; one must not worship other gods, believing they are independent in their powers.**

*kṛṣṇa-nāma bhakta-sevā satata karibe \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema lābha tāra avaśya ha-ibe \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who always chants Krishna’s name and serves the devotees will certainly attain pure love for Krishna.**

*haridāsa kāṅdi prabhu caraṇe paḍiyā \|*  
*nāme anurāga māge caraṇa dhariyā \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Hari Das began to weep and fell down before the Lord; taking hold of His feet, he begged to be given a taste for chanting His names.**

*haridāsa pade bhaktivinoda yāhāra \|*  
*harināma cintāmaṇi jīvana tāhāra \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name, this divine, wish-fulfilling jewel, is the life of Bhaktivinoda, whose only pleasure lies in the Supreme Lord, His devotees and devotional service.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the second chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “Taking up the Holy Name.”

## 3 - Namabhasa: The semblance of the Holy Name

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā jīvana \|*  
*sītādvaita jaya śrīvāsādi bhakta-jana \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to Sita Devi and Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to Srivasa Pandit and all the devotees!**

*haridāse mahāprabhu sadaya haiyā \|*  
*uṭhāya takhana padma-hasta prasāriyā \|\|2\|\|*  
*bale śuna haridāsa āmāra vacana \|*  
*nāmābhāsa spaṣṭa-rūpe bujhāo ekhana \|\|3\|\|*  
*nāmābhāsa bujhāile nāma śuddha habe \|*  
*anāyāse jīva nāma-guṇe tare yābe \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overflowing with compassion for Hari Das, Mahaprabhu stretched out His lotus hands and lifted him up. He said, “Listen now, Hari Das, to My request— Please explain the semblance of the Holy Name to Me clearly. If you explain Namabhasa, the jiva’s chanting will be purified and he will cross over by virtue of the Pure Name.**

## The semblance of the Name: Ignorance and obstacles are like clouds and mist respectively

*nāma sūrya-sama nāśe māyā andhakāra \|*  
*megha kujjhaṭikā nāme ḍhāke bāra bāra \|\|5\|\|*  
*jīvera ajñāna āra anartha sakala \|*  
*kujjhaṭikā-megha-rūpe haya ta prabala \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is like the sun, illusion like darkness; but just as clouds and mist repeatedly block the sunlight, the ignorance and self-created obstacles of the jiva are powerful coverings that block the sun of the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

Krishna and His name are both called the spiritual sun that destroys the darkness of ignorance and illusion. The sun of the Holy Name has risen in the world to show mercy to the conditioned soul. The conditioned soul’s ignorance is here compared to fog or mist, while the self-produced obstacles are compared to the cloud. These obstacles prevent the sun’s light from shining through and dissipating the darkness. Similarly, ignorance and anarthas cover the eyes of the jiva so that he cannot perceive the glory of the Holy Name. Of course, the sun is so great that it cannot be covered; we simply say that when something interferes with our seeing it.

*kṛṣṇa-nāma sūrya citta-gagaṇe uṭhila \|*  
*kujjhaṭikā-megha punaḥ tāhāre ḍhākila \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s name is the Sun; it rises in the sky of consciousness; but internal clouds and fog prevent us from seeing it.**

## The fog of ignorance – misunderstanding one’s true identity

*nāmera ye cit svarūpa tāhā nāhi jāne \|*  
*se ajñāna kujjhaṭikā andhakāra āne \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who does not know the true spiritual nature of the Holy Name is plunged into darkness, as though blanketed by a thick fog.**

*kṛṣṇa sarveśvara bali nāhi jāne yei \|*  
*nānā deve pūji karma-mārge bhrame sei \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna is the Supreme Person and lord of all creations— one who does not know this worships other gods with pious acts and rituals.**

*jīve cit svarūpa bali nāhi yāra jñāna \|*  
*māyā jaḍāśraye tāra satata ajñāna \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who does not know that the individual soul is a spiritual entity takes refuge in the nescient illusions of the world; this is ignorance.”**

*tabe haridāsa bale āja āmi dhanya \|*  
*mama mukhe nāma kathā śunibe caitanya \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das then said, “Today I am supremely fortunate, for Sri Chaitanya Himself will listen to me glorify the Holy Name.**

*kṛṣṇa jīva prabhu dāsa jaḍātmikā māyā \|*  
*ye nā jāne tāra śire ajñānera chāyā \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is the master and the jiva is His servant. Maya is inert matter. One who does not know this is covered by the shadow of ignorance.**

**Комментарий:**

One who is in ignorance does not know that Lord Krishna is supreme over all, nor that the gods and all other jivas are His eternal servitors. He does not know that the jiva’s nature is transcendental and that the material world is a manifestation of Maya, the Lord’s illusory energy. One who understands these things is freed from the darkness of ignorance.

## The cloud of anarthas

*asat-tṛṣṇā, hṛdaya-daurbalya, aparādha \|*  
*anartha e saba megha-rūpe kare bādha \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hankering for illusory things, weakness of heart and offenses— these are the anarthas that cloud the jiva and block his spiritual progress.**

**Комментарий:**

“Hankering for the impermanent” (*asat-tṛṣṇā)* means seeking enjoyment in anything other than Krishna. These three things are the clouds over the consciousness of the jiva.

*nāma-sūrya raśmi ḍhāke nāmābhāsa haya \|*  
*svataḥ siddha kṛṣṇa-nāme sadā ācchādaya \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They cover the sun of Krishna’s name, resulting in its becoming a mere semblance of Itself, This is not the Holy Name in Its true, self-effulgent glory.**

## The end of Namabhasa

*sambandha-tattvera jñāna yāvat nā haya \|*  
*tāvat se nāmābhāsa jīvera āśraya \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For as long as the jiva does not have knowledge of his relation to God, he is obliged to take refuge in the mere semblance of the Holy Name.**

*sādhaka yadyapi pāya sad-guru āśraya \|*  
*bhajana-naipuṇye megha ādi dūra haya \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the aspirant learns these truths from a genuine spiritual master, he attains expertise in bhajan and the clouds of his ignorance dissipate.**

## Sambandha, abhidheya, prayojan

*megha kujjhaṭikā gele nāma-divākara \|*  
*prakāśa haiyā bhakte dena prema-vara \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Once the clouds and mists have dissipated, the sun of the Holy Name shines forth and gives the blessing of prema to the devotee.**

*sad-guru sambandha jñāna kariyā arpaṇa \|*  
*abhidheya rūpe karāna nāmānuśīlana \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The authentic spiritual master bestows sambandha knowledge, and engages him in the practice of cultivating the Holy Name.**

*nāma-sūrya svalpa-kāle prabala haiyā \|*  
*anartha-kujjhaṭikā dena tāḍāiyā \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the sun of the Holy Name’s rays grow stronger and stronger, they quickly drive the fog of anarthas away.**

*prayojana-tattva tabe dena prema-dhana \|*  
*prāpta-prema jīva kare nāma-saṅkīrtana \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the Holy Name grants the ultimate goal of life, Krishna prema. Even so, the jiva who has prema still continues to chant the Holy Name.**

## Sambandha jnana

*sad-guru-caraṇe jīva śraddhā sahakāre \|*  
*prathame sambandha-jñāna pāya suvicāre \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the jiva faithfully takes refuge of a spiritual master, he first clearly learns of his relation with Krishna.**

*kṛṣṇa nitya prabhu āra jīva nitya dāsa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa prema nitya jīva-svabhāva prakāśa \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna is the eternal master and the jiva, His eternal servant. Love for Krishna is also eternal and apparent when the jiva’s true nature is manifest.**

*kṛṣṇa nitya dāsa jīva tāhā vismariyā \|*  
*māyika jagate phire sukha anveṣiyā \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The jiva is forgetful of this eternal relationship of service to Krishna and so wanders through the illusory world in search of happiness.**

*māyika jagat haya jīva kārāgāra \|*  
*jīvera vaimukhya doṣe daṇḍa pratikāra \|\|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This illusory world is the jiva’s prison house, the punishment he receives for turning his back on Krishna.**

**Комментарий:**

The abode of the Goddess consisting of fourteen different spheres is a jailhouse for the jiva who turns his back on Krishna, not a place where he can find true joy. The pleasure of sense gratification is ephemeral and thus simply another kind of misery. Punishment is most certainly a reaction to one’s activities, but in this case it is intended to reform the sinful tendencies of the individual.

*tabe yadi jīva sādhu vaiṣṇava kṛpāya \|*  
*sambandha jñānete punaḥ kṛṣṇa-nāma pāya \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If, however, the jiva receives the blessings of a saintly Vaishnava, he begins chanting Krishna’s name in correct understanding of his position.**

**Комментарий:**

The correct understanding of one’s position or sambandha jnana means to think, “I am an atomic particle of consciousness and the eternal servant of Krishna. Krishna is the infinite conscious entity and my only master. The material world is the prison house that is meant to reform my truant tendencies.”

*tabe pāya prema-dhana sarva-dharma-sāra \|*  
*yāhāra nikaṭe sāyujyādira dhikkāra \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then he attains love for Krishna, the essence of all religion, and despises the idea of liberation as merging into God’s existence.**

*yāvat sambandha jñāna sthira nāhi haya \|*  
*tāvat anarthe nāmābhāsera āśraya \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As long as one’s knowledge of Krishna and the self is unsteady,one is polluted by anarthas and can only chant a semblance of the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

As long as one has not learned the true relationship that exists between himself and the Supreme Lord from an authentic spiritual master, he remains affected by the anartha of ignorance.

## The benefits of chanting Namabhasa

*nāmābhāsa daśāte-o aneka maṅgala \|*  
*jīvera avaśya haya sukṛti prabala \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even so, many benefits accrue to one who chants the semblance of the Holy Name; he will most certainly accumulate a great deal of pious merit.**

**Комментарий:**

Of all the ways of achieving pious merit, chanting the semblance of the Holy Name is the best. It brings more auspicious benefits that fasting, yogic practice or sacrificial rituals.

*nāmābhāse naṣṭa haya āche pāpa yata \|*  
*nāmābhāse mukti haya kali haya hata \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The semblance of the Name removes whatever sins one has committed; The semblance of the Name gives liberation and destroys the influence of Kali;**

*nāmābhāse nara haya supaṅkti pāvana \|*  
*nāmābhāse sarva-roga haya nivāraṇa \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The semblance of the Name makes a man capable of purifying even the pious; The semblance of the Name protects one from all disease.**

*sakala āśaṅkā nāmābhāse dūra haya \|*  
*nāmābhāsī sarvāriṣṭa haite śānti pāya \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All worries are subdued by even a semblance of the Holy Name. One who chants the semblance of the Name is free from all fear and distress.**

*yakṣa rakṣa bhūta preta graha samudaya \|*  
*nāmābhāse sakala anartha dūre yāya \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Demons, ghosts, goblins and witches, wicked creatures of all sorts are kept at bay the influence of Namabhasa, as are all other obstacles.**

*narake patita loka sukhe mukti pāya \|*  
*samasta prārabdha karma nāmābhāse yāya \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even if one has fallen into hell, he still attains liberation easily, for even one’s old karma is destroyed by the semblance of the Holy Name.**

*sarva-vedādhika sarva tīrtha haite baḍa \|*  
*nāmābhāsa sarva-śubha-karma śreṣṭhatara \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Namabhasa is greater than the Vedas, than all the places of pilgrimage; as such, it is superior to any other kind of pious activity.**

## The shadow of the Holy Name takes one to Vaikuntha

*dharma artha kāma mokṣa catur-varga-dātā \|*  
*sarva-śakti dhare nāmābhāse jīva-trātā \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Namabhasa gives the chanter dharma, artha, kama and moksha, It is all powerful and has the power to deliver the conditioned souls.**

*jagat ānanda-kara śreṣṭha-pada-prada \|*  
*agatira eka gati sarva-śreṣṭha-pada \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It gives joy to all the world and raises one to the supreme state, giving the supreme state to even one who is without refuge.**

*vaikuṇṭhādi loka prāpti nāmābhāse haya \|*  
*viśeṣataḥ kali-yuge sarva-śāstra kaya \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Namabhasa takes one to the spiritual abode of Vaikuntha, especially in the Kali Yuga, declare all the scriptures.**

## The four kinds of Namabhasa

*caturvidha nāmābhāsa ei mātra jāni \|*  
*saṅketa o parihāsa, stobha, helā māni \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The semblance of the Holy Name manifests in four different ways: when uttered as a signal, as a joke, or in a mocking or careless fashion.**

**Комментарий:**

If one chants the Holy Name (1) as a name or signal (*saṅketa*)*,* (2) jokingly (*parihāsa*)*,* (3) mockingly (*stobha*) or (4) carelessly (*helā*), then such chanting is called Namabhasa. Thus according to the attitude with which one chants, there are four kinds of Namabhasa. From first to last, these attitudes are progressively more flawed.

## Sanket is of two kinds

*viṣṇu lakṣya kari jaḍa buddhye nāma laya \|*  
*anya lakṣya kari viṣṇu nāma uccāraya \|\|39\|\|*  
*saṅgete dvividha ei haya nāmābhāsa \|*  
*ajāmila sākṣī tāra śāstrete prakāśa \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sanket is when one utters Vishnu’s name but has a material concept of the Lord, or when one uses Vishnu’s name to indicate some other person or thing: these two attitudes mean that the sound “Vishnu” is only a semblance of His name. The scriptures have revealed the power of such chanting through the example of Ajamila.**

*yavana sakala mukta habe anāyāse \|*  
*hārāma hārāma bali kahe nāmābhāse \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the same way, all Muslims will be liberated by saying the word** ***hārām,*** **for this too is an instance of Namabhasa as a signal or sanket.**

*anyatra saṅkete yadi haya nāmābhāsa \|*  
*tathāpi nāmera teja nā haya vināśa \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is because, even if one uses It as a signal indicating something else, the semblance of the Name never entirely loses the Name’s real potency.**

## Chanting in jest

*parihāse kṛṣṇa-nāma yei jana kare \|*  
*jarāsandha sama sei e saṁsāre tare \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one chants the Holy Name of Krishna in jest, then like Jarasandha he is freed of the material condition.**

## Chanting mockingly

*aṅga-bhaṅgī caidya sama kare nāmābhāsa \|*  
*stobha mātra haya tabu nāśe bhava-pāśa \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sishupal called Krishna’s name while making mocking gestures; though he did so with disrespect, he was liberated from material existence.**

## Chanting carelessly

*mana nāhi deya āra avajñā bhāvete \|*  
*kṛṣṇa rāma bale helā nāmābhāsa tāte \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants inattentively or with disdain, may repeat “Krishna, Rama,” but he only chants the semblance of the Name called carelessness.**

*ei saba nāmābhāse mleccha-gaṇa tare \|*  
*viṣayī alasa jana ei patha dhare \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All four kinds of Namabhasa can bring salvation to even the most impure; those who are materialistic or lazy avail themselves of this path.**

## The difference between faithful chanting and faithless Namabhasa

*śraddhā kari kare nāma anartha sahita \|*  
*śraddhā-nāma haya sei tomāra vihita \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to Your teachings, O Lord, if someone chants the Name with faith, even while being afflicted by anarthas, that is called faithful chanting.**

*saṅketādi avajñā paryanta bhāva dhari \|*  
*nāma kare helāya ye śraddhā parihari \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, the four kinds of Namabhasa lead to a spirit of neglect and eventually blasphemy; one who chants in this way eventually loses all faith.**

*nāmābhāsa avadhi se helā nāma haya \|*  
*tāhāte-o mukti labhe pāpa haya kṣaya \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The worst type of Namabhasa is hela, or careless chanting, even though this too can bring one liberation and destroy one’s sins.**

## When anarthas are destroyed, Namabhasa becomes the Pure Name and gives prema

*kṛṣṇa prema chāḍi saba nāmābhāse pāya \|*  
*nāmābhāse punaḥ śuddha nāma haye yāya \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Other than Krishna prema, all perfections are available from Namabhasa. Moreover, Namabhasa can be purified and become the Pure Name.**

*anartha vigate yabe śuddha nāma haya \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema tabe tāra ha-ibe niścaya \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When anarthas are destroyed, Namabhasa becomes the Pure Name and then, in all certainty, one attains ecstatic love for Krishna.**

*nāmābhāse sākṣāt se prema dite nāre \|*  
*nāma haye prema deya vidhi anusāre \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Namabhasa itself cannot give the treasure of Krishna prema, but only does so, as a matter of course, when it becomes the Pure Name.**

## The difference between Namabhasa and offensive chanting

*ata eva nāma aparādha parihari \|*  
*nāmābhāsa kare yei tāre nati kari \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore I bow my head to the soul who stops committing offenses and engages in the chanting of the semblance of the Holy Name.**

*karma jñāna haite ananta śreṣṭhatara \|*  
*bali nāmābhāse jāni ohe sarveśvara \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting even a mere semblance of the Holy Name, O Supreme Lord, is still far superior to the spiritual disciplines of karma and jnana.**

*rati mūlā śraddhā yadi śuddha-bhāve haya \|*  
*tabe ta viśuddha nāma ha-ibe udaya \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one has the type of pure faith that is based in a specific relation with Krishna, then that leads the chanter to experience the Pure Name.**

## Two kinds of Namabhasa: shadow and reflection

*ābhāsa dvividha haya pratibimba chāyā \|*  
*śraddhābhāsa dvi-prakāra saba tava māyā \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, semblances are of two kinds—shadows and reflections. The semblance of faith also has these divisions, all manifestations of Your Maya.**

*chāyā śraddhābhāse chāyā-nāmābhāsa haya \|*  
*sei nāmābhāse jīvera śubha prasavaya \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who has shadow faith chants the shadow of the Holy Name, which though only a semblance of the Name, still brings auspiciousness to the jiva.**

**Комментарий:**

The scriptures are filled with words like *nāmābhāsa*, *vaiṣṇavābhāsa, śraddhābhāsa, raty-ābhāsa, premābhāsa, mukty-ābhāsa*, etc. In each case, the suffix *ābhāsa* (“semblance, facsimile, faint or indistinct image”) has a clear meaning that needs to be explained. There are in fact two kinds of *ābhāsa—*a semblance of the true form (*svarūpābhāsa*), and a semblance of the reflection (*pratibimbābhāsa*).

· *Svarūpābhāsa* means a restricted manifestation of something as it is, in all its splendor; for example, the way the sun appears only dimly through the clouds, with only a limited diffusion of light.

· *Pratibimbābhāsa,* on the other hand, means a distorted manifestation of the original object. This is the kind of *ābhāsa* being mentioned in the following dictum: *ābhāsas tu mṛṣā buddhir avidyā-kāryam ucyate* – “A semblance is a false understanding of something, and is the product of ignorance,” like the distorted appearance of the sun when reflected on the surface of water. As long as the sun of the Holy Name is covered by the cloud of the jiva’s ignorance and sinful tendencies, its light is perceived as dim and weak. Yet even in this condition, the Holy Name does a great deal of good in the world. *Pratibimba-nāmābhāsa* appears when the rays of the Holy Name are reflected on the lake of Mayavada. Though the Holy Name reflected in this way may bestow *sāyujyā mukti* on the persons who chants it, it will never result in the ultimate goal of chanting, Krishna prema. In fact, this type of Namabhasa is a major offense to the Holy Name and should therefore not really be counted as a Namabhasa at all.

Only the *chāyā-nāmābhāsa* can be subdivided into the four categories of Namabhasa previously described. The scriptures praise Namabhasa in general, but condemn the *pratibimba-nâmābhāsa*. The real difference between the two kinds of Namabhasa is that the “shadow” of the Holy Name is the result of ignorance, while the “reflection” of the Name is the result of deliberate error. The latter is a grave offense that blocks one from developing devotion.

The person who is a “semblance of a Vaishnava,” or *vaiṣṇavābhāsa*, is not honored as a genuine Vaishnava, but as long as he has not been contaminated by the Mayavadi philosophy, he should be given the respect due the lower category (*kaniṣṭha*) of devotee, or mundane (*prākṛta*) devotee. The reason for this is that he can easily progress to a higher stage of bhakti through contact with devotee saints. Pure Vaishnavas therefore consider such neophytes to be unspoiled and include them among their friends on whom they shower their blessings. They must not ignore them as they do the atheistic Mayavadis. The advanced devotee expands the neophyte’s limited understanding of devotion beyond some simple rituals that are the result of a purely conventional faith (*laukikī śraddhā*) and gives him the fundamental spiritual understanding that leads to service of the Lord and His pure devotees. If, on the other hand, someone should show an unbreakable faith in the Mayavada doctrines, he should be avoided.

The pure devotees will ressuscitate the small spark of devotion in the neophytes, encourage their propensity to worship the Deity and progressively give them sambandha jnana and establish them in devotional service of the Supreme Lord and His devotees. But if a neophyte exhibits a strong inclination to incorrigible Mayavadi attitudes, then he must be avoided.

## Pratimba-nāmābhāsa

*anya jīve śuddhā śraddhā kariyā darśana \|*  
*nija mane śraddhābhāsa āne yei jana \|\|58\|\|*  
*bhoga mokṣa vāñchā tāhe thāke nitya miśi \|*  
*aśrame abhīṣṭa lābhe yāte divā-niśi \|\|59\|\|*  
*śraddhāra lakṣaṇa mātra śraddhā tāhe naya \|*  
*tāke pratibimba śraddhābhāsa śāstre kaya \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After seeing the presence of pure faith in another jiva, one may experience a semblance of faith coming into his own heart. But desires for enjoyment and liberation remain mixed in with this semblance of faith, on which such a person is constantly fixed, hoping to achieve them without effort. He shows the external signs of faith, but does not have real faith. As such, it is only a semblance of true faith, its reflection.**

*pratibimba śraddhābhāse nāmābhāsa yata \|*  
*pratibimba-nāmābhāsa haya avirata \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The semblance of the Holy Name chanted with such reflected faith, has been given the name** ***pratibimba-nāmābhāsa*****.**

## Pratibimba-nāmābhāsa results in the hypocrisy of Mayavada

*ei nāmābhāse māyāvāda duṣṭa-mata \|*  
*praveśiyā śaṭhatāya haya pariṇata \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one chanting this sort of Namabhasa is further corrupted by Mayavada, then it turns into a purely hypocritical type of activity.**

## The hypocritical chanting of Pratibimba-nāmābhāsa is offensive

*nitya sādhya nāme sādhana buddhi kari’ \|*  
*nāmera mahimā nāśi aparādhe mari \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who thinks the Name, the goal, is only a means to achieve some end minimizes the Holy Name’s glories and destroys himself with his offenses.**

## The difference between the shadow and reflection types of Namabhasa

*chāyā nāmābhāse mātra hayata ajñāna \|*  
*hṛdaya-daurbalya haite anartha vidhāna \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The shadow type of Namabhasa is simply caused by ignorance; it is one of the unwanted consequences arising from weakness of the heart.**

*sei saba doṣe nāma karena mārjana \|*  
*pratibimba nāmābhāse doṣera vardhana \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord’s holy name rectifies all these faults, whereas** ***pratibimba-nāmābhāsa*** **chanting simply increases them.**

## Mayavada and devotion are mutually contradictory, for Mayavada is offensive

*kṛṣṇa-nāma rūpa guṇa līlādi sakala \|*  
*māyāvādi-mate mithyā naśvara sakala \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krishna’s names, forms, qualities and pastimes are all false amd temporary, according to the Mayavadi doctrine.**

*sei mate prema-tattva nitya nāhi haya \|*  
*bhakti-viparīta māyāvāda suniścaya \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to their philosophy, pure love for Krishna is not an eternal truth; it is thus clear that Mayavada is diametrically opposed to devotion.**

*bhakti-vairī madhye māyāvādera gaṇana \|*  
*ata eva māyāvādī aparādhī hana \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Mayavadis are counted among the enemies of devotion and are therefore offenders to the Holy Name.**

*māyāvādi mukhe nāma nāhi bāhirāya \|*  
*nāma bāhirāya tabu nāmatva nā pāya \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name cannot appear on the tongue of a Mayavadi; Its syllables may emanate from his mouth, but these are not the Name.**

*māyāvādī yadi kare nāma uccāraṇa \|*  
*nāmake anitya bali labhaye patana \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even if he should chant the Lord’s names, he falls down because he thinks them to be a temporary, material phenomenon.**

*nāmera nikaṭe bhoga-mokṣera prārthanā \|*  
*nāmera nikaṭe śāṭhya phalete yātanā \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He may pray to the Holy Name for sense pleasure and liberation, but this hypocritical attitude to the Name results in unrelenting misery.**

## When does the Mayavadi give up his offenses?

*tabe yadi māyāvādī bhukti mukti āśa \|*  
*chāḍiyā karaye nāma haye kṛṣṇa-dāsa \|\|72\|\|*  
*tabe tāre chāḍe māyāvāda duṣṭa-mata \|*  
*anutāpa saha haya nāma anugata \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**However, if the Mayavadi should give up his desire for pleasure and liberation, and take up chanting in the faith that he is Krishna’s eternal servant, then the corrupt ideas of Mayavada loosen their hold on him and with an attitude of repentance, he becomes a true follower of the Holy Name.**

*sādhu saṅge kare punaḥ śravaṇa kīrtana \|*  
*susambandha jñāna tāra ude tata kṣaṇa \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then goes on to hear and chant in the company of pure devotees and thus becomes aware of the true nature of God, the jiva and the world.**

*aviśrānta nāma kare paḍe cakṣu jala \|*  
*nāma kṛpā pāya citta haya ta sabala \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With this knowledge, he chants while continuously shedding tears  and thus receives the mercy of the Holy Name, gaining strength of heart.**

## The Mayavadi’s offense is to not recognize that bhakti is eternal

*kṛṣṇa-rūpa kṛṣṇa-dāsya jīvera svabhāva \|*  
*māyāvāda anitya kalpita bale saba \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s form, service to Krishna and the jiva’s individual nature itself are all considered by the Mayavadi to be transient and imaginary.**

*hena māyāvāda nāma aparāde gaṇi \|*  
*māyāvāda haya sarva vipadera khani \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This Mayavadi philosophy is a grievous offense to the Holy Name and the source of calamity for an aspirant to spiritual life.**

## The Namabhasa of the Mayavadis leads to a mere semblance of liberation

*nāmābhāsa kalpa-taru māyāvādi-jane \|*  
*abhīṣṭa arpaṇa kare sāyujya nirvāṇe \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even the semblance of the Holy Name is a wish-fulfilling tree, for it bestows on Mayavadis the** ***sāyujyā*** **liberation they desire.**

*sarva-śakti nāme āche tāi nāmābhāse \|*  
*pratibimba ha-ile-o dena mukty-ābhāse \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because the Name is omnipotent, even Its semblance as reflection (*****pratibimba*****) can give the semblance of liberation.**

*pañca-vidha mukti madhye sāyujya ābhāsa \|*  
*bhava kleśa nāśe mātra phale sarva-nāśa \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Of the five types of liberation, merging into God is but a facsimile of true release; Material suffering indeed terminates with it, but at the cost of losing one’s soul.**

## The Mayavadi does not find permanent happiness

*māyāya mohita jana tāhe sukha māne \|*  
*sukhābhāsa mātra pāya sāyujya nirvāṇe \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Captivated by Maya, the Mayavadis find** ***sāyujyā*** **pleasurable; but the snuffing out of personal identity is only a semblance of real happiness.**

*sac-cit-ānanda sevā parama nirvṛti \|*  
*sāyujya nā pāya kabhu hata-kṛṣṇa-smṛti \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Divine ecstasy is found in serving the form of existence, knowledge, and bliss, but this is impossible when one forgets Krishna in** ***sāyujyā*** **identity with Him.**

*yāṅhā nāhi bhakti prema nityatā viśvāsa \|*  
*nitya sukha kaiche tāhe ha-ibe prakāśa \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Where there is no belief in the eternal nature of bhakti and prema how can one ever expect to experience eternal bliss?**

## If the chanter of the Name’s shadow avoids contamination by Mayavadi ideas, he gradually comes to chant the Pure Name

*chāyā nāmābhāsī nāhi jāne duṣṭa-mata \|*  
*matavāde citta-bale nahe tāra hata \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The chanter of the Name’s shadow is not aware of mistaken concepts, and so his strength of heart is not dissipated by philosophical confusion.**

*se kevala nāhi jāne yathārtha prabhāva \|*  
*se prabhāva jñāna-dāna nāmera svabhāva \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His limitation is only that he is ignorant of the Holy Name’s true potency, but the inherent nature of the Name is to bestow such knowledge on the chanter.**

*meghācchanne sūrya-prabhā pratīta nā haya \|*  
*kintu meghe nāśi sūrya karena udaya \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For example, the sun may not be visible when the sky is cloudy, but the sun itself will disperse the clouds and shine through in full glory.**

*chāyā- nāmābhāsī dhanya sad-guru prabhāve \|*  
*alpa-dine nāma prema anāyāse pābe \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants the shadow Name is greatly benefited by the influence of a genuine guru, and quickly attains the Pure Name and love for Krishna.**

## A devotee should not associate with Mayavadis

*māyāvādī saṅga teṅha satarke chāḍiyā \|*  
*śuddha nāma parāyaṇa tuṣibe seviyā \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such a devotee should diligently avoid the association of Mayavadis and through service please Vaishnavas who are attached to the pure Holy Name.**

*ei ta tomāra ājñā śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya \|*  
*sei ājñā yei pāle sei jīva dhanya \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Chaitanya, this is Your injunction and whoever follows it is fortunate indeed.**

*ye nā pāle tava ājñā sei jīva chāra \|*  
*koṭī janme kichute-i nā habe uddhāra \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who disregard Your orders are wretches who will suffer for a hundred million births without release.**

*kusaṅga chāḍiye prabhu rākha tava pāya \|*  
*tava pādapadma vinā nā dekhi upāya \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My Lord, please rescue me from bad association and keep me by Your side,f or I can see no means of deliverance other than Your lotus feet.**

*haridāsa pada-dvandve vinoda yāhāra \|*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi sadā gāna tāra \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The lotus feet of Hari Das are the life of Bhaktivinoda, who forever sings this glorification of the jewel of the Holy Name.**

## 4 - Namaparadh: Blaspheming the saintly

*satāṁ nindā nāmnaḥ paramam aparādhaṁ vitanute*  
*yataḥ khyātiṁ yātaṁ katham u sahate tad vigarhām \|*

**Перевод:**

**To blaspheme the saintly is a supreme offense to the Holy Name, for how will the Name tolerate insults to those who spread Its glories? (*****Padma Purāṇa*****)**

*gadādhara-prāṇa jaya jāhnavā-jīvana \|*  
*jaya sītānātha śrīvāsādi bhakta-jana \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the life and soul of Gadadhar Pandit!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to the husband of Sita Devi!**  
**All glories to Srivasa Pandit and all the devotees!**

*prabhu bale haridāsa ebe sa-vistāre \|*  
*nāma aparādha vyākhyā kara ataḥ pare \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, O Hari Das, now please explain to Me in full all about the offenses to the Holy Name.**

*haridāsa bale prabhu more yā balābe \|*  
*tāhāi baliba āmi tomāra prabhāve \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das answered: O Lord, I will recite whatever You make me say, for everything I speak is inspired by You from within.**

## The Ten Offenses to the Holy Name

*nāma aparādha daśa-vidha śāstre kaya \|*  
*sei aparādhe mora baḍa haya bhaya \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures tell us there are ten kinds of offenses. All these offenses to the Holy Name make me very frightened.**

*eka eka kari āmi baliba sakala \|*  
*aparādhe vāṅci yāte deha more bala \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will enumerate them, one by one, and as I do, please give me the strength to avoid them, O my Lord.**

*sādhu-nindā anya-deva svātantrya manana \|*  
*nāma tattva guru āra śāstra vinindana \|\|6\|\|*  
*harināme artha vāda kalpita manana \|*  
*nāma-bale pāpa śraddhā-hīne nāmārpaṇa \|\|7\|\|*  
*anya śubha-karmera samāna kṛṣṇa-nāma \|*  
*e kathā balile aparādha aviśrāma \|\|8\|\|*  
*nāmete anavadhāna haya aparādha \|*  
*tāhāke purāṇa kartā balena pramāda \|\|9\|\|*  
*nāmera māhātmya jāne tabu nāhi bhaje \|*  
*ahaṁ mama āsaktite saṁsārete maje \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Blaspheming the saintly; thinking other gods are independent of the Lord; denigrating the teacher and the scriptures that explain the Name’s glories; inventing meanings of the Name or thinking its glories imaginary; sinning on the strength of chanting the Name, giving the Name to the faithless. Thinking that Krishna’s name is just another pious activity is another offense that has extensive repercussions; chanting inattentively is also an offense described by the author of the Puranas as** ***pramāda.*** **Remaining attached to ego and possessions and absorbed in material life, not worshiping the Holy Name, even after learning of Its glories.**

**Комментарий:**

The ten aparadhs are these: (1) To blaspheme of the devotees of the Lord; (2) To consider the gods to be independent and equal gods or to think that Lord Krishna’s name, form, qualities and pastimes are distinct or different from Him; (3) To disrespect the spiritual master who reveals the truth about the Holy Name; (4) To blaspheme the scriptures that describe the glories of the Holy Name; (5) To think the glories and fruits of the Holy Name found in the scriptures to be exaggerations or imaginary; (6) To commit sinful activities on the strength of chanting the Holy Name; (7) To give initiation in the Holy Name to a faithless person; (8) To equate chanting of the Holy Name with other pious activities; (9) To be inattentive while chanting the Holy Name; (10) To not develop love for the Holy Name even after learning Its glories because of one’s attachment to selfish purposes and possessions.

## To blaspheme the saintly is the first offense

*sādhu-nindā prathamāparādha bali’ jāni \|*  
*ei aparādhe jīvera haya sarva hāni \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To blaspheme a devotee of the Lord is called the first offense; this offense brings complete disruption to all aspects of life.**

## The essential and contingent characteristics of the saintly person

*sādhura lakṣaṇa tumi baliyācha prabho \|*  
*ekādaśe uddhavere kṛṣṇa-rūpe vibho \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As Krishna, You described the saintly person’s characteristics to Uddhava in the Eleventh Canto, O Lord.**

*dayālu sahiṣṇu sama droha-śūnya-vrata \|*  
*satya-sāra viśuddhātmā para-hite rata \|\|13\|\|*  
*kāme akṣubhita buddhi dānta akiñcana \|*  
*mṛdu śuci parimita-bhojī śānta-mana \|\|14\|\|*  
*anīha dhṛtimān sthira kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa \|*  
*apramatta sugambhīra vijita ṣaḍ-guṇa \|\|15\|\|*  
*amānī mānada dakṣa avañcaka jñānī \|*  
*ei saba lakṣaṇete sādhu bali jāni \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee is merciful, tolerant, equanimous, inimical to no living being, truthful, pure in heart, committed to the welfare of all living entities, his intelligence is not disrupted by desire; he is self-controlled and owns nothing; he is gentle, clean, regulated in his eating, and peaceful in mind; he is apathetic to great schemes, patient and steady. Krishna is his only shelter; he is free from intoxication; is grave and has untied the six knots, he expects no honors, yet respects all; he is expert, no cheater, and wise. I recognize a saintly person by all these characteristics.**

*ei saba lakṣaṇa prabho haya dvi-prakāra \|*  
*svarūpa taṭastha bhede kariba vicāra \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These characteristics can be divided into two types, O Lord: essential and contingent; I shall now explain which are which.**

**Комментарий:**

An essential characteristic (*svarūpa-lakṣaṇa*) is an attribute that cannot in any way be separated from a thing, while a contingent characteristic (*taṭastha-lakṣaṇa*) is one that only manifests from time to time, or when it comes in contact with some other thing.

## The essential characteristics are primary, the contingent characteristics are secondary and dependent

*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa haya svarūpa-lakṣaṇa \|*  
*taṭastha lakṣaṇe anya guṇera gaṇana \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A saintly person’s essential characteristic is that Krishna is his only shelter; I consider the other qualities I have listed to be contingent characteristics.**

*kona bhāgye sādhu-saṅge nāme ruci haya \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma gāya kare kṛṣṇa-pādāśraya \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If by some chance one associates with a sadhu, he will get a taste for the Holy Name; He sings the Krishna’s names and takes complete shelter of Krishna’s lotus feet.**

*svarūpa lakṣaṇa sei ha-ita ha-ila \|*  
*gāite gāite nāma anya guṇa āila \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From this one act, all the other, secondary qualities develop; and the more he chants, the more he develops all these virtues.**

*anya guṇa-gaṇa tāi taṭastha gaṇana \|*  
*avaśya vaiṣṇava dehe habe saṅghaṭana \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, though these virtues are only secondary characteristics, they inevitably manifest in the body of the Vaishnava.**

## Saintliness depends on one’s surrender to Krishna, not on Varnashram designations or external dress

*varṇāśrama cihna nānā-veṣera racanā \|*  
*sādhura lakṣaṇe kabhu nā haya gaṇanā \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One dresses differently according to his Varnashram status, but these are nowhere counted as characteristics of a saintly person.**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-śaraṇāgati sādhura lakṣaṇa \|*  
*tāra mukhe haya kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Surrender, or taking exclusive shelter of Krishna, is the sign of a sadhu; kirtan of Krishna’s holy names is always on such a person’s lips.**

*gṛhī brahmacārī vānaprastha nyāsi-bhede \|*  
*śūdra vaiśya kṣatra vipra-gaṇera prabhede \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The stages of life are householder, student, retired and renounced, the four classes are worker, merchant, soldier-politician, and intellectual-priest.**

**Комментарий:**

The four stages of life are known as ashrams*.* One who is legally married and lives with his wife is called a grihastha, or householder. One who prior to marriage studies in the company of other students is known as a brahmachari, or celibate student. One who has reached a certain age and goes to live in seclusion is called a vanaprastha, or retired person. One who has become fully renounced and indifferent to family relations is called a sannyasi or renunciate.

*sādhutva kakhana nāhi ha-ibe nirṇīta \|*  
*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa sādhu śāstrera vihita \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**None of these designations indicate whether or not someone is saintly; the scriptures tell us a sadhu is known by his exclusive commitment to Krishna.**

## The symptoms of a householder sadhu

*raghunātha-dāse lakṣye kariyā se bāra \|*  
*gṛhi-sādhu-jane śikhāyecha ei sāra \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On one occasion, You instructed everyone how to be a saintly householder through these verses that You spoke to Raghunath Das:**

**Комментарий:**

Raghunath Das lived in Saptagram. He belonged to the Kayastha or writer caste, which is socially lower than the Brahmins and generally served the function of accountants and scribes. Raghunath is counted among the Six Goswamis of Vrindavan and is often called Das Goswami.

*sthira haye ghare yāo nā hao bātula \|*  
*krame krame pāya loka bhava-sindhu-kula \|\|27\|\|*  
*markaṭa vairāgya chāḍa loka dekhāiyā \|*  
*yathāyogya viṣaya bhuñja anāsakta hañā \|\|28\|\|*  
*antare niṣṭhā kara bāhye loka vyavahāra \|*  
*acire śrī-kṛṣṇa tomāya karibe uddhāra \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Calm down and return home, Raghunath. Don’t be foolish. It takes time to cross the ocean of material suffering. Don’t take up ‘monkey renunciation’ simply to impress others; rather, enjoy worldly life in a moderate way without attachment. Fix your mind on Krishna while outwardly dealing with the world in an appropriate manner; It will not be long before Krishna delivers you.”**

**Комментарий:**

“Monkey renunciation” (*markaṭa vairāgya*) is the false renunciation of someone who adopts the dress of a monk while still harboring desires to enjoy sense gratification.

## The symptoms of a renounced sadhu

*punaḥ tumi tāra dekhi vairāgya grahaṇa \|*  
*ei mata śikṣā dile apūrva śravaṇa \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Later, when Raghunath Das left home and embraced a renunciate’s life, You again personally instructed him in the following wonderful way:**

*grāmya-kathā nā śunibe grāmya-vārtā nā kahibe \|*  
*bhāla nā khāibe āra bhāla nā paribe \|\|31\|\|*  
*amānī mānada hañā kṛṣṇa-nāma sadā labe \|*  
*vraje rādhā-kṛṣṇa-sevā mānase karibe \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Do not listen to gossip nor engage in gossip yourself. You should not eat very palatable food, nor dress very nicely. Expect no honors, but give respect to everyone; always chant Krishna’s name and mentally render service to Radha and Krishna in Vrindavan.”**

## The essential characteristic of both the householder and sannyasi sadhu is the same

*svarūpa lakṣaṇa eka sarvatra samāna \|*  
*āśramādi bhede pṛthak taṭastha vidhāna \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The essential characteristic of every saintly person is always the same, thought the contingent characteristics may vary according to his life station.**

*ananya-śaraṇe yadi dekhi durācāra \|*  
*tathāpi se sādhu bali sevya sabākāra \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What is more, if I see immoral actions in one who has taken full shelter, I still consider him a saintly person and worthy of service.**

**Комментарий:**

Taking exclusive shelter of Krishna is the hallmark of a pure devotee of Krishna. If someone has that essential characteristic, then the other, secondary characteristics will inevitably follow. If for any reason, however, the secondary characteristics or ethical virtues are not fully present, then though we may be tempted to consider that person immoral or even evil, we should not, for he is in fact a saintly person.

*ei ta śrī-kṛṣṇa-vākya gītā-bhāgavate \|*  
*ihāke pūjiba yatne sadā sarva mate \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This has been stated by Lord Krishna in the Gitaand the Bhagavatam, and it should be fully honored, like everything that Krishna says.**

*ihāte āche ta eka nigūḍha siddhānta \|*  
*kṛpā kari jānāyecha tāi pāi anta \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Within this statement there is a profound teaching, O Lord, that I have only been able to understand by Your grace.**

## It is offensive to criticize one who has taken full shelter of Krishna for his past sins, or the vestiges of his past sinful activity

*kṛṣṇa-nāme ruci yabe ha-ibe udaya \|*  
*eka nāme pūrva pāpa ha-ibeka kṣaya \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When someone develops a real taste for chanting Krishna’s names, then a single utterance of the Name destroys all previous sins.**

*pūrva-pāpa gandha tabu thāke kichu dina \|*  
*nāmera prabhāve krama hañā paḍe kṣīṇa \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even so, some vestiges of past sins may yet remain for some time, but these remnants are progressively lessened by the influence of the Name.**

**Комментарий:**

One who has a taste for chanting the Holy Name is freed from all past sins. The effects of past sins will no doubt remain for some time, but they too are eventually destroyed by the power of the Holy Name.

*śīghra sei pāpa-gandha vidūrita haya \|*  
*parama dharmātmā bali haya paricaya \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The very scent of a sinful life is quickly eradicated and the chanter is recognized as one fully committed to righteousness.**

*ye kayeka dina sei gandha nāhi yāya \|*  
*sādhāraṇa jana cakṣe pāpa bali bhāya \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nevertheless, if such sinful vestiges do not disappear immediately, they appear to an ordinary person as sinfulness.**

*se pāpa dekhiyā yei sādhu-nindā kare \|*  
*pūrva-pāpa lakṣi punaḥ avajñā ācare \|\|41\|\|*  
*sei ta pāṣaṇḍī vaiṣṇavera nindā doṣe \|*  
*nāma aparādha maji paḍe kṛṣṇa-roṣe \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even so, if one criticizes a devotee for such unethical and immoral behavior or remembers his past faults and disrespects him on that account, he is no better than an atheist because of his blasphemy; immersed in offenses to the Holy Name, he earns Krishna’s wrath. It is an offense to criticize a Vaishnava for sins he may have committed before having taken shelter of the path of devotion, or for lingering aspects of his character that may have been formed at that time.**

## Taking shelter of Krishna is the principal symptom of a sadhu; Claiming to be a sadhu is simply arrogance

*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa mātra kṛṣṇa-nāma gāya \|*  
*sādhu-nāme paricita kṛṣṇera kṛpāya \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As soon as one fully surrenders to Krishna, he chants Krishna’s names His reputation as a holy person comes about through Krishna’s blessings.**

*kṛṣṇa-bhakta vyatīta nāhika sādhu āra \|*  
*āmi sādhu bali haya dambha avatāra \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No one is more entitled to be called a sadhu than a devotees of Krishna, but one who says, “I am a sadhu” is the personification of arrogance.**

**Комментарий:**

The personification of arrogance is a hypocrite and a braggart, or one who tries to profit from his status as a holy man to make a living.

## A brief description of the sadhu

*se balibe—āmi dīna kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma yāra mukhe sādhu sei jana \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, one who says, “I am lowly and have no shelter other than Krishna,” and on whose lips Krishna’s name is always present is the truly saintly person.**

*tṛṇa haite hīna bali āpanāke jāne \|*  
*sahiṣṇu tarura nyāya āpanāke māne \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He knows himself to be lower than a blade of grass, and tries to be as tolerant as a tree.**

*nije ta amānī āra sakale mānada \|*  
*tāra mukhe kṛṣṇa-nāma kṛṣṇa-rati-prada \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He asks for no respect for himself, while giving honor to all others. Hearing the Holy Name from such a person will give you love for Krishna.**

## A Vaishnava devoted to the Holy Name is a real sadhu; Krishna’s powers pervade his body

*hena sādhu-mukhe yabe śuni eka nāma \|*  
*vaiṣṇava baliyā tāre kariba praṇāma \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If I hear the Holy Name even once from the lips of such a saint, then I recognize him as a Vaishnava and bow down to him.**

*vaiṣṇava se jagad-guru jagatera bandhu \|*  
*vaiṣṇava sakala jīve sadā kṛpā sindhu \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such a Vaishnava is the teacher of the world, a friend to all creatures, and an ocean of mercy to every living being.**

*e hena vaiṣṇava-nindā yei jana kare \|*  
*narake paḍibe se janma-janmāntare \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The rascal who blasphemes a Vaishnava of such stature will surely go to hell for lifetime after lifetime.**

*bhakti labhibāre āra nāhika upāya \|*  
*bhakti labhe sarva-jīva vaiṣṇava kṛpāya \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He loses all chance of attaining devotion to Krishna, for it is through the blessings of the Vaishnava that one gets bhakti.**

*vaiṣṇava dehete thāke śrī-kṛṣṇera śakti \|*  
*sei deha sparśe anye haya kṛṣṇa-bhakti \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s potencies take up residence in the Vaishnava’s body so that by merely touching him, one develops devotion to Krishna.**

**Комментарий:**

Devotion is the combined essence of the Lord’s hladini and samvit potencies. Such devotion is transmitted when a perfected devotee invests a seeker with the spirit of devotion. When that practitioner himself attains the perfection of devotion, he goes on to further invest some other seeker with that spirit. Devotion is a particular function of the transcendental nature; it uses the soul as a vehicle to reside and move about in this world. When a particular individual soul puts aside his resistance to devotion, then he becomes a potential carrier and it is possible for devotion to be transfered into him. The way this operates is a divine mystery.

*vaiṣṇava adharāmṛta āra pada jala \|*  
*vaiṣṇavera pada-rajaḥ tina mahā-bala \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The remnants of the Vaishnava’s food, the water that has washed his feet, the dust from his lotus feet—these are three powerful spiritual substances.**

## The transfer of a Vaishnava’s spiritual power

*vaiṣṇava-nikaṭe yadi baise kata-lakṣaṇa \|*
