*deha haite haya kṛṣṇa-śakti niḥsaraṇa \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one should sit near such a Vaishnava for a few moments, he will feel the spiritual power exuding from his body.**

*sei śakti śraddhāvān hṛdaye paśiyā \|*  
*bhaktira udaya kare deha kāṅpāiyā \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That spiritual potency then flows into a faithful person’s heart, causing devotion to well up in him and his body to tremble.**

*ye basila vaiṣṇavera nikaṭe śraddhāya \|*  
*tāhāra hṛdaye bhakti ha-ibe udaya \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**So anyone who sits down near a devotee with an attitude of faith will certainly experience the awakening of devotion in his heart.**

*prathame āsibe tāra mukhe kṛṣṇa-nāma \|*  
*nāmera prabhāve pābe sarva-guṇa-grāma \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The immediate symptom will be that he will begin chanting Krishna’s name; then, by the influence of the Name, he will obtain all good qualities.**

## The four categories of Vaishnava-ninda

*vaiṣṇavera jāti āra pūrva doṣa dhare \|*  
*kādācitka doṣa dekhi yei nindā kare \|\|58\|\|*  
*naṣṭa-prāya doṣa laye kare apamāna \|*  
*yama-daṇḍe kaṣṭa pāya se saba ajñāna \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who criticizes a Vaishnava for his birth, for previous flaws, or for some accidental fall-down that may have taken place, or insults him because of some remaining character flaw, is engaged in ignorant behavior that earns him the Lord of Death’s chastisement.**

**Комментарий:**

The blasphemer of the Vaishnavas is one who (1) criticizes a devotee for faults arising from his birth in a low caste or outcaste, (2) for faults committed accidentally or unintentionally, (3) for certain bad habits that he has not yet been able to overcome, (4) or for sins committed before he took shelter of the Lord. A person who wantonly blasphemes the devotees in this manner will never experience pleasure in chanting the Holy Names. Anyone who has taken to the path of pure devotion is a pure devotee. Even so, the four kinds of deficiencies mentioned here may appear in the devotee—he may be of a low birth, he may have been very sinful before taking to the devotional path, he may still engage in some unintentional sinful activity or have some bad habits or character flaws that he has not been able to rid himself of—but he cannot have any other kind of flaw. In other words, he cannot engage in deliberate, intentional sinful activity. That would make him a hypocrite and mean that he is no longer a genuine sadhu with the principal characteristic of being fully surrendered to Krishna—*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa.*

*vaiṣṇavera mukhe nāma-māhātmya-pracāra \|*  
*se vaiṣṇava-nindā kṛṣṇa nāhi sahe āra \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The glories of the Holy Name are constantly on the Vaishnava’s tongue, so how can Krishna abide someone who wantonly criticizes him?**

*dharma yoga yāga jñāna-kāṇḍa parihari \|*  
*ye bhajila kṛṣṇa-nāma sei sarvopari \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A person who has abandoned other paths of piety or philosophy to worship Krishna’s name stands above all other human beings.**

## The sadhu who takes complete shelter of the Holy Name does not blaspheme other gods or other teachings

*anyadera anya-śāstra nā kari nindana \|*  
*nāmera āśraya laya śuddha sādhu-jana \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A pure, saintly Vaishnava never blasphemes another’s gods or scriptures; his only concern is to take complete shelter of the Holy Name.**

*se sādhu gṛhastha ha-u athavā sannyāsī \|*  
*tāhāra caraṇa-reṇu pāite prayāsī \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should endeavor to take the dust from his lotus feet, irrespective of whether he is a householder or sannyasi.**

*yāba yata nāme rati se tata vaiṣṇava \|*  
*vaiṣṇavera krama ei mate anubhava \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A Vaishnava’s advancement is measured by his affection for the Holy Name; one can subjectively understand one’s own progress in the same way. One is a Vaishnava to the degree that he has affection or attachment for chanting the Holy Name.**

*ithe varṇāśrama dhana pāṇḍitya yauvana \|*  
*kona kārya nāhi kare rūpa bala jana \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In assessing someone’s status as a Vaishnava, class, social position, wealth, learning, youth or beauty, power or followers, mean nothing.**

*ata eva yini karilena nāmāśraya \|*  
*sādhu-nindā chāḍibena e dharma niścaya \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, the main duty of one who has taken shelter of the Holy Name is to give up the propensity to criticize the saintly devotees.**

*nāmāśrayā śuddhā bhakti bhakta bhakti-rūpā \|*  
*bhakta bhakti-vivarjitā ha-ile virūpā \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pure bhakti through refuge in the Name manifests as devotion to the devotee; chanting the Name without feeling love for the devotees is an aberration.**

*yāṅhā sādhu-nindā tāṅhā nāhi bhakti sthiti \|*  
*ata eva aparādhe tathā pariṇati \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Devotion cannot remain where there is blasphemy of devotees, therefore such an offense has dangerous ramifications.**

*sādhu-nindā chāḍi bhakta sādhu-bhakti kare \|*  
*sādhu-saṅga sādhu-sevā ei dharmācare \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**An aspirant should give up blasphemy and serve the Vaishnavas with affection, for this path is based on associating with holy persons and serving them.**

## Bad association is of two types, the first being association with womanizers

*asat-saṅga-tyāge haya vaiṣṇava-ācāra \|*  
*asat-saṅge haya sādhu-avajñā apāra \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One can only conduct oneself like a Vaishnava by shunning bad company, for one who remains in bad company inevitably shows disrespect to the saintly.**

*asat ye dvi-prakāra sarva-śāstre kaya \|*  
*sei duiyera madhye yoṣit-saṅgī eka haya \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures tell us that there are two kinds of bad company: the first of which is association with those addicted to womanizing.**

**Комментарий:**

The two kinds of bad company are the womanizer (*yoṣit-saṅgī*) and the non-devotee (*abhakta*). A woman devotee similarly should avoid the company of women who are addicted to flirtations with many men. There are two kinds of “womanizer”: the man who seeks illicit sexual relations with numerous different woman and the married man who is completely subjugated to the will of his wife. It goes without saying that a devotee should avoid such behavior himself.

*yoṣit-saṅgi-saṅgī punaḥ tāra madhye gaṇya \|*  
*tāra saṅga-tyāge jīva ha-ibeka dhanya \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who keeps the company of womanizers is also counted among those to avoid. By so doing, the jiva becomes fortunate.**

## A householder can also be a womanizer

*kṛṣṇere saṁsāre ye dāmpatya dharma thāke \|*  
*asat baliyā śāstra nā bale tāhāke \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is a legitimate householder life that is dedicated to Krishna; such a married existence is not condemned as impious in the scripture.**

*adharma saṁyoge āra straiṇa bhāve rata \|*  
*yoṣit-saṅgī jana duṣṭa śāstrera sammata \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**However, if a man becomes too attached to sensual pleasure in marriage, the scripture condemns him too as a type of womanizer.**

## The second kind of bad association, the non-devotee is of three kinds

*kṛṣṇete abhakta – asat dvitīya prakāra \|*  
*māyāvādī dharma-dhvajī nirīśvara āra \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who have no devotion to Krishna are the second kind of bad company. This group includes the Mayavadis, hypocrites, and atheists.**

**Комментарий:**

Mayavadis are those who do not accept that the Supreme Lord’s form is eternal. They say that the various forms of the Lord like Krishna, Nrisingha or Rama, as well as the jiva, are products of the material nature. A hypocrite (*dharma-dhvajī*) is a person who “waves the flag of religion,” i.e., makes an external show of devotion or renunciation in order to achieve some personal ends, while internally he possesses neither of these qualities. The word for atheist used here is *nirīśvara*, one who does not accept the existence of God.

## One should avoid the person who says criticizing such people is sadhu ninda

*varjile e saba saṅga sādhu-nindā naya \|*  
*ihāke ye nindā bale sei varjya haya \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rejecting all these kinds of association is not sadhu ninda. If someone says that it is, his company should also be avoided.**

*ei saba saṅga chāḍi ananya-śaraṇa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma kari pāya kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever rejects all such bad company, takes exclusive shelter of Krishna and chants His names, will attain love for His lotus feet.**

## The neophyte devotee

*sādhu-sevā-hīna arce laukika śraddhāya \|*  
*prākṛta vaiṣṇava haya vaiṣṇavera prāya \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A person with conventional faith who serves Krishna’s deity form, but does not serve the sadhu, is a mundane Vaishnava or “not quite a Vaishnava.”**

*vaiṣṇava-ābhāsa sei nahe ta’ vaiṣṇava \|*  
*kemane pāibe sādhu-saṅgera vaiṣṇava \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He is a mere facsimile of a Vaishnava and not truly a devotee, so how will he find a devotee with whom to have sainty association?**

*ata eva kaniṣṭha madhyete tāre gaṇi \|*  
*tāre kṛpā karibena vaiṣṇava āpani \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such a devotee is considered to be at the lowest stage of advancement; if he is fortunate, a pure Vaishnava will one day bless him out of spontaneous kindness.**

## The intermediate Vaishnava

*kṛṣṇa-prema kṛṣṇa-bhakte maitrī ācaraṇa \|*  
*bāliśete kṛpā āra dveṣī upekṣaṇa \|\|81\|\|*  
*karile madhyama bhakta śuddha bhakta hana \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāme adhikāra karena arjana \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who loves Krishna, and treats Krishna’s devotees as his friends, who is compassionate to the innocent and shuns the Lord’s enemies  is a pure devotee on the middle rung of advancement. He is earning the qualifications to chant the Holy Name.**

## The most advanced Vaishnava

*sarvatra yāṅhāra haya kṛṣṇa-daraśana \|*  
*kṛṣṇe sakalera sthiti kṛṣṇa prāṇa dhana \|\|83\|\|*  
*vaiṣṇavāvaiṣṇava-bheda nāhi thāke tāṅra \|*  
*vaiṣṇava uttama tini kṛṣṇa-nāma-sāra \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who sees Lord Krishna manifest everywhere in all things,and that everything rests in Him; for whom Krishna is his life and soul, no longer distinguishes between Vaishnava and non-Vaishnava. This person is on the highest level—the Holy Name is everything to him.**

## The intermediate devotee serves the Vaishnavas

*ata eva madhyama vaiṣṇava mahāśaya \|*  
*sādhu sevā rata sadā thākena niścaya \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Vaishnava on the middle level of advancement is a great soul and certainly remains fixed in the service of the saintly.**

**Комментарий:**

The *madhyama* devotee is already counted amongst the pure devotees. He is capable of discerning devotees from those who are not, especially as he feels the need to be engaged in serving the pure devotees. If a devotee on this level does not make distinctions between the Vaishnava and the non-Vaishnava, he ends up committing offenses. The madhyama Vaishnava goes out of his way to find advanced devotees so that he can serve them. On the other hand, the advanced devotee makes no such distinctions, as he cannot be adversely affected by bad association: he sees the divine presence in even the greatest atheist. He needs not seek out Vaishnava association for his own personal benefit. At the same time, he cannot commit offenses to the Vaishnava, as he sees the divine presence in all beings, and treats them accordingly.

## The mundane devotee can only chant the semblance of the Name

*prākṛta vaiṣṇava yei vaiṣṇavera prāya \|*  
*nāmābhāse adhikārī sarva-śāstra pāya \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The mundane devotee, who is not quite a Vaishnava, is only qualified to chant Namabhasa, so say all the scriptures.**

## The intermediate devotee is eligible to chant the Holy Name and he must avoid the offenses to the Name

*madhyama vaiṣṇava mātra nāme adhikārī \|*  
*śrī-nāma-bhajane aparādhera vicārī \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Only an intermediate Vaishnava is eligible to chant the Name, for he is able to recognize the offenses to the Name.**

*uttama vaiṣṇave aparādha asambhava \|*  
*sarvatra dekhena tini kṛṣṇera vaibhava \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is impossible for the truly advanced devotee to commit offenses, for he sees Krishna’s glories wherever he looks.**

*nija nija adhikāra kariyā vicāra \|*  
*sādhu-nindā aparādha kari parihāra \|\|89\|\|*  
*sādhu saṅga sādhu sevā nāma saṅkīrtana \|*  
*sarva jīve dayā ei bhakta ācaraṇa \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Every devotee should judge his own level of advancement and take care to avoid blaspheming other devotees; he should associate with and serve other Vaishnavas, chant the Name, and show compassion to all beings—this is the behavior of a devotee.**

**Комментарий:**

It is absolutely necessary for everyone to reflect on his own nature and level of spiritual advancement. The Vaishnava way of spiritual life means chanting the Holy Name while determinedly performing the duties that are associated with one’s own qualifications.

## What should one do if he commits offenses to the saintly?

*pramāde yadyapi ghaṭe sādhu-vigarhaṇa \|*  
*tabe anutāpe dhari se sādhu-caraṇa \|\|91\|\|*  
*kāṅdiyā baliba prabho kṣami aparādha \|*  
*e duṣṭa-nindake kara vaiṣṇava-prasāda \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If at any time you should accidentally abuse a Vaishnava then you should touch that devotee’s feet in a spirit of repentance; weeping, you should say, “Prabhu, forgive me my offense and, even though I am a wicked blasphemer, give me your Vaishnava blessings.”**

*sādhu baḍa dayāmaya tabe ārdra-mane \|*  
*kṣamibena aparādha kṛpā āliṅgane \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee by nature is compassionate. Moved by your contrition he will forgive and mercifully embrace you.**

*ei ta prathama aparādhera vicāra \|*  
*śrī-caraṇe nivedinu ājñā anusāra \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I here conclude my explanation of the first offense to the Holy Name which I offer to Your lotus feet in accordance with Your order.**

*hari-dāsa pāda-padme bhrara ye jana \|*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi tāhāra jīvana \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This Harinama Chintamani.is the life and soul of Bhaktivinoda, who is like a bumble-bee, lingering at the lotus feet of Hari Das Thakur.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the fouth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “The offense of blaspheming the saintly.”

## 5 - The offense of seeing other gods as independent of Krishna

*śivasya śrī viṣṇor ya iha guṇa nāmādi sakalaṁ*  
*dhiyā bhinnaṁ paśyet sa khalu hari nāmāhita karaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who considers the all auspicious Lord Viṣṇu's name, form, qualities and pastimes to be distinct from his person in the way that material signifiers are different from the signified object is acting in a way displeasing to the Holy Name.**

*jaya gadādhara-prāṇa jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*jaya sītānātha jaya gaura bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the life and soul of Gadadhar Prabhu!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to the husband of Sita Devi!**  
**All glories to all the devotees of Gauranga Mahaprabhu!**

*haridāsa bale tabe kari yoḍa-hāta \|*  
*dvitīyāparādha ebe śuna jagannātha \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das continued to speak, folding his hands: “O Lord of the Universe! Hear now about the second offense against the Holy Name.”**

## The Supreme Godhead

*parama advaya jñāna viṣṇu para-tattva \|*  
*cit-svarūpa jagadīśa sadā śuddha-sattva \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Vishnu is the one Supreme Truth, non-dual and transcendental. His essence is spiritual, pure being; He is the Lord of the universes.**

*goloka-vihārī kṛṣṇa se tattvera sāra \|*  
*catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi guṇe alaṅkṛta rasādhāra \|\|4\|\|*  
*ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa nārāyaṇa svarūpe prakāśa \|*  
*sei ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa viṣṇu-sāmānya-vilāsa \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The essence of the Godhead is Krishna, whose abode is Goloka, He is the repository of sixty-four qualities and all the rasas. Sixty of those excellences manifest in Lord Narayan; these sixty qualities are common to all the forms of Vishnu.**

*puruṣāvatāre āra svāṁśa avatāre \|*  
*sei ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa spaṣṭa kārya anusāre \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This includes the Purush avatars and other personal expansions of the Lord, who clearly display these sixty qualities in accordance with their actions.**

**Комментарий:**

Vishnu is another name for Narayan, the Lord of Vaikuntha. He is Lord Krishna’s *vilāsa-vigraha* or partial expansion. The Sankarshan form of Narayan in Vaikuntha expands into the Causal Ocean as Maha Vishnu, the first Purusha expansion. Maha Vishnu expands into undifferentiated matter as Garbhodakashayi Vishnu, the totality of all spiritual being within the material universes. This form of Vishnu then expands further as Kshirodakasayi Vishnu, who is the representative Purusha form residing within every living entity. These then are the three Purusha Avatars. Kshirodakashayi Vishnu is the source of the incarnations of personal portions like Matsya and Kurma. All these incarnations are forms of the same Vishnu principle and possess the sixty transcendental characteristics described in the *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu* (2.1). The empowered incarnations (Shaktyavesh avatars) are Vishnu’s separated portions, *jīva-tattva*, as exemplified by Parashuram and Prithu.

## The jivas are Vishnu’s separated parts

*viṣṇura ye vibhinnāṁśa dui ta prakāra \|*  
*pañcāśat guṇa jīve bindu bindu āra \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vishnu’s separated parts are of two types. The first, the jivas, possess only fifty of the Lord’s qualities, and those only in part.**

## The gods are also separated parts, but are not ordinary jivas

*giriśādi deve ei guṇa pañcāśat \|*  
*tad-adhika parimāṇe sarvadā saṁyuta \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Shiva and the other gods possess the same fifty divine virtues, but in greater quantity than found in the ordinary jivas.**

**Комментарий:**

The gods possess more of these qualities than the infinitesimal jiva souls. The jivas possess them in miniscule quantities, whereas the gods have them in greater amounts.

*tad-vyatīta āra pañca guṇa aṁśa māne \|*  
*prakāśita āche tabe vicitra-vidhāne \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Besides these, they possess another five special qualities that are found in them partially, in accordance with their functions.**

**Комментарий:**

Shiva and the other gods possess five extra qualities, but only in part when compared to their full manifestation, which occurs only in the Vishnu forms.

## Sixty qualities appear in the Vishnu forms

*sei pañca pañcāśat guṇa-pūrṇa tāya \|*  
*viṣṇute virājamāna sarva śāstre gāya \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the scriptures tell us that these fifty-five qualities are present to the fullest degree in the Vishnu forms.**

*tad-vyatīta āra pañca-guṇa nārāyaṇe \|*  
*āche tāra sattā kabhu nāhi anya jane \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Besides them, however, another five qualities are found only in Narayan. These are only found in Him and not in any other living being.**

*ṣaṣṭhi-guṇe viṣṇu-tattva parama īśvara \|*  
*giriśa ādi anya-deva tāṅhāra kiṅkara \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Possessing sixty qualities in full, Vishnu is thus the Supreme Lord, whereas Shiva and the other gods are His servitors.**

*vibhinnāṁśa giriśādi jīva śreṣṭhatara \|*  
*viṣṇu sarva-jīveśvara sarva-deveśvara \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The gods are the Lord’s separated parts, but superior to other jivas, whereas Vishnu is master and controller of both the jivas and gods.**

## The ignorant equate Lord Vishnu with the gods

*anya-deva saha sama viṣṇuke ye māne \|*  
*se baḍa ajñāna īśa-tattva nāhi jāne \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who equates Lord Vishnu with the other gods is an ignorant fool who knows nothing of the principle of Godhead.**

*e jaḍa jagate viṣṇu parama īśvara \|*  
*giriśādi yata deva tāṅra vidhikara \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vishnu is the supreme master and controller of this creation, whereas Shiva and the other gods simply carry out His orders.**

*keha bale māyāra tri-guṇe tridiveśa \|*  
*sarvadā samāna brahma tattva sa-viśeṣa \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say there are three gods, one for each of the gunas of Nature; but always the same truth of Brahman, but with attributes.**

**Комментарий:**

This is the Mayavadi position. Mayavadis say that Brahman has no attributes, but accepts them for the purpose of the material creation. When Brahman manifests in the material creation, He takes on different forms, principal among which are the three deities governing the gunas of nature—passion, goodness and ignorance—and performing the functions of creation, destruction and maintenance.

## Resolving various controversies

*śāstrera siddhānte tabu pūjya nārāyaṇa \|*  
*brahmā śiva sṛṣṭi-laya kāryera kāraṇa \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**According to the verdict of the scriptures, only Narayan is worshipable; Brahma and Shiva are the causes of the work of creation and destruction.**

*vāsudeva chāḍi yei anya-deve bhaje \|*  
*īśvara chāḍiyā sei saṁsārete maje \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who ignores Lord Vishnu to worship any other god, has rejected the true God and remains immersed in material existence.**

*keha bale—viṣṇu para-tattva baṭe jāni \|*  
*sarva-viṣṇumaya viśva veda-vākya māni \|\|19\|\|*  
*ata eva sarva-deve viṣṇu adhiṣṭhāna \|*  
*sarva devārcane haya viṣṇura samāna \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say: “Of course, I accept that Vishnu is the one Supreme Truth, and I accept the Vedic teaching that He pervades the entire universe. Even so, I believe that because He is present within all the gods, we are in fact venerating Him when we worship other gods.”**

*ei ta niṣedha-para vākya vidhi naya \|*  
*anya deva pūjāra niṣedha ei haya \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such statements are meant as a prohibition and not as an injunction; their real purpose is to discourage the worship of other gods.**

**Комментарий:**

When the scriptures say that the other gods are aspects of Lord Vishnu, this is not done to incite people to worship the gods, but rather the reverse. When one worships Lord Vishnu, all the other gods are automatically honored, so there is no need to offer them separate worship.

*sarva viṣṇu-maya viśva e kathā balile \|*  
*viṣṇu-pūjā kaile saba deve pūjā mile \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The entire universe is pervaded by Vishnu, but saying this means that by worshiping Him one gets whatever benefits come from worshiping other gods.**

*taru-mūle jala dile śākhāra ullāsa \|*  
*pallave ḍhālile jala vṛkṣera vināśa \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By watering the roots of a tree, all the branches are nourished; if one only waters the leaves and twigs, however, it withers and dies.**

*ata eva pūji viṣṇu anya-deva tyaji’ \|*  
*tāhātei anya deva kāje kāje pūji \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I therefore worship Vishnu alone and not the other gods, for by so doing I worship the other gods automatically.**

*ei vidhi – devera sammata cira-dina \|*  
*durvipāke ei vidhi chāḍe arvācīna \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the Vedic injunction, approved from time immemorial; only inexperienced and unfortunate persons ignore this principle.**

**Комментарий:**

The jiva’s misfortune is the result of tendencies deeply rooted in his nature. One who is bewildered by the material nature does not know that the worship of Lord Vishnu is beyond the three gunas of Prakriti and is thus eternally approved by the Vedas.

*māyāvāda-doṣe jīva kali āgamane \|*  
*bahu-deva pūje viṣṇu-sāmānya-darśane \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With the advent of Kali, jivas are contaminated by Mayavada, and so worship many gods, thinking them all to be equal to Lord Vishnu.**

*eka eka deva eka eka phala dātā \|*  
*sarva phala dātā viṣṇu sakalera pātā \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Each of the gods has the power to bestow certain specific gifts; but only Lord Vishnu can offer all benedictions and protect everyone.**

*kāmi jana yadi tattva jānibāre pāre \|*  
*viṣṇu pūji phala pāya chāḍe devāntare \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Should those with material desires come to know of Lord Vishnu’s status, they give up their gods and worship Him alone to get what they want.**

## A householder Vaishnava’s responsibilities

*gṛhastha ha-iyā yei viṣṇu-bhakta haya \|*  
*sarva-kārye kṛṣṇa pūje chāḍiyā saṁśaya \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who have taken up householder life and are devotees of Vishnu should worship Lord Krishna in everything they do without fear or doubt.**

*niṣekādi śmaśānānta saṁskāra yata \|*  
*tāhāte pūjaye kṛṣṇa veda mantra mata \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In all the rites of passage, from conception to the cremation ground, they should worship Krishna in accordance with the Vedic mantras.**

*viṣṇu vaiṣṇavera pūjā vedete vidhāna \|*  
*deva-pitṛ-gaṇe kṛṣṇa nirmālya pradāna \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The real injunction of the Vedas is to worship of Vishnu and the Vaishnavas; one honors the forefathers and gods by offering them Vishnu’s remnants.**

*māyāvādi-mate pitṛ-śrāddha yei kare \|*  
*yevā anyadeva pūje aparādhe mare \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever offers shraddh to the forefathers following the Mayavadi beliefs or worships other gods, commits an offence and comes to ruin.**

*viṣṇu-tattve dvaita-buddhi nāma aparādha \|*  
*sei aparādhe tāra haya bhakti bādha \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Another offence is to think that the different forms of the Godhead are distinct from Vishnu; this is an obstacle on the path of devotion.**

*śivādi devatā gaṇe pṛthak īśvara \|*  
*mānile nāmāparādha haya bhayaṅkara \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To consider gods like Shiva to be separate supreme divinities is an offence with fearful consequences.**

**Комментарий:**

Vishnu is God, but Shiva and the other gods are also gods or *īśvaras* is polytheism, and ths is an offense. However, if one offers respects to the deities as Krishna’s empowered servants or as incarnations of His qualities, then there is no offence. None of the gods has any power independent of those given by Lord Vishnu, the one Supreme Lord.

*viṣṇu-śakti parā śakti haite deva yata \|*  
*bhinna śakti siddha naya vedera sammata \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the gods derive their powers from the One Supreme Powerful; the Veda does not support that they are independently powerful.**

*śiva-brahmā-gaṇapati-sūrya-dikpāla \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti-balete īśvara cira-kāla \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Shiva, Brahma, Ganesh, Surya and the lords of the ten directions have been invested with Krishna’s potencies and hence are powerful.**

*ata eva pareśvara eka-mātra jāni \|*  
*āra saba deve tāṅra śakti-madhye gaṇi \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I conclude therefore that there is only one Supreme Lord and all other deities are only different aspects of His potencies.**

*ata eva sarva-kārye karma jaḍa bhāva \|*  
*chāḍiyā gṛhastha pāya bhaktira sad-bhāva \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Householder devotees should give up all mundane fruitive activities and simply seek out pure devotional service in whatever they do.**

## How Vaishnavas satisfy their householder obligations

*bhaktira sad-bhāve thāki’ sat-kriyā karaṇe \|*  
*deva-pitṛ-gaṇe tuṣe nirmālya arpaṇe \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Householders should remain fixed in devotion while making an honest livelihood; they may satisfy the gods and forefathers with offerings of Krishna prasad.**

*bahu deva-devī pūjā karibe varjana \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakta bali’ sabe karibe tarpaṇa \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They should abandon the worship of many gods and goddesses, but may make them offerings knowing they are Krishna’s devotees.**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa vaiṣṇavārcane sarva phala pāya \|*  
*nāme aparādha nahe sadā nāma gāya \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One can realize all one’s goals through worshiping Krishna and His bhaktas; such a person always chant the Holy Names, but never does so offensively.**

## Rules for the four varnas

*jagate mānava-gaṇa varṇa-dharmācari \|*  
*karibeka jīvana-yātrā dharma patha dhari \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All persons in the world should work according to their Varnashram type, earning their livelihood ethically according to the principles of religion.**

**Комментарий:**

Sanatan Dharma, or “the eternal religion,” is the term given to the principle whereby every human being leads an ethical life according to his or her status in the four social divisions, or Varnashram. The ancient seers of in the holy land of India developed and implemented the Varnashram system as a scientific organization of human society. This Varnashram system is present in some form or other in every country in the world, though never in its pure form. Human nature cannot develop fully without these social divisions. Even those in mixed or uncivilized societies can, with good fortune, become free from sin and engage in purified acts and transform their lives into ones that are centered around Krishna, which is the goal of the system.

## Rules for outcastes

*saṅkara antyaja sabe tyaji nīca-dharma \|*  
*śūdrācāra kare sadā saṁsārera karma \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are lowborn or outcastes should give up their sinful habits and follow the duties of the Shudra class for their livelihood.**

*saṅkara antyaja thākibeka śūdrācāre \|*  
*cāturvarṇa vinā dharma nāhika saṁsāre \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For people from these classes should remain fixed in the Shudra duties, for in this world there can be no religious life outside the four varnas.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who earn their livelihood in accordance with the Varnashram system, will reach the first stages of love of Krishna by following the path of devotion.

*cātur-varṇa varṇa-dharme karibe saṁsāra \|*  
*śuddha kṛṣṇa-bhakti bale habe sadācāra \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Householders of all castes should support their family through their Varnashram duties, while purifying their habits through pure devotion to Krishna.**

*cātur-varṇa yadyapi śrī-kṛṣṇa nāhi bhaje \|*  
*varṇa-dharmācāre thāki’ rauravete maje \|\|46\|\|*  
*varṇa vinā gṛhasthera nāhi āra dharma \|*  
*varṇa dharmācāre gṛhasthera saba karma \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though the Varnashram system is not in itself direct worship of Krishna and by following it exclusively one may still end up in a hellish state, there is no religious life for a householder outside of Varnashram, so all of one’s activities should be governed by its principles.**

*varṇa-dharme e saṁsāra nirvāha karibe \|*  
*yāvad artha parigrahe śrī-kṛṣṇa bhajibe \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Householders should earn their living by following their Varnashram duties and worship Krishna within the means that this permits.**

*nisargataḥ vidhi-vākya ye paryanta nara \|*  
*varṇa-dharma sva-nirvāhe karibe ādara \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To the extent that the scriptural rules govern a man’s nature, he should respect his Varnashram duties for maintaining self and family.**

*bhakti-yoga-nāme ei tattva-nirūpaṇa \|*  
*bhakti-yoge bhāvodaya siddhānta-vacana \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All these points have been made in the cause of furthering bhakti yoga, for the truth is one only attains love of Krishna through devotion.**

*bhāvodaye vidhira pravṛtti nāhi raya \|*  
*bhāvodita kārye deha-yātrā siddha haya \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one comes to this stage of direct experience, there is no further need of such regulation, for any activity performed in that state of consciousness is sufficient for the maintenance of the body.**

**Комментарий:**

The Varnashram Dharma is necessary for as long as one needs to be bound by rules and regulations. One who remains situated in the Varnashram and engages in the worship of the Supreme Lord’s holy name continues to be purified until he reaches the first stages of prema, known as *bhāva*. One on this stage of spiritual advancement experiences the benefit of leading a regulated life without having to remain separately conscious of the scriptural guidelines. He is naturally on a very high level of moral and ethical life. This is not a state that can be attained by an ordinary person. When one’s pure feelings of devotion to the Lord awaken, then he is self-motivated to act and needs no external regulation.

*gṛhi-vaiṣṇavera ei advaya-sādhana \|*  
*śrī-viṣṇu advaya-tattve dvaita-nivartana \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no better practice than this for the householder Vaishnava, for through it, one erases all dualities in the non-dual substance, Vishnu.**

## The pure understanding of Vishnu is that He is not different from His name or attributes

*āra eka kathā āche dvaita-nivartane \|*  
*viṣṇu-nāma viṣṇu-rūpa viṣṇu-guṇa-gaṇe \|\|53\|\|*  
*viṣṇu haite pṛthag rūpe nā mānibe kabhu \|*  
*advaya akhaṇḍa viṣṇu cinmayatve vibhu \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One more thing that needs to be said about the dissolution of dualities: Vishnu’s name, Vishnu’s form and all of Vishnu’s virtues should never be considered distinct from His essence. He is the supreme being, the one, indivisible, non-dual spiritual substance.**

*ajñānete yadi haya dvaita upadrava \|*  
*nāmābhāsa haya tāra prema asambhava \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever out of ignorance becomes confused by apparent distinctions will only chant the semblance of the Holy Name and never attain prema.**

*sad-guru-kṛpāya sei anartha vināśa \|*  
*bhajite bhajite śuddha-nāmera prakāśa \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Through the blessings of a Sat Guru, such impurities will be eradicated, and as one continues to chant, one will experience the Pure Name.**

## The Mayavadi falls into useless argument and offence

*mata-vāda-jñāne dvaita haila pravartana \|*  
*aparādha haya āra nahe nivartana \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This dualism springs out of a attachment to a sectarian view; It is an offense that is difficult to eradicate.**

*māyāvādī bale brahma haya para-tattva \|*  
*nirviśeṣa nirvikāra nirākāra sattva \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Mayavadi says, “The supreme truth is Brahman, a state of being that is without form, transformation or attributes.**

*viṣṇu-rūpa viṣṇu-nāma māyāya kalpita \|*  
*māyā antardhāne viṣṇu hana brahma-gata \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vishnu’s form and name are imaginary products of the illusory energy, and when our illusory projections dissipate, they will merge into Brahman.”**

*e saba kutarka mātra satya śūnyavāda \|*  
*para-tattve sarva-śakti abhāva-pramāda \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These are all false arguments that are in fact a kind of nihilism. It is foolishness to say that no powers are present in the Supreme Truth.**

*śaktimān brahma yei sei viṣṇu haya \|*  
*nāmera vivāda-mātra vedera nirṇaya \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Brahman is omnipotent, and His name is Vishnu. Both names designate the same substance, according to the Veda.**

**Комментарий:**

Mayavadis have a narrow vision of the Supreme Truth. The material world is full of wonderful variety; the Mayavadis argue that the spiritual world is its opposite and must therefore be without any such variety. With their limited faith, the Mayavadis gradually become dry and meditate on an imaginary Brahman that is without features and thus of little interest. They cannot accept that the Supreme Brahman’s name, form, attributes and activities are all integral aspects of His eternal identity. Once one accepts this truth, he recognizes that the Supreme Truth designated by the term Brahman is none other than Vishnu Himself. The doctrine of illusion (“Mayavada”) is an unfortunate condition that the jiva falls into. Pure devotees reject this narrow doctrine and believe in the identity of the Lord’s name, form, attributes and activities with His essence.

## The difference between Vishnu and the Mayavada notion of Brahman

*viṣṇu para-tattva tāṅra nirviśeṣa dharma \|*  
*sa-viśeṣa dharma saha haya eka marma \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vishnu is the Supreme Truth. He possesses both an impersonal aspect as well as a personal one. Both aspects ultimately have the same significance.**

*viṣṇura acintya-śakti virodha-bhañjana \|*  
*anāyāse kari kare saundarya sthāpana \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vishnu’s inconceivable potency erases any contradiction between the two and easily establish the beautiful realization of their oneness.**

**Комментарий:**

One who accepts the fact of the Lord’s inconceivable potencies realize that He cannot possibly be without attributes. Thus all the opposition brought up by argumentative doubters is eliminated.

*jīva-buddhi sahajete ati alpatara \|*  
*acintya-śaktira bhāva nā kare gocara \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Human intelligence is by nature extremely limited and cannot easily fathom the Lord’s inconceivable potencies.**

*nija-buddhye cāhe eka sthāpite īśvara \|*  
*khaṇḍa-jñāne pāya brahma-tattvete avara \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who try to conceive of God with their limited intelligence, can only come up with a limited idea, that of the impersonal Brahman.**

*viṣṇura parama pada chāḍi devārcita \|*  
*brahme baddha haya nāhi bujhe hitāhita \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Diverted from Vishnu’s supreme abode, which is worshiped by all the gods, they are limited to the Brahman concept and ignorant of their own welfare.**

*cinmaya-svarūpa-jñāna ye bujhite jāne \|*  
*viṣṇu viṣṇu-nāma-guṇa eka kari māne \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who knows how to recognize the transcendental essence of the Supreme makes no distinction between Vishnu and His name and attributes.**

*ei ta viśuddha jñāna śrī-kṛṣṇa-svarūpa \|*  
*sambandha buddhite labhi bhaje nāma-rūpa \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the pure understanding of Krishna’s transcendental identity; one who knows these truths worships the Lord’s name and form.**

## How to properly understand the identity of Shiva and Vishnu

*jaḍa-nāma jaḍa-rūpa-guṇe yei bheda \|*  
*se bheda cit-tattve nāi ei ta prabheda \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A duality exists between a mundane epithet and the object it identifies, but such a duality does not exist on the spiritual platform.**

*viṣṇu-tattve bheda-jñāna anartha vikāra \|*  
*śivete viṣṇute bheda ati avicāra \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking the various forms of Vishnu to be different from one another results from anarthas. It is also a blunder to think Shiva distinct from Vishnu.**

**Комментарий:**

It is an error to think that the various forms of Vishnu are different from each other. This kind of erroneous distinction arises when one thinks Shiva to be independent of Vishnu.

## The difference between a devotee’s behavior and that of a Mayavadi

*nāmaika-śaraṇa yei bhakti mahājana \|*  
*ekeśvara kṛṣṇe bhaji chāḍe anya jana \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee who has taken single-minded shelter of the Name is an elevated soul. He worships Krishna as the one Supreme Lord and rejects all the other gods.**

*anya-deva anya-śāstra nindā nāhi kare \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-dāsa bali anye pūje samādare \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even so, he does not condemn other gods or other scriptures, but venerates and respects the gods as servitors of Lord Krishna.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotee does not condemn other scriptures or other gods, because he tries to steer clear of fruitless arguments. Sometimes other scriptures establish one or the other gods as the supreme deity in order to respond to the particular tastes or qualifications of individual aspirants. Even so, these scriptures are ultimately trying to elevate their followers to become devotees of Krishna. For this reason, one should never criticize other scriptures and the gods described therein, for such criticism is an offense. The mercy of the Holy Name and the goddess of pure devotion come to one who is free from the tendency to criticize.

*pratidina gṛhi-bhakta nirmālya arpaṇe \|*  
*deva pitṛ sarva jīve karena tarpaṇe \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Every day, the householder devotee takes Krishna’s prasad and offers it to the gods, the forefathers and all creatures, satisfying them.**

*yathā yathā anya deve karena darśana \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-dāsa bali’ tāṅre karena vandana \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever and wherever such a devotee sees other deities being worshiped, he offers them praise in the knowledge that they are Krishna’s servants.**

*māyāvādi-gaṇa yadi viṣṇu-pūjā kare \|*  
*prasāda nirmālya bhakta nāhi laya ḍare \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, if a Mayavadi worships Lord Vishnu, out of fear, the devotee does not take prasad from that worship.**

*māyāvādī hari-nāme aparādhī haya \|*  
*tāhāra pradatta pūjā hari nāhi laya \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mayavadis are offenders to the Holy Name, therefore Krishna does not accept the worship they offer Him.**

*anya-deva-nirmālya grahaṇe aparādha \|*  
*śuddha bhakti sādhane sarvadā sādhe bādha \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is an offence to accept the remnants of offerings to other gods, and if accepted they simply create obstructions to one’s progress in bhakti.**

*tabe yadi śuddha bhakta śrī-kṛṣṇa pūjiyā \|*  
*anya deve pūjā kare tat-prasāda diyā \|\|78\|\|*  
*se prasāda grahaṇete nāhi aparādha \|*  
*sei rūpa devārcane nāhi bhakti-bādha \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, if a pure devotee worships Lord Krishna and then worships other gods with His remnants, then there is no objection to accepting these remnants as prasad, and such worship of the gods creates no obstacles on the path of devotion.**

*śuddha-bhakta nāma aparādhī nāhi haya \|*  
*nāma kari’ prema pāya nāme deya jaya \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pure devotees never commit offenses to the Holy Name; their chanting results in prema and through the Name they become glorious.**

## The remedy for this offense

*pramāde yadyapi haya anye viṣṇu-jñāna \|*  
*tabe anutāpe kari viṣṇu-tattva-dhyāna \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who out of illusion or madness takes another to be Vishnu should repent by meditating on Vishnu’s true nature.**

*śrī-viṣṇu smariyā kari aparādha kṣaya \|*  
*yatne dekhi āra nā se aparādha haya \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By remembering Vishnu, all offences are dissolved, but one should take care that the same error is not repeated.**

**Комментарий:**

Remembrance of the Lord is the most effective penance. The Vedas always recommend imperiled Brahmins to meditate upon the lotus feet of Lord Vishnu for protection. Remembering the Lord’s name is the same as meditating upon His lotus feet.

*pūrva doṣa kṣamā śīla bhaktera bāndhava \|*  
*dayāra sāgara kṛṣṇa kṣamāra arṇava \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna is an ocean of mercy and the friend of the devotees; He always forgives the devotee’s previous errors.**

*bahu deva sevi saṅga kariba varjana \|*  
*ekeśvara vaiṣṇavera kariba pūjana \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I vow to avoid the company of those who worship many gods and revere the devotees who worship Lord Krishna exclusively.**

*hari-dāsa pade bhaktivinoda ye jana \|*  
*harināma-cintāmaṇi tāhāra jīvana \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Harinama Chintamani is the life and soul of Bhaktivinoda,  who takes pleasure in devotion to Srila Hari Das Thakur’s lotus feet.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the fifth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “The offense of considering other gods to be independent of Krishna.”

## 6 - Showing disrespect to the spiritual master guror avajñā

*pañca-tattva jaya jaya śrī-rādhā-mādhava \|*  
*jaya navadvīpa vraja yamunā vaiṣṇava \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the Pancha Tattva!**  
**All glories to Sri Sri Radha Madhava!**  
**All glories to Nabadwip and Vrindavan,**  
**to the Yamuna River and all the Vaishnavas.**

*hari-dāsa bale prabhu kari nivedana \|*  
*tṛtīyāparādha nāme ye rūpe ghaṭana \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das said, Lord, I now humbly submit to You how the third offence to the Holy Name comes about.**

*vistāri baliba āmi tomāri ājñāya \|*  
*yei saba aparādha guru avajñāya \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As You have asked me to do so, I will explain at length all the offenses that arise out of disrespecting the guru.**

*bahu yoni bhrami’ mānava-śarīra*  
*durlabha śubhada ati \|*  
*tathāpi anitya pāileka*  
*yeiyāvat jīvane sthiti \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Through countless wombs the soul wanders before attaining a human body, so auspicious and rare. Once attaining it, one must bear its impermanence constantly in mind and make the most of it through its duration.**

**Комментарий:**

After wandering through 8,400,000 different species, the soul finally attains a human body as a result of unintentional pious activities (*ajñāta-sukṛti*). A birth in the human form is most valuable, because only in that body is it possible to make real spiritual advancement, which is impossible in any other type of body. The denizens of heaven are limited to enjoying the results of their past good works, but they cannot voluntarily initiate any spiritual activities. Animals, on the other hand, are lacking the consciousness required for free will or independent action. Thus it is only while in the human form of life that one can undertake service to the Supreme Lord.

*parama maṅgala labhibāre tare*  
*yadi nā yatana kare \|*  
*punarāya bhave anitya śarīra*  
*labhiyā ābāra mare \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those not making the effort to attain the supreme benediction. will once again have to take another impermanent body, to be born and die in this world, again and again.**

*subodha ye haya durlabha nṛ-deha*  
*labhiyā bhava saṁsāre \|*

**Перевод:**

**An intelligent person who recognizes how rare the opportunity that comes from obtaining a human form of life…**

## A spiritual master is essential for everyone

*guru karṇa-dhāra samāśraya kari*  
*kṛṣṇa ānukūlye tare \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**… will take full shelter of a spiritual master as a helmsman, and crosses the material ocean, if Krishna is favorable.**

**Комментарий:**

Such a spiritual master is the only helmsman who can save the jiva drowning in this ocean of material nescience. Those individuals who attempt to cross this ocean by the limited intelligence available to them are extremely foolish. In this world, we all require the help of a teacher’s instructions in in order to achieve success, in whatever field we happen to take interested. How, then, can one expect to obtain success in the supreme subject matter without the aid of an accomplished guide? Only a person who is accomplished, i.e., has himself attained expertise and experience in the subject matter, is qualified to be guru.

*śānta kṛṣṇa-bhakta lakṣaṇa ye guru*  
*sa-dainya vacane tāṅre \|*  
*santoṣa kariyā kṛṣṇa-dīksā laya*  
*yāya saṁsārera pāre \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should approach the qualified guru, a peaceful devotee of Krishna, with humble words, thus satisfying him. Then one should take initiation in the Krishna mantra from him, and thus cross over the ocean of birth and death.**

*sahaje jīvera āche kṛṣṇe mati*  
*vṛthā tarke tāhā yāya \|*  
*vitarka chāḍiyā sumati āśraye*  
*guru hate mantra pāya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, a jiva naturally and easily attains Krishna consciousness but it dissipates when one engages in false argument. One who abandons useless disputation and adopts a sincere attitude will receive the mantra from the spiritual master.**

*gṛhī jīva-gaṇa varṇāśrame thāki’*  
*sad-guru āśraya kare \|*

**Перевод:**

**All householders who follow the Varnashram dharma should take shelter of a qualified guru.**

## If someone from the higher castes is qualified, he is worthy of being guru

*brāhmaṇa ācārya sarva-varṇe haya*  
*yadi kṛṣṇa bhakti dhare \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmins are the natural preceptors of all the other castes, if they are endowed with devotion to Krishna.**

*brāhmaṇa kulete supātra abhāve*  
*anya kule dīkṣā pāya \|*  
*ucca varṇa guru gṛhīra ucita*  
*guru śiṣya parīkṣāya \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But if one finds no worthy preceptor amongst the Brahmins, one should take initiation from someone in another caste. The householder should properly take a guru of a higher caste, after both guru and disciple examine each other.**

## More important than caste is qualification

*kṛṣṇa tattva vettā prakṛta ye haya*  
*se ha-ite pāre guru \|*  
*kibā vipra śūdra ki gṛhī sannyāsī*  
*guru hana kalpa-taru \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who genuinely knows the teachings of Krishna is qualified to become a spiritual master. Whether Brahmin or Shudra, householder or sannyasi, such a guru can bestow all blessings on his disciple.**

*varṇera maryādā pātrera vicāre*  
*paramārthe laghu ati \|*  
*supātra milana prayojana sadā*  
*yadi cāi śuddhā rati \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When ascertaining the guru’s spiritual qualifications, respect for caste is in fact a very minor consideration. The greatest importance must be placed on finding someone truly worthy if one wishes to attain the mood of pure devotion.**

*supātrera prāpti mūla prayojana*  
*pavitra suvarṇa hena \|*  
*tāhe ucca varṇa labhile saṁyoga*  
*sohāgā suvarṇe yena \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Finding a qualified teacher is the basic necessity, like finding pure gold. If besides being qualified, the guru is of a higher caste, then that is like gold toned with borax.**

**Комментарий:**

One must seek out a qualified spiritual master. For social reasons, it is usually expedient to take a guru from a higher caste. As a result it is recommended that a householder who can find a spiritual master in a higher caste not deliberately look for one of a lower caste. Nevertheless, it should be remembered that one must never accept a higher caste individual or hereditary guru as spiritual master if he is lacking the suitable qualifications.

## A renunciate may take shelter of a guru in the renounced orders

*ye kona kāraṇe sei gṛhi dharma*  
*chāḍi anyāśrama laya \|*  
*tāhe paramārtha nā pāiyā śeṣe*  
*sādhu guru anveṣaya \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If for any reason one has abandoned his household duties to embrace the life of a renunciate, yet has been unable to realize the supreme goal of life, he too should seek out a saintly spiritual master.**

*tāhāra pakṣete agṛhī ācārya*  
*praśasta sakala mate \|*  
*tāṅra dīkṣā śikṣā pāiyā se jana*  
*bhāse nāma-rasāmṛte \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For such a person, a teacher in the renounced orders is the most suitable choice, according to all schools of thought. He should take both initiation and instruction from such a teacher, and immerse himself in the nectar of the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

Even if one has renounced householder life, it is still obligatory to seek out a qualified guru, but such a guru should be selected from amongst renunciate Vaishnavas.

## A householder who renounces does not have to give up his householder guru

*gṛhī bhakta-jane virāga labhile*  
*chāḍaye saṁsāra vidhi \|*  
*tabu pūrva-guru caraṇa āśraya*  
*karibe jīvanāvadhi \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee who becomes indifferent to householder life and renounces the rules and regulations of that life, still remains the disciple of the guru by whom he was initiated; this person is to remain his guru throughout his life.**

*gṛhi jana madhye gṛhi guru śasta*  
*yadi śuddha bhakta hana \|*  
*natuvā agṛhī suyogya ha-ile*  
*guru-yogya sarva-kṣaṇa \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**However, for householders, a householder guru is advisable as long as the guru is a pure devotee. If one cannot find such a spiritual master, a worthy sannyasi may be sought out to act as his guru.**

**Комментарий:**

If a householder finds a qualified householder guru, then he may take initiation from him, otherwise he should choose a renunciate spiritual master.

*sad-guru pāiyā bhajie bhajite*  
*bhāvera udaya yabe \|*  
*saṁsāra virakti saṁsāra chāḍiyā*  
*vairāgī ha-ibe tabe \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever takes a Sat Guru and engages in bhajan, quickly attains to the first stages of divine love. When he becomes fully indifferent to worldly life, he can renounce householder life to become a monk.**

## On taking renounced orders, one takes a sannyas guru

*vairāgya āśrama grahaṇete tyāgī*  
*puruṣa ha-ibe guru \|*  
*tāṅhāra caraṇe śikhibe virāga*  
*guru śikṣā kalpa taru \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one takes the renounced order of life, he must take a male guru from that station. From such a siksha guru, who brings all blessings, he will learn the rules and techniques of renunciate life.**

## The diksha and siksha gurus must be respected equally

*dīkṣā śikṣā bhede guru du’ prakāra*  
*ubhaye samāna māna \|*  
*arpibe sujana paramārtha dhana*  
*anāyāse yadi cān \|\|20\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are two kinds of spiritual masters: initiator and instructor; Both are equally worthy of respect. If a pious individual wants to attain the supreme goal with a minimum of difficulty, he will give such respect.**

**Комментарий:**

There are two kinds of spiritual master. The diksha guru is the initiating spiritual master who gives his disciple initiation into the mantra (diksha). The teacher who explains one’s constitutional position and relation to Krishna is the siksha guru. One should only have one mantra guru, but may have many instructing spiritual masters. Since both are spiritual instructors, they must be given the same degree of respect.

*kṛṣṇa-nāma mantra dena dīkṣā guru*  
*śikṣā guru tattva dātā \|*  
*vaiṣṇava sakala śikṣā guru hana*  
*sarva śubha janayitā \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The diksha guru imparts Krishna’s name and mantra to his disciple, while the siksha guru imparts spiritual knowledge. Every single Vaishnava is a siksha guru to other people and able to bestow unlimited blessings on the world.**

## One should follow the teachings of the founder of one’s sampradaya

*sādhu sampradāye ācārya sakala*  
*śikṣā guru pratiṣṭhita \|*  
*ādyācārya yini guru śiromaṇi*  
*pūji tāṅre yathocita \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the acharyas of the saintly disciplic successions are the established instructing gurus for that disciplic line. The founders of these lines are the topmost gurus and we should offer them the appropriate worship.**

**Комментарий:**

The “saintly disciplic successions” are the four Vaishnava schools. Each of these disciplic successions or traditions (*paramparā, sampradāya*) teach mantras, doctrines, devotional practices and the goal of life. The disciplic succession that teaches Mayavada is considered unacceptable to the Vaishnavas, and one should affiliate oneself with one of the authorized sampradayas by taking initiation in order to protect himself from them. One should give all respect to the teachings established by the masters who founded the respective Vaishnava schools. Sri Ramanuja, Sri Madhavacarya, Sri Nimbarkacarya, and Sri Vishnu Swami are the four original acharyas of the Vaishnava schools. Madhvacharya is the founder of our school.

*tāṅra susiddhānta anugata haye*  
*nā māniba anya śikṣā \|*  
*tāṅhāra ādeśa pāliba yatane*  
*nā la-iba anya dīkṣā \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**We should follow their well-thought out conclusions, and not accept any other teaching. We vow to obey the instructions that the founder-guru has left us and not accept any other initiation.**

## One should choose a guru from an authorized school

*sampradāya guru-gaṇe śikṣā guru jāni \|*  
*anya-mata paṇḍitera śikṣā nāhi māni \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Knowing the founder of their sampradaya to be their siksha guru I do not accept even a learned scholar’s teaching if it differs from theirs.**

*sei mate suśikṣita sādhu sucarita \|*  
*dīkṣā guru yogya sadā jāne supaṇḍita \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A learned person knows that a virtuous saintly person, highly educated in the teaching of the founder’s tradition, is qualified to give initiation.**

## There is no benefit by taking the Krishna mantra from a Mayavadi

*māyāvādi-mate thāke kṛṣṇa mantra laya \|*  
*tāra paramārtha lābha kabhu nāhi haya \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A follower of Mayavada who yet takes initiation in the Krishna mantra will never attain the supreme destination.**

## Never take a guru who is not a pure devotee

*ye anyāya śikhe yei śikṣā deya āra \|*  
*ubhaye narake yāya nā pāya uddhāra \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Both one who learns in a dishonest manner and the one who so teaches suffer both in this life and afterward without hope for salvation.**

*śuddha bhakti chāḍi yini śikhilena bāda \|*  
*tāṅhāra jīvana mātra vāda visaṁvāda \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who teaches a philosophy devoid of devotion leads a life full of argument and counter-argument.**

*se kemane guru habe uddhāribe jīve \|āpani asiddha anye kibā śubha dibe \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**How can such a person become a guru? Who can he save? Unsuccessful himself, how can he do any good for anyone else?**

*ata eva śuddha bhakta ye se kene naya \|*  
*upayukta guru haya sarva-śāste kaya \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, any pure devotee, whatever else he may be, is capable of acting as guru, so say all the scriptures.**

## The Guru Principle

*dīkṣā guru śikṣā guru duṅhe kṛṣṇa dāsa \|*  
*duṅhe vraja-jana kṛṣṇa śaktira prakāśa \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Both the siksha guru and the diksha guru are Krishna’s servants Both are manifestations of Krishna’s power and reside eternally in Vraja.**

*guruke sāmānya jīva nā jānibe kabhu \|*  
*guru kṛṣṇa śakti kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha nitya prabhu \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Never think that the spiritual master is an ordinary mortal; the guru is Krishna’s potency, Krishna’s beloved, our eternal master.**

**Комментарий:**

One should never make the mistake of identifying the guru as an ordinary mortal. One should devote oneself to the guru in the knowledge that he is Krishna’s eternal associate, empowered by His superior spiritual potency. The Mayavadis hold that the spiritual master is identical with Krishna in every way, but this idea is not acceptable to the pure Vaishnavas. Devotees and pious people in general should be on their guard against this misconception. If Mayavadi ideas insinuate themselves into a Vaishnava’s consciousness, his or her entire practice of devotion will be ruined.

*ei buddhi saha sadā guru bhakti kare \|*  
*sei guru-bhakti bale saṁsārete tare \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who devotes himself to the guru in this consciousness frees himself from the material predicament on the strength of this devotion.**

## How to revere the spiritual master

*agre guru pūjā pare śrī kṛṣṇa pūjana \|*  
*gurudeve śrī-kṛṣṇa prasāda samarpaṇa \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Before worshiping Krishna, one must first worship the guru. One offers the spiritual master Krishna’s prasad.**

**Комментарий:**

One worships the spiritual master by making offerings of a seat, water for washing the feet, water to drink, a bath, clothes and ornaments. Then, with the spiritual master’s permission, one may begin worshiping the Holy Couple. When one has finished worshiping the Deities, prasadam is given to the spiritual master first, then to the other Vaishnavas, gods and forefathers.

*guru ājñā laye kṛṣṇa pūjibe yatane \|*  
*śrī guru smariyā kṛṣṇa balibe vadane \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should worship Krishna with the guru’s permission, and chant the Lord’s names while remembering the guru.**

## How one must have faith in the guru

*gurute avajñā yāra tāra aparādha \|*  
*sei aparādhe tāra haya bhakti bādha \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who shows disrespect to the guru commits an offence, an offence that impedes his advancement in spiritual life.**

*guru kṛṣṇa vaiṣṇavete sama bhakti kari \|*  
*nāmāśraye śuddha bhakta śīghra yāya tari \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who is equally devoted to the guru, Krishna and the Vaishnavas, and who takes shelter of the Holy Name is a pure devotee and is quickly delivered.**

*gurute acalā śraddhā kare yei jana \|*  
*śuddha nāma bale sei pāya prema dhana \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One whose faith in his guru is unshakable will reach the goal of Krishna prema on the strength of chanting the Pure Name.**

## When to reject a guru

*tabe yadi e rūpa ghaṭanā kabhu haya \|*  
*asat saṅge gurura yogyatā haya kṣaya \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nevertheless, it may happen that for some reason the spiritual master falls into bad company and becomes bereft of qualifications.**

*prathame chilena tini sad guru pradhāna \|*  
*pare nāma aparādhe hañā hata jñāna \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the time the disciple accepted the guru, he was highly exalted, but by committing offenses he loses his spiritual understanding.**

*vaiṣṇave vidveṣa kari chāḍe nāma rasa \|*  
*krame krame hana artha kāminīra vaśa \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He becomes inimical to Vaishnavas and loses all taste for chanting, and gradually succumbs to the desire for wealth and women.**

*sei guru chāḍi śiṣya śrī-kṛṣṇa-kṛpāya \|*  
*sad guru labhiyā punaḥ śuddha nāma gāya \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The disciple should leave such a guru and, if Krishna is merciful, he will find out a Sat Guru and again take up the Pure Name.**

## The guru and disciple should test each other before establishing a relationship

*ayogya śiṣyere guru kariben daṇḍa \|*  
*bhajiyā ayogya guru śiṣya haya paṇḍa \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The spiritual master must be strict with unworthy disciples, while a disciple who takes shelter of an unqualified guru becomes worthless.**

*duṅhera yogyatā yata dina sthira raya \|*  
*paraspara sambandha kakhana tyajya naya \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As long as both of them remain stable in their qualifications, there is no question of either one of them breaking off their relationship.**

**Комментарий:**

The guru-disciple relationship is a permanent one. If both maintain their purity and sincerity, their eternal relationship is never broken. However, if the spiritual master becomes contaminated, the disciple should immediately repudiate him. Similarly, the spiritual master should repudiate the disciple who is insincere and fallen. If either of them fail to do so, they will fall down. The evidence for such repudiation of a guru by disciple or disciple by spiritual master is given elsewhere.

## One should only take a guru after testing him

*sad gurura prati yei avajñā ācare \|*  
*se pāpiṣṭha aparādhī sarvatra saṁsāre \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who shows disrespect for an authentic spiritual master is the most sinful offender anywhere in the world.**

*ata eva prathame viśeṣa yatna kari \|*  
*śuddha bhakte la-ibena guru-rūpe vari \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should therefore take particular care from the very beginning that he has taken a pure devotee for his spiritual master.**

**Комментарий:**

The scriptural injunction is that both the spiritual master and his disciple should test each other prior to cementing their relationship by initiation. This is not necessary in the case of a spiritual master who is a *kula-guru*, or one coming in a family that has traditionally given initiation to the client family, especially not if the *kula-guru* is qualified. Of course, if he is not qualified, then one should seek out a genuine saintly Vaishnava from whom to take initiation. When we take care to examine ordinary items in our daily lives before acquiring them, then how much more care we should take before making a decision on a matter of as great importance as taking a spiritual master! One who does not do so is a true unfortunate. If one has taken an unqualified *kula-guru*, then he or she should give him the money he requests and respectfully ask to be released from any obligations, and then go off in search of a bona fide spiritual master.

*guru tyāga kleśa yena kabhu nāhi ghaṭe \|*  
*e rūpa cintile kabhu nā paḍe saṅkaṭe \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one thinks beforehand, “May I never have to suffer the distress of rejecting my guru,” this difficultywill never arise.**

*guru yathā bhakti-hīna śiṣya tāra prāya \|*  
*ata eva śuddha guru labe parīkṣāya \|\|48\|\|*  
*sad guru avajñā aparādha bhayaṅkara \|*  
*ei aparādhe naṣṭa haya deva nara \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If a guru has no devotion, his disciple will be just like him, so one should take care to examine the guru to see if he is pure. The offense against a pure devotee guru is a terrible thing, so disastrous that it can ruin both man and god alike.**

## How to honor the spiritual master

*guru śayyāsana āra pādukādi yāna \|*  
*pāda pīṭha snānodaka chāyāra laṅghana \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should never step on or over the guru’s seat or bed, his shoes, vehicle, footstool, bathwater, or his shadow.**

*gurura agrete anya pūjā dvaita jñāna \|*  
*dīkṣā vyākhyā prabhutvādi karibe vandana \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should not worship another person, think another to be his equal, initiate, explain, or give orders in the guru’s presence.**

*yathā yathā gurura pāibe daraśana \|*  
*daṇḍavat paḍi bhūme karibe vandana \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever and wherever the disciple sees the spiritual master, he should fall on the ground and offer his reverence.**

*guru nāma bhaktite karibe uccāraṇa \|*  
*guru ājñā helā nā karibe kadācana \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The guru’s name must be uttered with great devotion and one should never show indifference to his commands.**

*gurura prasāda sevā avaśya karibe \|*  
*gurura apriya vākya kabhu nā kahibe \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should eagerly honor the remnants of his meals and never say anything displeasing to him.**

*gurura caraṇe dainye la-ibe śaraṇa \|*  
*karibe gurura sadā priya ācaraṇa \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should humbly take shelter of his feet, and always act in ways that please him.**

*e rūpa ācāre kṛṣṇa nāma saṅkīrtane \|*  
*sarva siddhi haya prabho bale śruti gaṇe \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who acts in this way will attain all perfection through chanting the Holy Name, so say the scriptures.**

*nāma guru prati yadi avajñā ghaṭaye \|*  
*duṣṭa saṅge duṣṭa śāstra mata samāśraye \|\|57\|\|*  
*tabe sei saṅga sei śāstra dūra kari \|*  
*vilāpa kariba sei guru pade dhari \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one should disrespect the Nama-guru, due to the influence of bad association or unauthorized readings, he should renounce that association and teaching and fall down at the spiritual master’s feet and beg forgiveness.**

**Комментарий:**

The term *nāma-guru* used here refers to the person who teaches us about the Holy Name, tells us of its supremacy, and then initiates one in the Holy Name or the mantra, which also is made up of names of the Lord. The initiating spiritual master or diksha guru is also the Nama-guru. The mantra loses its meaning and purpose if it is separated from the Holy Name, and conversely, simply by uttering the holy name of the Lord, the mantra is automatically chanted.

*kṛpā kari gurudeva ha-ibe sadaya \|*  
*nāme prema dibe se vaiṣṇava dayāmaya \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The spiritual master will be merciful and forgive him, and compassionately bless him with love for Krishna’s name.**

*hari-dāsa pada reṇu bharasā yāhāra \|*  
*nāma cintāmaṇi gāya tṛṇādhika chāra \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I, who am lower than the blade of grass, whose only hope is in the dust of Hari Das Thakur’s lotus feet, sing the** ***Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the sixth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “The offense of showing disrespect to the spiritual master.”

## 7 - Blaspheming the scriptures

*śruti śāstra nindanam*  
*jaya jaya gadāi gaurāṅga nityānanda*  
*jaya sītāpati jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu!**  
**All glories to the husband of Sita, Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to all the devotees of Lord Gauranga!**

*haridāsa bale prabhu caturthāparādha \|*  
*śruti-śāstra-vinindana bhakti-rasa-bādha \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das said, “Lord, I will now tell you of the fourth offense, blasphemy of the scriptures, which impedes one from relishing devotion.”**

## The Vedas are the only authoritative source of knowledge

*śruti-śāstra veda upaniṣat purāṇa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-niśvasita haya sarvatra pramāṇa \|\|3\|\|*  
*viśeṣataḥ aprākṛta-tattve jñāna yata \|*  
*sakali āmnāya siddha tāhe ha-i rata \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The revealed literature includes the Veda, Upanishads and Puranas; they are authoritative in all matters, for they were exhaled by the Lord. but especially in questions of transcendental understanding. All are confirmed by sacred tradition, and so I adhere to them.**

*jaḍātīta vastu indriyera agocara \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-kṛpā vinā tāhā nā haya gocara \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That which is beyond matter cannot be perceived by the senses and cannot be experienced except through Krishna’s grace.**

**Комментарий:**

The senses are only capable of experiencing material objects. They are incapable of reaching things that are beyond matter. The Supreme Lord is completely spiritual and therefore transcendental to matter. Nevertheless, He has mercifully given the descending knowledge for the ultimate benefit of humanity. The word *āmnāya* used here specifically refers to scriptural knowledge received through the proper authorized disciplic succession or *paramparā*.

*karaṇāpaṭava bhrama vipralipsā āra \|*  
*pramāda sarvatra nara jñāne doṣa ei cāra \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Imperfect senses, proneness to error, deception, and illusion— these four flaws affect all kinds of human knowledge.**

*sei saba doṣa śūnya veda catuṣṭaya \|*  
*veda vinā paramārthe gati nāhi haya \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The four Vedas are free from these flaws, and thus no one can attain the supreme good without the Veda.**

*māyā baddha jīve kṛṣṇa bahu kṛpā kari \|*  
*veda purāṇādi dila ārṣa jñāna dhari \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna, out of mercy to the conditioned souls bound by Maya, has given the Vedas and Puranas by revealing them to the seers.**

**Комментарий:**

The word *ārṣa-jñāna* means revealed knowledge, that is, the knowledge saintly seers (rishis) receive in the depths of their most profound meditation*.*

## Ten basic teachings are found in the revealed scriptures

*sei śruti śāstre jāni karma jñāna chāra \|*  
*nirmala bhaktite mātra pāi sarva sāra \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From the Shruti, we learn that the paths of karma and jnana are worthless, and that one can attain the supreme essence only through spotless devotion.**

*māyā mūḍha jīve karma jñāne śuddha kari \|*  
*śuddha bhakti adhikāra śikhāilā hari \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord purifies the bewildered jivas through ritual and philosophy and thus elevates them to eligibility for pure devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

The ultimate verdict of the Shrutis is that the rituals and other prescribed duties (*karma*) and the philosophical and metaphysical discussions (*jñāna*) found therein give insignificant results. Once that has been made clear, they teach that one can attain the essential truth of all things through pure devotion.

*pramāṇa se veda vākya naya-ṭī prameya \|*  
*śikhāya sambandha prayojana abhidheya \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Shruti is the authoritative source of knowledge, teaching nine basic truths encompassing theology, devotional practice, and the ultimate goal of life.**

*ei daśa mūla sāra avidyā vināśa \|*  
*kariyā jīvera kare suvidyā prakāśa \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taken together, these are called the ten basic teachings that destroy ignorance, and when imbibed by the jiva make him truly wise.**

## (1) Hari is the Supreme Truth. (2) He possesses all powers. (3) He is the embodiment of joy.

*prathame śikhāya para tattva eka hari \|*  
*śyāma sarva-śaktimān rasa-mūrti-dhārī \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The first portion of this teaching is that the one Supreme Truth is Hari; Shyamasundar, the supreme powerful Lord who possesses a delightful form.**

*jīvera paramānanda karena vidhāna \|*  
*saṁvyoma dhāmete tāra nitya adhiṣṭhāna \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He brings the highest joy to the jivas, and has His eternal abode in the spiritual sky.**

*e tina prameya haya śrī-kṛṣṇa-visaye \|*  
*veda-śāstra śikṣā dena jīvera hṛdaye \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These three objects of knowledge are related to Krishna; they are imparted by the Vedic scriptures into the heart of the jiva.**

## (4) The jiva

*dvitīye śikhāya vibhinnāṁśa jīva tattva \|*  
*ananta saṅkhyaka cit paramāṇu sattva \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Secondly, the scriptures teach about Krishna’s separated parts, the jivas: they are atomic sparks of consciousness that exist in infinite number.**

## The distinction between (5) eternally bound and (6) eternally liberated souls.

*nitya baddha nitya mukta bhede jīva dvi-prakāra \|*  
*saṁvyoma brahmāṇḍa bhari saṁsthiti tāhāra \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are two kinds of jivas: the eternally bound and the eternally liberated; the former fill the spiritual world, the latter the material creation.**

## The bound soul

*baddha jīva māyā bhaji’ kṛṣṇa bahirmukha \|*  
*ananta brahmāṇḍe bhoga kare duḥkha sukha \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The bound souls have turned their backs on Krishna and serve Maya. As such, they enjoy happiness and distress within the unlimited material worlds.**

## The liberated soul

*nitya mukta kṛṣṇa bhaji’ kṛṣṇa pāriṣada \|*  
*para vyome bhoga kare premera sampada \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Liberated souls are Krishna’s eternal companions, who serve and worship Him; they enjoy the pleasures of loving Krishna in the supreme heaven.**

*tina-ṭī prameya ei jīvera viṣaye \|*  
*śruti śāstra śikṣā dena kṛṣṇa dāsī haye \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These three teachings related to the individual soul are taught by the revealed scriptures, which render service to Krishna.**

## (7) The relation between them is one of inconceivable oneness in difference

*cid-vyāpāra āra yata jaḍera vyāpāra \|*  
*sakali acintya bhedābheda prakāra \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever exists in the spiritual and material worlds is inconceivably one and different from the Supreme Lord.**

*jīva jaḍa sarva vastu kṛṣṇa śakti maya \|*  
*avicintya bhedābheda śruti śāstre kaya \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The individual souls and matter are manifestations of Krishna’s potencies, so the Shrutis tell us they are simultaneously one and different from Him.**

*ei jñāne jīva jāne āmi kṛṣṇa dāsa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa mora nitya prabhu cit sūrya prakāśa \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the jiva realizes this, he thinks, “I am Krishna’s servant. He is my eternal master and the divine spiritual sun of which I am but a ray.”**

*śakti pariṇāma mātra veda śāstra bale \|*  
*vivartādi duṣṭa-mate veda ninde chale \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedic scriptures tell us the jiva is a transformation of Krishna’s energy; to say he is Krishna Himself transformed is a way of blaspheming the Veda.**

**Комментарий:**

The Veda teaches the doctrine known as *parināma*, i.e., the inconceivable transformation of Brahman’s potencies. The theory that Brahman’s essence transforms into different names and forms is called *vivarta-vāda.* It goes completely against the Vedic teachings.

## These seven prameyas are called sambandha jnana

*ei ta sambandha jñāna sāta-ṭī prameya \|*  
*śruti-śāstra śikṣā dena ati upādeya \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The seven subjects outlined above are the basic theology taught by the revealed scriptures, truths most relishable.**

*veda punaḥ śikṣā dena abhidheya sāra \|*  
*nava vidha kṛṣṇa bhakti vidhi rāga āra \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas then go on to teach the active response to this knowledge, the nine kinds of devotional activity, performed either out of duty or love.**

## (8) The abhidheya is the nine kinds of bhakti

*śravaṇa kīrtana smṛti pūjana vandana \|*  
*paricaryā dāsya sakhya ātma-nivedana \|\|27\|\|*  
*bhaktira prakāra madhye nāma sarva-sāra \|*  
*praṇava-māhātmya veda karena pracāra \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing, chanting, remembering, deity worship and prayer, rendering service, identification as the Lord’s servant or as His friend, and complete surrender: The essence of these nine divisions of bhakti is chanting the Holy Name, as indicated by the glorification of the syllable OM throughout the Vedas.**

## (9) The ultimate goal of life is love for Krishna

*śuddha bhakti samāśraya kariyā mānava \|*  
*kṛṣṇa kṛpā bale pāya premera vaibhava \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The human being who takes complete shelter of pure devotion attains the glory of love for Krishna through His mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

Pure devotion is defined as the proclivity to constantly cultivate a favorable consciousness of Krishna, where there is no desire other than furthering one’s devotion to Him, and which consciousness is not obscured by ritualism or philosophical speculation. If mixed with other kinds of ritualism or philosophical theories, it cannot be called pure devotion. All the Vedic literature teaches taking full shelter of the Holy Name in this spirit of pure devotion.

## To criticize the above teachings is an offense to the Holy Name

*e nava prameya śruti karena pramāṇa \|*  
*śruti tattvābhijña guru karena sandhāna \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The disciple learns these nine basic principles ascertained by the Shruti from a spiritual master well-versed in their teachings.**

*e hena śrutira yei kare vinindana \|*  
*nāma aparādhī sei narādhama jana \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The foolish person who criticizes these teachings is an offender against the Holy Name and the lowest among men.**

## Philosophies in opposition to the Shruti

*jaimini kapila nagna nāstika sugata \|*  
*gautama – e chaya jana hetu-vāde rata \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaimini, Kapila, the Jain Mahavira, Charvaka, the Buddha and Gautama all propagated theories ruined by sophistry.**

*veda māne mukhe tabu īśa nāhi māne \|*  
*karma kāṇḍa śreṣṭha bali jaimin bākhāne \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaiminiostensibly accepted the Veda, but did not believe in God. He taught that the ritualistic portions of the Veda were their topmost part.**

*īśvara asiddha kapilera kalpanāya \|*  
*tabu yoga māne artha bujhā nāhi yāya \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kapilawas an agnostic who imagined that God’s existence cannot be proved, and yet he accepted yogic practice, which is completely incomprehensible.**

*nagna se tāmasa tattva karaya vistāra \|*  
*vedera viruddha dharma karaye pracāra \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The naked Jain Mahavira’s teachings are in the mode of ignorance and go completely against those of the Vedic scriptures.**

*nāstika cārvāka kabhu veda nāhi māne \|*  
*sugata bauddherā eka prakāra bākhāne \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Charvaka was an atheist who never accepted the Vedic authority and the Buddhists following Sugata taught much the same.**

*gautama nyāyera kartā īśvara nā bhaje \|*  
*tāra hetu vāda mate nara mātra maje \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gautama propagated the Nyaya system and did not worship the Supreme Lord; anyone taken in by his sophistries becomes deeply entangled.**

## Through these teachings, one blasphemes the revealed scripture

*ei saba duṣṭa mate śrutira nindana \|*  
*kabhu spaṣṭa kabhu gupta bujhe vijña jana \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These mischievious doctrines are in fact offensive to the Veda; though sometimes overt, sometimes subtle, the wise recognize it as such.**

*ei saba mate thāki aparādhī haya \|*  
*ata eva ei sabe tyajibe niścaya \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who follow such teachings become offenders to the Holy Name, therefore one should take care to avoid them at all costs.**

## Mayavada is another very dangerous philosophy that goes against the Veda

*ei saba kumata chāḍi āra māyāvāda \|*
