*śuddha bhakti anubhavi haya nirvivāda \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should carefully abandon all the above doctrines, as well as Mayavada, for only then can one get an unadulterated taste for pure devotion.**

*māyāvāda asat śāstra gupta bauddha mata \|*  
*vedārtha vikṛti kali kālete sammata \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mayavada is an impure teaching that is in fact camouflaged Buddhism. It is a distortion of the Vedic teaching that has been widely accepted in the Kali Yuga.**

*umāpati brāhmaṇa rūpete prakāśila \|*  
*tomāra ājñāya teṅha ācārya ha-ila \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On Your orders, O Lord, Lord Shiva took the form of a Brahmin and became Shankaracharya in order to teach this doctrine.**

*jaimini ye rūpa mukhe veda mātra māne \|*  
*śrutira vikṛta artha jagate bākhāne \|\|43\|\|*  
*māyāvādī guru sei rūpa bauddha dharma \|*  
*veda vākye sthāpi ācchādila bhakti marma \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Just as Jaimini seemingly upheld the authority of the Vedas but propounded a distorted version of its conclusions to the world, the Mayavadi gurus similarly present the Buddhist teachings on the basis of Vedic proofs, but hide the Vedas’ essential message of devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

Ashtavakra, Dattatreya, Govinda, Gaudapada, Shankaracharya and his followers — these are all teachers of Mayavada. The ultimate goal of Buddhism is for the jiva to merge into non-existence or Nirvana. Even though Buddhists do not believe in a spiritual reality named Brahman, their ultimate principle of the void (*śūnya-vāda*) is in fact in every way identical with the Mayavadis’ formless, impersonal Brahman of pure consciousness. These concepts are diametrically opposed to the doctrine of devotional service.

*ei saba matavāde bhakti dūre yāya \|*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāmete jīva aparādha pāya \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All such philosophic doctrines distance one from bhakti, however, and by following them one becomes an offender to Krishna’s holy name.**

**Комментарий:**

Some Mayavadis chant the Holy Name even while believing in their atheistic doctrine. Their chanting is offemsive.

## The process for understanding the real meaning of the Shruti

*śrutira abhidhā vṛtti kari saṁyojana \|*  
*śuddha bhakti jīva pāya prema dhana \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who can harmonize the direct meaning of all the Shruti texts attains the wealth of prema through pure devotional service.**

**Комментарий:**

Wherever the literal meaning (*abhidhā vṛtti*) of a text is applicable, it is wrong to infer other meanings. If one rigorously follows the literal meaning of the Vedic texts, one comes to the conclusion that their teaching is pure devotion to Krishna.

*śrutite lakṣaṇā kare ayathā prakāre \|*  
*nitya satya dūre yāya aparādhe mare \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who infer meanings of texts usually do so incorrectly; distanced from the eternal truth, they are destroyed by offenses.**

*sarva veda sammata praṇava kṛṣṇa nāma \|*  
*sei nāme jīva saba pāya nitya dhāma \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The syllable OM, found in all the Shrutis, is Krishna’s name, by chanting It, the jiva attains the Lord’s eternal abode.**

*praṇava se mahā-vākya haya kṛṣṇa nāma \|*  
*tāhāte-i śrī-bhaktera satata viśrāma \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This syllable, the essence of the Veda, is a name of Krishna in which the saintly devotees find constant repose.**

*veda bale nāma cit svarūpa jagate \|*  
*nāmera ābhāse siddha haya sarva mate \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas declare the Name to be the Lord’s spiritual form in the world; this truth is fully revealed by even the semblance of the Holy Name.**

## The scriptures teach nothing but the chanting of the Holy Name

*ei saba veda śikṣā abhāgā nā māne \|*  
*nāme aparādha kare vedera nindane \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The unfortunate fool who disregards all such Vedic teachings commits the offense to the Holy Name of blaspheming the scripture.**

*śuddha nāma parāyaṇa yei mahājana \|*  
*vedāśraye pāya nāma rasa prema dhana \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great soul who is dedicated to chanting the Holy Name tastes the wealth of prema in the Name while taking guidance from the scripture.**

*sarva veda bale gāo hari-nāma sāra \|*  
*pāibe paramā prīti ānanda apāra \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the Vedas exhort us to sing the holy names of Hari and you will attain the incomparable ecstasy of divine love.**

*veda punaḥ bale yata mukta mahājana \|*  
*para-vyome sadā kare nāma saṅkīrtana \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas further say, “All the truly liberated souls eternally sing the Holy Names in the highest heaven.”**

## Tantras in the mode of ignorance also oppose the Vedas

*kali yuge mahājana māyā śakti bhaje \|*  
*cid ātmā puruṣa kṛṣṇa nāma rasa tyāje \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the Kali Yuga, some persons worship the goddess of the Illusory Energy and renounce the pleasures of the supreme soul Krishna’s holy name.**

*tāmasika tantra dhari śruti nindā kare \|*  
*madya-māṁse prīti kari adharmete mare \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They follow Tantras in the mode of darkness, which blaspheme the Shruti, and take pleasure in meat and liquor and other sinful activity.**

*se saba ninduka nāhi pāya kṛṣṇa nāma \|*  
*kabhu nāhi pāya kṛṣṇera vṛndāvana dhāma \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**None of these blasphemers can ever know the Holy Name, nor will they ever enter the Supreme Lord’s eternal abode of Vrindavan.**

## We need Maya Devi’s heartfelt blessings

*māyā devī se saba pāṣaṇḍe adhogati \|*  
*diyā nāmāmṛta āra nāhi dena mati \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The goddess Maya drags all such heretics ever downwards, depriving them of the will to relish the nectar of the Holy Name.**

*tabe yadi sādhu sevāya tuṣṭa hana māyā \|*  
*akapaṭe dena tabe kṛṣṇa pada chāyā \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nevertheless, if pleased with one who serves the saintly devotees; she stops deceiving them and leads them to refuge at Krishna’s lotus feet.**

*māyā kṛṣṇa dāsī bahirmukha jīve daṇḍe \|*  
*māyā pūjile-o śubha nāhi pāya bhaṇḍe \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Maya is Krishna’s maidservant: she punishes those who reject Him; yet, the hypocrite who worships her directly gains nothing of value.**

*kṛṣṇa nāma kare yei māyā devī tāre \|*  
*niṣkapaṭe kṛpā kari laya bhava pāre \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To those who chant the Holy Names, however, Maya Devi gives her heartfelt blessings and leads them across material ocean.**

**Комментарий:**

In this world, Maya Devi is worshiped as the goddess Durga or Kali. She is the shadow of Krishna’s internal, spiritual potency. Her primary purpose is to purify the souls who resist their eternal identity as Krishna’s servants and gradually leads them into awareness of Krishna. She thus gives two kinds of blessing: one is *niṣkapaṭa* (honest and unrestricted, or “heartfelt”), the other *sakapaṭa* (deceptive). When she gives her sincerest blessings, Maya Devi leads the jiva to bhakti through her own facility of giving understanding. When she gives her deceptive blessings, then she maintains her hold over the jiva by giving him temporary material pleasures. The ultimate manifestation of this deceptive form of mercy is shown when she casts the jiva into the state of extinction in the formless Brahman. Such a jiva is doomed.

*ata eva śruti-nindā aparādha tyaji \|*  
*aharahaḥ nāma saṅkīrtana rase maji \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one should give up all forms of blasphemy of the scriptures and remain absorbed day and night in the chanting of the Holy Name.**

## The remedy for this offense

*pramāde yadyapi haya se śruti-nindana \|*  
*anutāpe kari punaḥ se śruti vandana \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If ever I accidentally criticize the revealed scriptures, I shall sincerely repent of my error and glorify them.**

*kusuma tulasī diyā sei śruti gaṇe \|*  
*bhāgavata saha sadā pūjiba yatane \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Offering flowers and tulasi to all the revealed scriptures, I shall carefully worship them alongside the Srimad Bhagavatam.**

*bhāgavata śruti sāra kṛṣṇa avatāra \|*  
*avaśya karibe more karuṇā apāra \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Bhagavatam is the essence of the Shruti and Krishna’s incarnation and will surely shower its unlimited compassion on me.**

**Комментарий:**

The Srimad Bhagavatam is the essence of all the revealed scriptures. Those whose good fortune has yet to shine speak ill of the Bhagavatam. This is proof of their misfortune.

*haridāsa-pada-rajaḥ bharasā yāhāra \|*  
*nāma-cintāmaṇi hāra galāya tāhāra \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hankering for the dust of Srila Hari Das Thakur’s lotus feet, I wear this necklace of divine gems,** ***Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*****, around my neck.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the seventh chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “The offense of blaspheming the holy scriptures.”

## 8 - Doubting the Glories of the Holy Name

*tathārtha vādo hari nāmni kalpanam*  
*jaya gaura gadādhara śrī-rādhā-mādhava \|*  
*jaya gaura-līlā-sthalī jāhnavī vaiṣṇava \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Radha and Madhava!**  
**All glories to the place of Gauranga’s pastimes!**  
**All glories to the Ganges River and all the Vaishnavas!**

*hari nāme artha vāda kalpanā cintana \|*  
*pañcamāparādha prabho śrī śacīnandana \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O my Lord, son of Sachi! imagining the glories of the Holy Name to be exaggeration is the fifth offense against the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

The term *artha-vāda* means to think that the description of the Holy Name’s glories in the scriptures is not entirely true, but a kind of hyperbole only meant to incite people to chant. It is ture that in the karma-kanda and jnana-kanda sections of the Vedas, ritualism and philosophical speculation are praised in order awaken interest in these practices. This does not apply in the case of the Holy Name and to think it does is an offense.

## The glories of the Holy Name

*smṛti kahe helāya śraddhāya nāma laya \|*  
*kṛṣṇa tāre kṛpā kari hayena sadaya \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Smritis say whoever utters the Holy Name, either in faith or neglect, attracts the mercy of Lord Krishna and receives His blessings.**

*nāmera sadṛśa jñāna nāhika nirmala \|*  
*nāmera sadṛśa vrata nāhika prabala \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no knowledge as pure as the Holy Name. There is no vow as powerful as chanting the Holy Name.**

*nāmera sadṛśa dhyāna nāhi e jagate \|*  
*nāmera sadṛśa phala nāhi kona mate \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this world there is no meditation equal to chanting the Holy Name. No accomplishment is in any way equal to the Holy Name.**

*nāmera sadṛśa tyāga kona rūpe naya \|*  
*nāmera sadṛśa śama kabhu nāhi haya \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No renunciation is in any way equal to chanting the Holy Name. No mastery of the senses equals the chanting of the Holy Name.**

*nāmera sadṛśa puṇya nāhi e saṁsāre \|*  
*nāmera sadṛśa gati nā dekhi vicāre \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No pious act in this world equals the chanting of the Name. After much consideration, I see no refuge equal to the Holy Name.**

*nāma-i parama mukti nāma ucca gati \|*  
*nāma-i parama śānti nāma ucca sthiti \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I see no goal in life as elevated as the Holy Name, the supreme liberation; The Holy Name alone is the supreme peace and highest state of being.**

*nāma-i parama bhakti nāma śuddhā mati \|*  
*nāma-i parama prīti nāma parā smṛti \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is the supreme devotion; it is the purest state of mind; The Holy Name is the supreme love; it is the highest remembrance;**

*nāma-i kāraṇa tattva nāma sarva prabhu \|*  
*parama ārādhya nāma guru-rūpe vibhu \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is the cause of all causes; it is the lord of all creation; The Name is supremely worshipable; it acts as the supreme guide and guru.**

## Of all God’s names, “Krishna” is supreme

*sahasra viṣṇu nāmera tulya haya eka rāma nāma \|*  
*tina rāma nāma tulya eka kṛṣṇa nāma \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reciting Vishnu’s thousand names is equal to saying once the name of Rama; but just one utterance of Krishna’s name equals three repetitions of “Rama.”**

## One suffers due to thinking the glories of the Name are exaggerated

*śruti gaṇa nāmera māhātmya sadā gāya \|*  
*nāmera cit-tattva bali jagate jānāya \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Shrutis constantly glorify the wonders of the Holy Name; they tell the world that the Name is a transcendental, spiritual thing.**

*śruti smṛti pradarśita nāmera ye phala \|*  
*tāhe artha vāda kare pāṣaṇḍa prabala \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The benefits of chanting the Name are shown in both Shruti and Smriti, so anyone who claims they are an exaggeration must be a hardened atheist.**

*hari-nāme artha vāda ye adhama kare \|*  
*se pāpiṣṭha narakete paci’ paci’ mare \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The lowly creature who considers the Holy Name’s glories to be imaginary is a most sinful person whose life will continue to deteriorate until death.**

*ye bale nāmera phala-śruti satya naya \|*  
*nāme ruci dite mātra tata phala kaya \|\|15\|\|*  
*śāstrera tātparya āra jīva hitāhita \|*  
*se adhama nāhi jāne bujhe viparīta \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who say, “The benefits attributed to chanting the Name are not true; they have been made only to increase our attraction for the Holy Name,” have no idea of the scriptures’ meaning or what is beneficial for humanity. Their understanding is thus completely topsy turvy.**

**Комментарий:**

Without an accumulation of the type of merit that leads to devotion (*bhakti-sukṛti*), one can never develop faith in the science of bhakti-yoga. Of all the types of devotional activity, the chanting of the Holy Name is the best. Without sufficient prior pious actions, no one can acquire a taste for chanting nor have faith in its unlimitedly wonderful results. Those who are attached to just one section of the scriptures cannot perceive their overall purpose.

## The claims made for the chanting are true; no further explanation is necessary

*karma kāṇḍa āche ta kaitava svārtha jñāna \|*  
*bhakti tattve nāme tāhā nahe vidyamāna \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The chicanery of purely selfish motivation is rife in the karma kanda, which is completely absent in discussions of bhakti or the Holy Name.**

*karma kāṇḍa phala śruti rocanārtha jāni \|*  
*bhakti tattve phala śruti nitya satya māni \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The glorifications of ritualistic activity’s benefits are meant to entice people, whereas the glorifications of bhakti are simple statements of eternal fact.**

*nāma tattve śāṭhya nāhi pāya kabhu sthāna \|*  
*nijera nāhika svārtha nāma kari dāna \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**False pretence has no place in the teachings of the Holy Name, and one who distributes the Name never does so for selfish material gain.**

## One must analyze the meaning of the karma kanda

*nāma dāna śraddhāvāne yei jana kare \|*  
*kṛṣṇa dāsya kare sei svārtha parihāre \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The guru who gives the Holy Name to a faithful aspirant is rendering service to Krishna and acting entirely selflessly.**

*karma karāile yājakera artha lābha \|*  
*ata eva tāhe kaitavera ta prabhāva \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ritualist priest performs sacrifices to make money from his client, therefore his actions are influenced by duplicity.**

*veda smṛti nāma phala ananta bākhāne \|*  
*svārtha buddhi śūnya se ye tāhā nāhi māne \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The revealed scriptures all tell of the unlimited benefits of the Name; those who do not believe it have no sense of their own true self-interest.**

**Комментарий:**

One’s true self-interest is spiritual in nature. When we speak of selfishness, we mean an attitude based on a misconceived notion of the self as the body or mind.

*karma saba śubhāśubha jaḍera āśraye \|*  
*jaḍa-maya-phala yāce yajamāna caye \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The law of works is all about good and bad results in this world and the ritualist seeks mundane fruits through his religious acts.**

*karma phala dūre pheli’ yebā kare karma \|*  
*hṛdaya viśuddha tāra haya ei marma \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who engages in religious ritual, casting aside those fruits, will find that such activity purifies the heart. This is their real purpose.**

*viśuddha hṛdaye ātma-rati sunirmala \|*  
*udaya ha-iyā haya kramaśaḥ prabala \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the heart is pure, one develops pure love for the true self and this unblemished attitude gradually grows ever stronger.**

**Комментарий:**

Pure love for the true self (*ātma-rati*) implies a parallel detachment for the things of the world.

## The Holy Name is divine and spiritual. Its glories cannot be exaggerated

*nāma sei ātma-rati nije upasthita \|*  
*sādhana kālete sādhya vastura vihita \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such pure love for the true self is directly present in the Name, for the goal of spiritual life is directly present, even at the time of practice.**

*karmera carama phala nāma-rasa haya \|*  
*sādhu-rūpe anuṣṭhita karmete niścaya \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ultimate positive effect of all religious ritual is a taste for chanting, but it can only be achieved if performed with this higher understanding.**

*ata eva caudda loka bhramiyā brāhmaṇa \|*  
*yei phala nāhi pāna nāma tāhā hana \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is the one thing the ritualistic Brahmin will never find even after wandering through the fourteen strata of the universe.**

*nāma phala sarvopari avaśya ha-ibe \|*  
*karmī jñānī hiṁsā kari nāme ki karibe \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The benefits of chanting are above anything obtained elsewhere. Its glories cannot be diminished by envious karmis or jnanis.**

## Even the semblance of the Holy Name gives all the results of karma and jnana.

*sarva-karma-phala nāmābhāse labdha haya \|*  
*sarva-jñāna-phala nāmābhāsete milaya \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the results of the karma kanda can be had from the semblance of the Name; and so it is for all the results of the jnana kanda.**

*ābhāse milila yadi eta ucca phala \|*  
*nāma vastu tato’dhika pradāne prabala \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**So if mere Namabhasa can offer such wonderful results, then how much more the Pure Name has to offer!**

**Комментарий:**

In fact, Namabhasa can bestow more on the jiva than either karma or jnana. If such benefits accrue from chantintg the mere semblance of the Name, then it stands to reason that much greater benefits come from the pure Name.

*ata eva śāstre yata nāma phala gāya \|*  
*śuddha nāmāśrita jana niścaya tā pāya \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, whatever fruits the scriptures say are gained from chanting come easily to a devotee who who has taken refuge in the Pure Name.**

## Nothing auspicious comes to one who doubts the benefits of chanting

*ihāte sandeha yāra se adhama jana \|*  
*nāma aparādhe tāra avaśya patana \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who doubt these facts are but the lowest of humanity who are doomed by their offenses to the Holy Name.**

*vede rāmāyaṇe āra bhārate purāṇe \|*  
*ādi antye madhye harināmere bākhāne \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The revealed scriptures – Vedas, Ramayana, Mahabharata, and Puranas, from beginning to middle to end, are but a commentary on the Holy Name.**

*nāma-phala śruti vākya anādi niścala \|*  
*tāhe artha-vāda kalpanāra kibā phala \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They consistently glorify the benefits of chanting as eternal and unchanging. What is gained by someone who interprets these texts as imaginary?**

## The Holy Name’s potencies are unlimitedly greater than those of jnana and karma.

*nāma nāmī eka nāme diyā sarva śakti \|*  
*sarvopari kariyācha tava nāma śakti \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Name and Named are one, O Lord, so all Your powers are in Your name. You have thus made devotion to Your holy name the topmost religious act.**

*tumi ta svatantra tattva sarva śaktimān \|*  
*tomāra icchāya yata vidhira vidhāna \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You are the fully independent and omnipotent Supreme Substance. The existence of all rules and all systems arises out of Your desire.**

*karmake karecha jaḍa āra brahma jñāne \|*  
*diyācha nirvāṇa śakti svatantra vidhāne \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You have designated ritualism as mundane, while in knowledge of Brahman You have placed Your impersonal energy, by Your independent will.**

*icchāmaya tumi prabhu svīya nāmākṣare \|*  
*arpiyācha saba śakti āra ke ki kare \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then by this same free will, You have placed in Your own name Your entire personal potency. How can anyone change this?**

**Комментарий:**

You are the supreme independent, free-willed Person. You have placed all Your potencies in Your own name, so how can anyone’s objections to this be taken seriously?

*ata eva tava nāma sarva śaktimān \|*  
*nāme artha-vāda nāhi karibe vidvān \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore Your names are themselves all-powerful; so a wise person will not think their glories to be exaggerations.**

## The remedy for the arthavada offense

*nāme artha-vāda aparādha ghaṭe yadi \|*  
*dante tṛṇa dhari yāi vaiṣṇava-saṁsadi \|\|41\|\|*  
*aparādha jānāiyā vaiṣṇava-caraṇe \|*  
*kṣamā māgi kākuti kariyā ṛju-mane \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who offends the Holy Name by doubting its glories, should humbly go to the assembly of Vaishnavas and there confess the offense to the Vaishnavas and plead for their forgiveness in a straightforward manner.**

*nāmera mahimā jñātā bhāgavata jana \|*  
*kṣamā kari kṛpā kari dibe āliṅgana \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of the Lord know the glories of the Holy Name and will forgive the offender, to whom they will give their merciful embrace.**

*nāme artha vāda āra kalpana-manana \|*  
*kabhu nāhi habe citte māyā viḍambana \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Never again will he think the Name’s glories are exaggeration or imaginary; never again will the bewilderments of illusion affect his mind.**

*artha-vāda-kārī saha haile sambhāṣaṇa \|*  
*sa-cele jāhnavī-jale kariba majjana \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I vow that should I ever talk with one who doubts the power of the Name, I shall immediately immerse myself, fully clothed, into the Ganges.**

**Комментарий:**

One should not even look at the face of someone who doubts the power of the Holy Name. If one should accidentally meet or talk with such a person, then one should immediately bathe, fully clothed, in the holy water of the Ganges for purification. If the Ganges is not nearby, one should bathe in some other pure water. In the event that this too is not possible, one should bathe mentally for self-purification.

*kṛṣṇa priyā vaṁśī kṛpā bharasā yāhāra \|*  
*harināma cintāmaṇi tāra alaṅkāra \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I, Bhaktivinoda, whose hopes rest in the mercy of Krishna’s beloved flute, decorate myself with the jewel of the Holy Name,** ***Harināma-cintāmaṇi*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the eighth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “The offense of doubting the glories of the Holy Name.”

## 9 - Committing sins on the strength of chanting

*nāmno balād yasya hi pāpa buddhir*  
*na vidyate tasya yamair hi śuddhiḥ*

*gaura gadādhara jaya jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*jaya jaya sītādvaita jaya bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to Sita Devi and Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to all the devotees!**

## All anarthas disappear as a result of chanting the Holy Name

*haridāsa bale nāma śuddha sattva maya \|*  
*bhāgyavān jīva kare nāmera āśraya \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das said: The Holy Name is pure existence through and through. Only the most fortunate soul takes shelter of the Holy Name.**

*ati śīghra tāhāra anartha dūre yāya \|*  
*hṛdaya-daurbalya āra sthāna nāhi pāya \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such a soul’s anarthas are quickly removed from the heart and weakness of spirit no longer finds a place there.**

*nāme dṛḍha haile nāhi haya pāpe mati \|*  
*pūrva pāpa dagdha haya citta śuddha ati \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One fixed in the Holy Name has no desire to commit sin; his previous sins are burned and the mind purified.**

*pāpa āra pāpa-bīja pāpera vāsanā \|*  
*avidyā tāhāra mūla e tina yantranā \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sin, the seed of sin, that is, the desire to commit sin, and ignorance, the root of sin—these three are the source of suffering.**

**Комментарий:**

The desire to commit sin is the seed of sinful activity, and this desire comes out of fundamental ignorance of one’s eternal identity. These three elements of sin are identified as *kleśa*.

*sarva jīve dayā āsi ha-ibe udaya \|*  
*jīvera maṅgala ceṣṭā satata karaya \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Compassion for all creation then awakens in such a soul who starts constantly acting for the ultimate benefit of all living beings.**

*jīvera santāpa kabhu sahite nā pāre \|*  
*yāhe para-tāpa yāya tāra ceṣṭā kare \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those fixed in the Holy Name cannot bear a jiva’s slightest suffering, and do everything possible to relieve them of their pain.**

*viṣaya-pipāsā ati tuccha mane haya \|*  
*indriya-lālasā tāra citte nāhi raya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They find the thirst for sense pleasures to be exceedingly trivial; lust to please the senses finds no place in their heart.**

*kanaka-kāminī ceṣṭā prati ghṛṇā kare \|*  
*yathā dharma lābhe tuṣṭa thāki prāṇa dhare \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They finds all endeavor to enjoy wealth and women repulsive and are satisfied to live from whatever they gain through honest work.**

*bhakti anukūla saba karaye svīkāra \|*  
*bhakti pratikūla nāhi kare aṅgīkāra \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They accept only what is favorable for executing devotional service and do not take up any activity that is unfavorable to such devotion.**

*kṛṣṇa rakṣā kartā eka mātra bali jāne \|*  
*jīvane pālana-kartā kṛṣṇa ihā māne \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They know that Krishna alone can protect them and that only Krishna will maintain them in their lives.**

*ahaṁ mama buddhy-āsakti nā rākhe hṛdaye \|*  
*dīna-bhāve nāma laya sakala samaye \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They harbor no attachment for ego, nor for any possessions, and constantly chant the Holy Name with a humble heart.**

*svabhāvataḥ yāra ei rūpa nāmāśraya \|*  
*pāpe mati pāpācāra tāhāra ki haya \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who spontaneously take shelter of the Holy Name in this way neither commit nor are they inclined to any sinful act.**

## Previous sin and its residue are quickly destroyed

*pūrva duṣṭa-bhāva tāra krame haya kṣīṇa \|*  
*pavitra svabhāva śīghra ha-ibe pravīṇa \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the chanter’s previous sinful character gradually diminishes, his pure nature rapidly takes control of his being.**

*ei sandhi kāle pūrva pāpera sambandha \|*  
*thākite-o pāre kichu dina pāpa gandha \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**During this transformative stage, some effects of past sin may remain, and the faint residue of such sin linger on for some time.**

**Комментарий:**

After developing a taste for chanting the Holy Name, one finds oneself in an in-between state or *sandhi-kāla.* In this state of affairs, one has no inclination to perform new sinful acts, but past habits may still remain to some extent, even though they are in the process of dissipating. This is what is mean by “residue of sin.”

*nāmera saṁsarge yata sumati udaya \|*  
*haye sei pāpa gandha śīghra kare kṣaya \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All good qualities and tendencies come out of contact with the Name; their arousal quickly overcomes this clinging odor of sin.**

*pratijñā karecha nātha arjuna nikaṭe \|*  
*mora bhakta kabhu nāhi paḍibe saṅkaṭe \|\|17\|\|*  
*saṅkaṭa samaye āmi ha-iba sahāya \|*  
*ata eva pāpa yāya tomāra kṛpāya \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, You made a solemn promise to Arjuna: “My devotee will never fall into danger. If ever there is a crisis, I personally come to their rescue.” Because of this, all their sins are destroyed by Your mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhagavad Gita 9.30-31: “Even if a person of very bad behavior worships Me with undivided devotion, he is to be thought of as saintly, for he has the proper resolution. He quickly becomes righteous and attains lasting peace. O Son of Kunti, declare it boldly that no devotee of Mine is ever lost.”

*jñāna mārgī kaṣṭe pāpa kariyā damana \|*  
*tavāśraya chāḍi śīghra haya ta patana \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those on the philosophical path overcome sin with great difficulty; but because they do not take shelter of You, they easily fall down.**

*tava padāśraya yāra sei mahājana \|*  
*vighna nā pāibe kabhu siddhānta vacana \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, the elevated soul who takes shelter of Your lotus feet will never be impeded by obstacles. This is my conclusion.**

## A devotee who accidentally commits a sin has no need for other atonements

*yadi kabhu pramāde ghaṭaya kona pāpa \|*  
*bhakta tabu nāhi sahe prāyaścitta tāpa \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee may commit some sin by accident, but even so he is unable to abide the misery of ritualistic atonements.**

**Комментарий:**

The word for atonement is *prāyaścitta.* The scriptures are full of penances prescribed for specific transgressions. These usually take the form of some austerity or voluntary self-deprivation. Atonements that are not specifically conducive to awakening love for Krishna are seen by devotees as a terrible waste of time. A Vaishnava’s only austerity is to act in relation to Krishna.

*se pāpa kṣaṇika nāhi pāya avasthiti \|*  
*nāma rase bhese yāya nā deya durgati \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee’s fall in to sin is momentary, it does not become a habit; it is merely a passing phase that is drowned in the nectar of the Holy Name.**

## What happens to a person who commits sin on the strength of chanting?

*kintu yadi kona jana nāme kari bala \|*  
*ācare nūtana pāpa, se jana cañcala \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, if someone thinks that because the Name is so powerful he may go on committing new sins, he is fickle and undependable.**

*se kevala kapaṭatā kariyā āśraya \|*  
*nāma-aparādha pāya śoka-mṛti-bhaya \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such devotees have taken refuge in nothing but hypocrisy and their offenses to the Name subject them to the fear of loss and death.**

## The difference between accidental and premeditated action

*pramāda ghaṭanā āra vicārita karme \|*  
*sampūrṇa prabheda āche bhakti-śāstra marme \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**An accidental event and a premeditated action are completely different according to all the devotional scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Sin can take place in two different ways. On the one hand, one may suddenly engage in sinful acts in response to unexpected temptation; on the other, one may do so after careful consideration, i.e., planning beforehand, “I will commit such and such a sin.” There is a big difference between these two ways of acting, mainly in the depth of remorse felt by the sinner afterwards.

## For one who has taken shelter of the Name, even the intention to commit sin is an offense

*saṁsārī mānava yebā ācaraye pāpa \|*  
*prāyaścitta āche tāra āra anutāpa \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ordinary worldly householder who commits a sin must engage in some act of penance, accompanied by remorse.**

*kintu nāma-bale yadi pāpe kare mati \|*  
*prāyaścitta nāhi tāra baḍa-i durgati \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But that person who intends to commit sin on the strength of the Name is doomed, for no amount of penance will help him.**

*bahu yama yātanādi pāile-o tāra \|*  
*sei aparādha ha-ite nā haya uddhāra \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even after being much punished by Yamaraj after death, the offender will still not be delivered from this offense.**

*pāpa mati-mātre haya e-rūpa yantranā \|*  
*pāpācāre yata doṣe tāra ki gaṇanā \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If the mere intent to commit sin causes such suffering, who can estimate the consequences of actually performing the acts themselves?**

## The sinful acts of deceitful hypocrites who place their hope in the Holy Name is nothing more than monkey renunciation

*śāstre śuniyāche nāma yata pāpa hare \|*  
*koṭi janme mahā pāpī karite nā pāre \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One may hear from the scripture, “The Name can destroy more sin than the greatest sinner could perform in a million lifetimes.”**

*pañca vidha pāpa mahā pātaka avadhi \|*  
*nāmābhāse yāya śāstra gāya niravadhi \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are five kinds of sin, culminating with the** ***mahāpātaka*** **or mortal sin, but all of these are destroyed by merely the semblance of the Holy Name.**

*sei ta bharasā kari pravañcaka jana \|*  
*śaṭhatā kariyā nāma karaye grahaṇa \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Placing their hopes in scriptural promises of this sort, some rascal charlatans deceptively take up the chanting.**

*kaṣṭera saṁsāra chāḍi vairāgīra veśe \|*  
*kanaka kāminī āśe phire deśe deśe \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking household life too troublesome, they dress up like monks and wander from place to place in search of wealth and women.**

*tumi ta balecha prabhu markaṭa vairāgī \|*  
*kāminī sambhāṣi phire dharma gṛha tyāgī \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord, You have named such persons “monkey renunciates,” for they associate with women though they have left married life.**

**Комментарий:**

Chaitanya Mahaprabhu condemned such false renunciation in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (Antya 2.102ff), in connection with the behavior of Chota Hari Das. A “monkey renunciate” is one who keeps the company of women after taking the renounced orders.

## If one does not take sincere shelter of the Holy Name, this offense is unavoidable.

*vairāgyera chale keha gṛhe kāṭe kāla \|*  
*sambhāṣya nā haya saba viśvera jañjāla \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who continue to live with their wives while dressing like monks should never be spoken to, for they are the dregs of human society.**

*gṛhe thāku vane yāu tāte nāhi doṣa \|*  
*niṣpāpe karuk nāma pāiyā santoṣa \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whether one lives with the family or becomes a hermit is of no consequence; one should take satisfaction in chanting the Name while leading a sinless life.**

**Комментарий:**

A devotee who has taken shelter of the Holy Name may remain in householder life or lead a life of renunciation, for if one’s home environment is conducive for chanting, then it is better than renunciation. Conversely, if one’s householder life is an obstacle to chanting, then it is a Vaishnava’s duty to renounce it.

*nāma bale pāpa mati mahā aparādha \|*  
*tāhāte majile haya bhakti tattve bādha \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intent to sin on the strength of chanting is a great offense and anyone who does it will never understand the meaning of bhakti.**

## Those still chanting Namabhasa become offenders by associating with such hypocrites.

*nāmābhāsī janera kusaṅga yadi haya \|*  
*tabe ei aparādha ghaṭibe niścaya \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If person on the Namabhasa stage falls into bad company, then this offense will most certainly arise.**

*śuddha nāmodaya yāra hṛdaye ha-ibe \|*  
*ei nāma aparādha tāra nā ghaṭibe \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, one in whose heart the Pure Name has arisen is never affected by this offense to the Holy Name.**

## Pure devotees are free from the ten Namaparadhs

*śuddha-nāmāśrita jane aparādha daśa \|*  
*kona rūpe kona kāle nā kare paraśa \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who have taken complete shelter of chanting the pure Name remain ever untouched by any of the ten Namaparadhs.**

*nāmāśrita jane nāma sadā rakṣā kare \|*  
*aparādha kabhu tāra nā ha-ite pāre \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name protects those who take refuge of It, and thus there is no possibility of their committing offenses.**

*yata dina śuddha nāma nā haya udaya \|*  
*tata dina aparādha ākramaṇe bhaya \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But as long as the pure name has not risen in the devotee’s heart, there is always a danger of committing these offenses.**

*ata eva nāmābhāsī yadi bhāla cāya \|*  
*nāma bale pāpa buddhi ha-ite palāya \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, those devotees chanting Namabhasa who seek their own welfare should at all costs flee from sinful intentions on the strength of chanting.**

## For how long must one struggle to avoid this offense?

*śuddha-nāmāśrita-jana-saṅga-bala dhari’ \|*  
*aparādha sa-tarkatā sarvadā ācari’ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking strength in the company of those who chant the Pure Name, I always stay on the alert for offenses to the Holy Name.**

*śuddha-nāma yāra mukhe tāra dṛḍha mana \|*  
*kṛṣṇa haite vicalita nahe eka kṣaṇa \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants the Holy Name purely has strength of mind and so does not waver from Krishna for a second.**

*ata eva nāma bala yata dina naya \|*  
*tata dina aparādhe karibeka bhaya \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, as long as one does not have firm resolve in chanting, he will be in danger of committing offenses to the Holy Name.**

*viśeṣa yatane pāpa buddhi dūra kari’ \|*  
*aharniśi mukhe balibeka hari hari \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee must especially guard against a sinful mentality by continuously chanting the name of Hari, day and night.**

*śrī-guru-kṛpāya habe susambandha-jñāna \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti kṛṣṇa-nāma tāhāte vidhāna \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the spiritual master’s grace, the aspirant will gain proper knowledge and through it, pure devotion to Krishna and His holy name.**

## The remedy for this offense

*yadyapi pramāde nāma-bale pāpa-buddhi \|*  
*śuddha vaiṣṇavera saṅge kari tāra śuddhi \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even if by some error you should commit this offense, you can still be purified by remaining in the company of pure Vaishnavas.**

*pāpa-spṛhā bāṭapāḍa pathe āsi’ dhare \|*  
*viśuddha vaiṣṇava gaṇa patha rakṣā kare \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The temptation to sin is like a highway robber, always ready to pounce; but the Vaishnavas are like bodyguards who protect the traveller.**

*uccaiḥ-svare ḍāki rakṣakera nāma dhari’ \|*  
*palāibe bāṭapāḍa āsibe praharī \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As soon as you call out aloud, appealing to the guardians by name, the highwaymen flee, fearing the imminent arrival of the police.**

*ādare balibe bhāi nāhi kara bhaya \|*  
*āmi ta rakṣaka tava śuna mahāśaya \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Vaishnava will reassure you, saying, “Brother! Sister! have no fear, I am here to protect you. Have confidence in me.”**

*kevala vaiṣṇava-pada-dāsya-vrata yāra \|*  
*harināma-cintāmaṇi pāya sei chāra \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I, Bhaktivinoda, whose one vow is to serve the Vaishnavas’ lotus feet, sing the** ***Harināma-cintāmaṇi*****, though I am worthless.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the ninth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “The offense of sinning on the strength of chanting the Holy Name.”

## 10 - Instructing the faithless person in the Holy Name

*aśraddadhāne vimukhe 'py aśṛṇvati*  
*yaś copadeśaḥ śiva nāmāparādhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**It is a great offense to the Holy Name to instruct someone who has no faith, who is opposed to devotion, or who is not attentive.**

*gaura gadādhara jaya jāhnavā-jīvana \|*  
*jaya jaya sītādvaita jaya bhakta-gaṇa \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to Sita Devi and Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to Srivasa Pandit and all the devotees!**

*kara yuḍi’ haridāsa balena vacana \|*  
*āra nāma aparādha karaha śravaṇa \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das joined his hands in supplication and said: Now, Lord, hear from me about the next offense to the Holy Name.**

## The qualification to chant comes from faith, or believing deeply in the Holy Name.

*yāhāra hṛdaye śraddhā nā ha-ila udaya \|*  
*nāma nāhi śune bahirmukha durāśaya \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wretched creature in whose heart faith has not arisen does not really hear the Holy Name, for he has turned away from the Lord.**

*nā janme se janāra nāme adhikāra \|*  
*śraddhā mātra adhikāra ei tattva-sāra \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such a person has no qualification for chanting the Holy Name, for the essence of Vaishnava teaching is that only faith makes us worthy.**

*sajjāti, sat-kula, jñāna, bala, vidyā dhana \|*  
*nāme adhikāra dite nā haya kāraṇa \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**High birth, noble relations, knowledge, strength, education or wealth, confer no special right to enter into the mysteries of the Holy Name.**

*nāmera māhātmya yei sudṛḍha viśvāsa \|*  
*śāstra-mate śraddhā sei sarvatra prakāśa \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who has unshakable belief in the glories of the Holy Name will always show full faith in the verdict of the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The Holy Name is the jiva’s most valued possession. Faith means to believe that by doing nothing more than taking shelter of Krishna, one has already completed whatever good deeds or religious acts are necessary. One who does not have such faith lacks the necessary qualifications to chant the Holy Name.

## One who gives the Holy Name to the faithless becomes an offender.

*śraddhā nāhi janme yāra hari nāma tāre \|*  
*sādhu jana nāhi dena vaiṣṇava ācāre \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A sadhu should not give the Holy Name to one in whom faith has not taken birth; this is the rule of Vaishnava behavior.**

*śraddhā hīna jana yadi hari nāma pāya \|*  
*avajñā karibe mātra sarva śāstre gāya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**People without faith who receive the Holy Name will simply neglect or disrespect it, say the scriptures.**

*śūkarake dile ratna se cūrṇa karibe \|*  
*bānarake dile vastra chiṅḍiyā phelibe \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If you give a hog a jewel, he will grind it into dust, and if you give a silk cloth to a monkey, he will tear it into shreds.**

*śraddhā hīna peye nāma aparādhe mare \|*  
*saṅge saṅge guruke abhakta śīghra kare \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A faithless person who takes the Name dies of his offenses and immediately turns his teacher into a non-devotee.**

## How to behave with a faithless person who wants to be initated

*śraddhā virahita jana śaṭhatā kariyā \|*  
*hari nāma māge vaiṣṇavera kāche giyā \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A person devoid of faith sometimes falseheartedly approaches a Vaishnava to beg initiation into the Holy Name.**

*tāhāra vañcanā vākya bujhi sādhu jana \|*  
*hari nāma nāhi dena tāre kadācana \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sadhu sees through such imposture every time and will refuse to give him initation in the Holy Name.**

*sādhu bale ohe bhāi śāṭhya parihara \|*  
*pratiṣṭhāśā dūre rākhi nāme śraddhā kara \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sadhu says to him, “Brother, give up your false heart. Give up your desire for prestige and have faith in the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

The falseheartedness of the pretender takes the following forms: “Normally, after one gets the Holy Name, there is no more fear of committing sin, for the Holy Name takes away all sins. If I chant constantly, people will take me to be a Vaishnava and offer me respect. Then I will be able to achieve many of my personal goals. I have lost so much social prestige because of leading a sinful life, but by receiving initiation in the Holy Name, I will be able to recover my social position. I will thus become very happy materially by taking up the chanting.”

*nāme śraddhā haile nāma anāyāse pābe \|*  
*nāmera prabhāve e saṁsāre tare yābe \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will readily give you the Holy Name when you have faith, then you will easily cross the material ocean, by the power of the Name.**

*yata dina nāhi tava nāme śraddhā bhāi \|*  
*nāma laite tomāra ta adhikāra nāi \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But, my dear brother, you have no right to be initiated into the chanting as long as you have no real faith in the Holy Name.**

*śrī-nāma-māhātmya sādhu-śāstra-mukhe śuna \|*  
*pratiṣṭhāśā chāḍi’ dainya karaha grahaṇa \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Go and hear the glories of the Holy Name from saints and scriptures; then give up your desire for prestige and adopt a humble attitude.**

*nāme śraddhā hale tabe guru mahājana \|*  
*nāma arpibena bhāi nāma mahā dhana \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Once you have faith in the Name, then a capable spiritual master will happily give you the great treasure of the Name, my dear brother.”**

*śraddhā hīna jane artha lobhe nāma diyā \|*  
*narakete yāya nāmāparādha majiyā \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who gives initiation in the Holy Name out of a desire for wealth falls down into hell as a result of his offense.**

**Комментарий:**

If the guru knows that a person approaching him for initiation is insincere, faithless or motivated, and still initiates him, either for the material gain or the prestige it will bring him, is committing an offense to the Holy Name. If, on the other hand, at the time of initiating him he thought the disciple was faithful but later discovers him to be a faithless pretender, he must take the appropriate steps.

## The remedy for this Namaparadh

*pramāde yadyapi nāma upadeśa haya \|*  
*śraddhā hīne tabe guru pāya mahā bhaya \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If a guru inadvertently instructs a faithless person in the Holy Name, he places himself in great danger.**

*vaiṣṇava samāje tāhā kari vijñāpana \|*  
*sei duṣṭa śiṣya tyāga kare mahā jana \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He should publicly announce his mistake to the Vaishnava community and publicly renounce the insincere disciple.**

*tāhā nā karile guru aparādha krame \|*  
*bhakti hīna durācāra haya māyā bhrame \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If he does not do so, the spiritual master will be adversely affected by his offense; he will lose his devotion and fall into sin and illusion.**

*ata eva prabhu yāre ādeśa karile \|*  
*nāma pracārite tāre ei ājñā dile \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, O Lord, when You commanded Your devotees to preach the Holy Name, You instructed them in the following way:**

## The orders of the Lord on this matter

*śraddhāvān jane kara nāma upadeśa \|*  
*nāma mahimāya pūrṇa kara sarva-deśa \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Instruct only the faithful person in the chanting of the Holy Name, fill every land with the glorification of the Holy Name.**

*ucca saṅkīrtane kara śraddhāra pracāra \|*  
*śraddhā labhi jīva kare sad guru vicāra \|\|24\|\|*  
*sad guru nikaṭe kare śrī nāma grahaṇa \|*  
*anāyāse pāya tabe kṛṣṇa prema dhana \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Propagate faith in the Holy Name by loudly singing Its glories. When the jivas get such faith, they will be able to recognize the Sat Guru and will take initation in the Holy Name from him. Then they will easily obtain love of God, Krishna prema.**

*cora veśyā śaṭha ādi pāpāsakta jane \|*  
*chāḍāiyā pāpa mati dibe śraddhā dhane \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The guru should first make sinners like thieves, prostitutes, and cheaters give up their sinful ways and then implant faith in their hearts.**

*suśraddha haile dibe nāma upadeśa \|*  
*ei rūpe nāma diyā tāra sarva deśa \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“When they have obtained such firm faith, they can be given initiation; You can only save the world if you distribute the Name in this fashion.”**

## The result of this Namaparadh

*ihā nā kariyā yini dena nāma dhana \|*  
*sei aparādhe tāṅra narake patana \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who does not heed this instruction and goes on initiating will fall into a hellish existence as a result of his offense.**

*nāma peye śiṣya kare nāma aparādha \|*  
*tāhāte gurura haya bhakti-rasa bādha \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The disciple who after initiation commits offenses to the Holy Name disrupts the spiritual master’s absorption in spiritual life.**

*ei nāma aparādhe duṅhe śiṣya guru \|*  
*narakete yāya ei aparādha uru \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This offense is bad for both spiritual master and disciple, causing both of them to fall down into hell.**

## Give faith first, then initiation

*jagā mādhā prati tumi mahā kṛpā kari \|*  
*nāme śraddhā diyā nāma dile gaura hari \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Gaura Hari, You bestowed such blessings on Jagai and Madhai: You first instilled faith in them and then gave them the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

These brothers are the ancestors of the Roy family established in Mayapur. Their names were Jagadananda and Madhavananda. People corrupted their names to Jagai and Madhai when they saw them engaged in greatly sinful acts.

*adbhuta caritra tava sarva jana gaṇa \|*  
*śraddhāya karuka anukaraṇa caraṇa \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, Your character and activities are exemplary; may everyone faithfully follow in Your footsteps.**

*bhakta pāda bhaktite vinoda yāhāra \|*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi alaṅkāra tāra \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose only pleasure is in devotion to the Vaishnavas, wears this** ***Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*** **as his only ornament.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the tenth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “Instructing the faithless person in the Holy Name.”\|

## 11 - Thinking the Holy Name is just another pious activity

*dharma vrata tyāga hutādi sarva*  
*śubha kriyā sāmyam api pramādaḥ \|*

### 0

### Verse 1

*jaya jaya gauracandra nāma avatāra*  
*jaya jaya harināma sarva-tattva-sāra \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Lord Gaurachandra,**  
**who has descended to propagate the Holy Name!**  
**All glories to the Holy Name,**  
**the essence of all spiritual teaching!**

### Verse 2

*haridāsa bale prabhu kara avadhāna*  
*anya śubha karma nahe nāmera samāna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Srila Hari Das Thakur continued: O Lord, hear me now: No other pious activities can ever be equal to the Holy Name.**

## The original form of the Holy Name

### Verse 3

*tumi ta cinmaya sūrya tomāra svarūpa*  
*sampūrṇa cinmaya ei tattva aparūpa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! You are like the spiritual sun. Your essential being is completely spiritual. This is the wonderful truth.**

### Verse 4

*sarvatra cinmaya tava śrī-vigraha haya*  
*nāma dhāma līlā tava sampūrṇa cinmaya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Your every appearance, Your form is always completely spiritual, as are Your name, abode, and pastimes.**

### Verse 5

*tava mukhya nāma saba tomāte abhinna*  
*jaḍīya vastura nāma vastu haite bhinna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Your primary names are all completely non-different from You, unlike material things, whose names differ from their substance.**

### Verse 6

*bhakta mukhe āise nāma goloka ha-ite*  
*ātmā haite dehe vyāpi’ nāce jihvādite \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name descends from Goloka onto the pure devotee’s tongue; It then emanates from his soul, permeates his body, and dances on his tongue.**

### Verse 7

*ei jñāne nāma laile haya tava nāma*  
*nāme jaḍa buddhi yāra tāra duḥkha-grāma \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Your name is truly present for one who chants with this knowledge, whereas one who nourishes a material concept of the Name experiences misery.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who think that the Holy Name is a mundane sound must suffer lengthy punishment in hell. If you catch sight of the face of such an offender, you should bathe fully clothed while concentrating on Lord Vishnu.

## Krishna’s lotus feet are the goal. The means of attaining them are varied according to the qualifications of the aspirant.

### Verse 8

*tomāre pāite śāstra upāya kahila*  
*adhikāra bhede tāhā nānā vidha haila \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures have described different means to attain You, but these differ according to the candidate’s level of qualification.**

**Комментарий:**

The scriptures prescribe three basic systems for attaining Krishna, karma, jnana and bhakti. Those who are the most immersed in mundane consciousness are best suited for the ritualistic method of karma. Those who are extremely attached to Maya are advised to take up the path of non-dual knowledge. Devotional service, however, is recommended for all living beings without distinction.

## The nature of karma. Other auspicious acts are all material, while the ultimate goal is spiritual.

### Verse 9

*jaḍa-buddhi-jana jaḍa-dravya-kālāśraye*  
*tomāra sādhana kare śamanera bhaye \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those with mundane intelligence are under the influence of time and matter; they practice religious life motivated by their fear of death.**

### Verse 10

*tumi ta abhaya prada advitīya hari*  
*tomāra caraṇa mātra bhavārṇave tarī \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No one compares to You, O Lord, as the deliverer from fear; Your lotus feet are the only boat that can take us across this ocean.**

### Verse 11

*sei pada lābhe yata upāya sūjila*  
*jaḍa-bhāvāśraye saba jaḍīya ha-ila \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You have created so many ways of attaining those lotus feet, but when done in mundane consciousness, they too are mundane.**

### Verse 12

*iṣṭa-pūrta āra yajñādika puṇya karma*  
*snāna, homa, dāna, yoga, varṇāśrama dharma \|\|*

### Verse 13

*tīrtha-yātrā, vrata, pitṛ-karma, dhyāna, jñāna*  
*daiva-karma, tapaḥ, prāyaścittādi-vidhāna \|\|*

### Verse 14

*sakala-i jaḍīya dravya kariyā āśraya*  
*upāya-svarūpe sadā śubha karma haya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pious acts like public works or fire sacrifices, ablutions, charity, yoga, or the practice of Varnashram, pilgrimage, fasting, offerings to forefathers, meditation, metaphysics, propitiation of the demigods, austerities and penances— all these means for attaining the Supreme are auspicious acts, even though they make use of worldly materials.**

### Verse 15

*upāya dhariyā pāya upeya carame*  
*anitya upāya chāḍe siddha-samāgame \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By following them, one eventually attains the Ultimate Goal, progressively giving up impermanent means once success has been achieved.**

## On attaining perfection in devotion, one naturally abandons mundane rituals, etc.

### Verse 16

*pūrṇānanda lābha haya sarva-siddhi-sāra*  
*jīvera upeya tāhā śuna sārātsāra \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The highest spiritual success manifests in the full experience of joy, and this is the ultimate goal. Now hear the secret of how to attain it.**

## Pious works are a means

### Verse 17

*jaḍa dravya kāla haya nirānanda-maya*  
*kauśale jīvera tāhe krama siddhi haya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Since material objects and time are devoid of transcendental joy, by gradually become expert, the jiva is elevated to perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

The evolution of the jiva from mundane religiosity to Krishna consciousness is the master plan of Providence, which impels the jiva to aspire for perfection. Conditioned souls cannot avoid being in contact with matter. Whatever they do or think is mixed up with the material nature. The “expertise” (*kauśala*) in works spoken of here comes about when one seeks devotion to Krishna, which is purely spiritual, within this material world.

### Verse 18

*ataeva śubha karma sakala-i upāya*  
*upeya carama-siddhi prema-rūpe bhāya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, all these pious activities are meant for spiritual advancement, but their ultimate spiritual perfection is divine love for Krishna.**

## Through these means, the goal is only attained after much delay.

### Verse 19

*sarva śubha karma siddhi vilambe udaya*  
*upeya upāye vyavadhāna hetu haya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attaining the ultimate goal through auspicious works takes time because there is such a gulf of difference between the means and the end.**

**Комментарий:**

There is an especially great difference between the means and the end when the means consists of material activities and the end is divine love for Krishna.

## The Holy Name functions as a means while one is on the stage of practice

### Verse 20

*harināma e jagate dile kṛpā kari’*  
*siddhi-lābhe śiṣṭa jīva la-ibeka vari’ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Out of Your mercy, You have brought into this world the Holy Name, which the successful jiva continues to chant even after attaining perfection.**

### Verse 21

*upāya ha-ila nāma śāstrera sammata*  
*anya śubha karma madhye ha-ila gaṇita \|\|*

### Verse 22

*sarveśvara viṣṇu yena brahmā-śivāsane*  
*devatā lakṣaṇe gaṇya ha-ila tribhuvane \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This very Name is also the means, according to all the scriptures, and is thus sometimes counted as one among the auspicious activities in the same way that Lord Vishnu, the Supreme Lord of all creation, is often listed as one of the gods, as though on the same level as Shiva or Brahma.**

## The Holy Name is in pure being. Because of their offenses the Mayavadis conceive of the Name as just another pious activity.

### Verse 23

*nāmera svarūpa haya śuddha sattva maya*  
*jaḍa gandha śuddha nāme kabhu nāhi raya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is fully spiritual and transcendental to matter. It is never tainted by matter in the slightest.**

### Verse 24

*jaḍībhūta-jīva nāme jaḍa-bhāva-dāne*  
*anya śubha karma saha eka kari’ māne \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Due to contact with matter, the jiva attributes material qualities to the Name, thus thinking it equal to other pious acts.**

## The jiva is spiritual in his pure state, but due to ignorance thinks himself to be a product of the material energy.

### Verse 25

*māyāvāda ha-ite ei nāma aparādha*  
*yāhāra daurātmye sadā haya bhakti-vāda \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This offense to the Holy Name arises out of Mayavada, the corrupting influence of which destroys one’s devotional spirit.**

## Though the Holy Name is the means, it is also the end.

### Verse 26

*kṛṣṇa-nāma haya prabhu pūrṇānanda tattva*  
*upeya vā siddhi bali yāhāra mahattva \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s name is the Lord Himself, the repository of spiritual bliss, the object and perfection of spiritual life. That is its glory.**

### Verse 27

*upāya ha-iyā āvirbhūta dharātale*  
*upeya upāya aikya sarva śāstre bale \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This very Name has descended to this world to become the means; thus all scriptures say that the means and the end are, in this case, one.**

### Verse 28

*adhikāra-bhede yini upāya svarūpa*  
*tini-i upeya anye baḍa aparūpa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Depending on one’s level of advancement, the Name is either the means, or the wondrous end of spiritual perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

As long as the jiva has not attained delight in the Self, he takes the Name to be the means to reach that goal of supreme self-satisfaction.

## Auspicious works are the secondary means; chanting the Holy Name is primary

### Verse 29

*ataeva upāya dvividha guṇa dhāma*  
*gauṇopāya śubha karma mukhyopāya nāma \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are thus two ways of approaching the supreme goal, both auspicious, but pious works are secondary; chanting the Holy Name is primary.**

**Комментарий:**

Though the chanting of the Holy Name is one means to achieving spiritual perfection, it is the chief means. All other auspicious activities are always to be considered secondary. One able to understand this will perceive the distinction between the Holy Name and all other auspicious acts.

## The Holy Name is beyond the senses.

### Verse 30

*ataeva śāstre yata anya śubha karma*  
*nāma saha nahe ei sarva śāstra marma \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, all the auspicious activities prescribed in the scriptures are never equal to the Holy Name. This is what the scriptures intend.**

### Verse 31

*sarala hṛdaye yabe kṛṣṇa-nāma gāya*  
*atīndriya-sukha āsi cittake nācāya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the simple soul chants the Holy Name then transcendental joy enters the heart and makes it dance.**

### Verse 32

*sei sukha kṛṣṇa-nāma-svabhāva tatpara*  
*ātma-rati ātma-krīḍā nāhi yāra para \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This joy is an integral part of the essence of the Holy Name which is beyond mere delight in the soul or the play of the soul.**

## The joys of sayujya or kaivalya are shadows of the blissful joy of pure devotion

### Verse 33

*brahma jñāne yoge ye ānanda vaibhava*  
*jaḍera viccheda sukha chāyā anubhava \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ecstatic joy that come as a result of knowledge of Brahman or yoga result from freeing oneself from matter, and are a shadow experience.**

### Verse 34

*abhedya kaivalya sukha svalpa bali’ jāni*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāmānanda-sukha bhūmā bali’ māni \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I think that the joys of absolute isolation of the soul\[57\] are insignificant, whereas the ecstasies of chanting the Holy Name are great (*****bhūmā*****).**

## The differences between other auspicious works and the Holy Name

### Verse 35

*sādhana-kālete nāma upāya svarūpa*  
*siddhi-kāle upeya se ei aparūpa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While one is still in the stage of practice, the Holy Name is the means, but, when perfected, it is wondrously realized as the goal of that practice.**

### Verse 36

*upāya svarūpa nāme upeyatva siddha*  
*anya śubha karme aiche nahe ta prasiddha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the Holy Name, even though itself the means, is established as the goal. The same thing cannot be said of other auspicious works.**

### Verse 37

*anya śubha karma yata saba jaḍāśrita*  
*nāma ta cinmaya sadā svataḥ siddhodita \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All other such pious deeds are under the auspices of matter, whereas the Holy Name is purely spiritual and self-manifested.**

### Verse 38

*sādhana kāle-o nāma śuddha sunirmala*  
*sādhakera anarthete dekhāya sa-mala \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even during in the sadhana stage, the Holy Name is pure and spotless; any seeming imperfections come from the sadhaka’s imperfections.**

### Verse 39

*sādhu-saṅge nāma laite jaḍa-buddhi yāya*  
*anartha niḥśeṣa haile śuddha nāma bhāya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting in the company of devotees, material consciousness dissipates, and with the extinction of the anarthas, the Pure Name manifests.**

### Verse 40

*anya śubha karmī kare tyajiyā upāya*  
*upeya parama bhāva carame āśraya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those engaged in other practices must ultimately give them up in order to take full shelter of the ultimate goal, the Supreme Truth.**

### Verse 41

*kintu nāmāśrayī jana nāma nāhi tyaje*  
*nāmera śuddhatā mātra siddhi-kāle bhaje \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who has taken shelter of the Holy Name, however, never gives it up, but simply experiences It in Its pure state on attaining perfection.**

### Verse 42

*anya śubha karma haite ati vilakṣaṇa*  
*nāmera svarūpa haya apūrva lakṣaṇa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is thus totally different from other auspicious acts; the Holy Name in its pure essence is completely unique.**

### Verse 43

*sādhana daśāya ei vilakṣaṇa jñāna*  
*guru kṛpā haite haya vedera pramāṇa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the grace of his spiritual master and from the Vedic evidence, the devotee should attains this understanding while still in the sadhana stage.**

**Комментарий:**

If one has faith, then one keeps the company of devotees. In their association, one begins practical devotional service, which results in the cleaning of the heart of all anarthas. The Holy Name manifests in its pure form to the degree that a devotee’s heart has been cleansed of anarthas through such practice. As such, his commitment to the chanting becomes stable and he comes to the stage of delight in the soul (*ātma-rati*). One must become aware of all these details of devotional life and have faith in the process on the stage of practice, by the grace of the spiritual master. Without it, one will simply go on committing offenses to the Holy Name and multiply rather than decrease his anarthas.

### Verse 44

*sādhana daśāya yini ei jñāna hīna*  
*nāma aparādhī tiṁha ati arvācīna \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who does not have this knowledge in the sadhana stage inevitably becomes an offender, for he is the most neophyte of neophytes.**

### Verse 45

*nāma sarvopari nāma-tulya kichu naya*  
*e dṛḍha viśvāsa kari yei nāma laya \|\|*

### Verse 46

*acire tāṅhāte haya śuddha-nāmodaya*  
*pūrṇānanda nāma-rasa karena āśraya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The Holy Name is above all; the Holy Name has no equal.” One who chants the Name with firm faith in these truths quickly experiences the advent of the Pure Name and plunges into the delights of full joy inherent in the Name.**

## The remedy for this Namaparadh

### Verse 47

*kāhāro yadyapi anya śubha karma sane*  
*nāme sama buddhi haya duṣkṛti bandhane \|\|*

### Verse 48

*se duṣkṛti kṣaya lāgi karibe yatana*  
*nāme śuddha buddhi pābe prema dhana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even if someone equates the Holy Name with auspicious works, as a result of being conditioned by past sinful activities, he should diligently rid himself of such sinful acts, for only then will he get a correct understanding of the Name and Krishna prema.**

**Комментарий:**

The sinful activities refered to here are offenses to the Vaishnavas. The consequence of such offenses is the tendency to prefer the Mayavadis’ interpretation of the Holy Name. In order to develop faith in the Holy Name, one must remain in the company of devotees and put an end to the sin of Vaishnava aparadh.

### Verse 49

*antyaja gṛhastha śuddha nāma parāyaṇa*  
*tāṅra pada dhūli dehe karibe mṛkṣaṇa \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He should approach a lower caste householder who is very dedicated to the Name, take the dust of his feet, and smear it over his body.**

**Комментарий:**

If a Vaishnava is dedicated to the chanting of the Pure Name, one should give up all consciousness of his caste, class or racial antecedents and smear the dust of his feet all over one’s own body in faith and devotion.

### Verse 50

*khāibe adharāmṛta pibe pada-jala*  
*tabe śuddha nāme mati ha-ibe nirmala \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He should eat the remnants of his food and drink water that has washed his feet, then his mind will be sanctified and able to chant the Pure Name.**

### Verse 51

*kāli dāse ei rūpe duṣkṛti khaṇḍana*  
*punaḥ tava kṛpā-prāpti gāya jagaj-jana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kali Das released himself from his past offenses in this way and then attained Your direct blessings. All the world sings this truth.**

### Verse 52

*āmi jaḍa buddhi nātha eka mātra gāi*  
*nāma-cintāmaṇi-tattva kabhu nāhi pāi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, I am absorbed in mundane consciousness, all I can do is sing the glories of the Holy Name, though I can never truly understand them.**

**Комментарий:**

Srila Hari Das Thakur’s commitment to chanting the Holy Name

### Verse 53

*kṛpā kari’ nāma-rūpe āmāra jihvāya*  
*nirantara nāca prabhu dhari tava pāya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! Be merciful and appear on my tongue in Your holy name; dance there constantly, O Lord! I fall at Your lotus feet and beg You.**

### Verse 54

*rākha iṅhā lao tāṅhā tava icchā mata*  
*yāṅhā rākha deha more kṛṣṇa-nāmāmṛta \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You may keep me in this world or take me to Your abode, as You please, but whatever You do with me, feed me the divine nectar of Your names.**

**Комментарий:**

“Here” means in this material world, “there” means in the spiritual sky.

### Verse 55

*jagajjane nāma dite tava avatāra*  
*jagajjana-mājhe more kara aṅgīkāra \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You have descended into this world to distribute the Holy Name, so kindly also consider me one of those You intended to bless.**

### Verse 56

*āmi ta adhama tumi adhama tāraṇa*  
*ubhaye sambandha ei patita pāvana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am the most lowly, whereas You are committed to the uplift of the lowest; this is our eternal relationship, O Savior of the fallen!**

### Verse 57

*acchedya sambandha ei tomāya āmāya*  
*yāra bale nāmāmṛta e adhama cāya \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the strength of this unbreakable connection between us, O Lord, I beg You to shower me with the nectar of the Holy Name.**

## Why the Name is the religion of the age

### Verse 58

*kali-yuge suduḥsādhya anya śubha karma*  
*ataeva nāma āsi’ ha-ila yuga dharma \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the Kali Yuga, it is difficult to achieve success by other means, therefore the chanting of the Holy Name is the yuga dharma.**

**Комментарий:**

The Holy Name is the moste efficient religious practice in any age, but in the Age of Kali, no other practice is dependable. As such, the Holy Name has appeared specifically in this age to liberate the people of the world from their sufferings.

### Verse 59

*haridāsa-dāsa bhaktivinoda se jana*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi gāya akiñcana \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, who takes pleasure in serving the servant of Hari Das, though devoid of qualification, recites this** ***Harināma-cintāmaṇi*****.**

## 12 - Chanting Inattentively pramādaḥ

*jaya jaya mahāprabhu jaya bhakta-gaṇa \|*  
*yāṅdera prasāde kari nāma-saṅkīrtana \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Lord Gaurachandra,**  
**who has descended to propagate the Holy Name!**  
**All glories to the Holy Name,**  
**the essence of all spiritual teaching!**

## The offense known as pramada or inattentive chanting

*haridāsa bale prabhu hethā sanātane \|*  
*āra ta gopāla bhaṭṭa dakṣiṇa bhramaṇe \|\|2\|\|*  
*śikhāile apramāde śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajane \|*  
*pramādake aparādhe karile gaṇana \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das said, “O Lord, to Srila Sanatan Goswami here in Jagannath Puri and to Srila Gopala Bhatta Goswami while wandering in South India, You taught that one must worship the Lord without being distracted; You thus counted inattentiveness amongst the offenses in chanting.**

**Комментарий:**

Mahaprabhu instructed Sanatan Goswami: *eka aṅga sādhe keha sādhe bahu aṅga \| niṣṭhā haite upajaya premera taraṅga \|\|* “Whether a person executes only one or many of the processes of devotional service, the waves of love of Godhead will arise if he or she practices with fixed determination or *niṣṭhā*.” (CC 2.22.130) The nishtha spoken of here is achieved by giving up inattentiveness in chanting.

*anya aparādha tyaji sadā nāma laya \|*  
*tabu nāme prema nāhi haya ta udaya \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some people give up all the other offenses and chant constantly, but still do not experience the awakening of prema.**

*tabe jāni pramāda nāmete aparādha \|*  
*prema bhakti sādhanete kariteche bādha \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When I see this, I know that the offense of inattentiveness is interfering with their attainment of prema bhakti.**

## Inattentiveness is known as pramāda

*pramāda anavadhāna ei mūla artha \|*  
*ihā haite ghaṭe prabhu sakala anartha \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The root meaning of** ***pramāda*** **is inattentiveness; from it arise all the other anarthas.**

## Three kinds of inattention

*audāsīnya jāḍya āra vikṣepa e tina \|*  
*prakāra anavadhāna bujhibe pravīṇa \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wise should know that there are three kinds of inattention : indifference (*****audāsīnya*****), lethargy (*****jāḍya*****) and distractedness (*****vikṣepa).***

## Until one develops enthusiasm for chanting, one should be especially careful

*kona bhāgye kona jīvera śraddhā yadi haya \|*  
*tabe tiṅha harināma grahaṇa karaya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who by some good fortune develops faith takes up chanting the Holy Name.**

*yatna kari smare nāma saṅkhyāra sahita \|*  
*tabe nāme anurāga haya ta udita \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who carefully remembers the Holy Name, according to a fixed number will quickly develop an enthusiasm for chanting.**

**Комментарий:**

One should chant the Mahamantra keeping count on a rosary made of tulasi beads; one will feel when it is time to increase the number of rounds chanted.

*ye paryanta anurāga nā haya udaya \|*  
*se paryanta yatna kari nāma sadā laya \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As long as one does not have such natural enthusiasm, one should take care to constantly chant the Holy Name attentively.**

## One who is careless cannot fix his mind

*nisargataḥ loka saba viṣaye āsakta \|*  
*smṛti-kāle viṣaya antare anurakta \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All people are naturally conditioned to be attached to sense objects, so when remembering the Name, their minds are attracted to other objects.**

*ruci yāya anya sthāne nāme udāsīna \|*  
*nāme citta magna nahe jape pratidina \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They have a taste for something else, and are indifferent to the Holy Name; thus though chanting every day, their minds are not absorbed in their japa.**

*citta eka dike āra anya dike nāma \|*  
*tāhāra maṅgala kise haya guṇa dhāma \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The mind goes off one way, while the Name is elsewhere. How then can such a person benefit, O most virtuous Lord?**

*lakṣa nāma haile pūrṇa saṅkhyā mālā gaṇi \|*  
*hṛdaye nahila rasa bindu guṇa maṇi \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He may complete a lakh of Names on his japa mala, but, O Lord, not a drop of ecstatic taste touches his heart**

*ei ta anavadhāna doṣera prakāra \|*  
*viṣayī hṛdaye prabhu baḍa durnivāra \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the result of inattentiveness while chanting, O Lord. This flaw is difficult to eradicate from hearts attached to sense objects.**

## The rules for chanting carefully

*sādhu saṅge svalpa kāla chāḍiyā viṣaya \|*  
*nirjane la-ile nāma ei doṣa kṣaya \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up sense objects, spend some time in the company of Vaishnavas, one should chant the Name in a secluded place to eradicate the above flaw.**

**Комментарий:**

At first one should begin by keeping this rule for half an hour in such. By chanting or meditating in the company of advanced Vaishnavas one will see their mood and by emulating it, develop the desire to give up the indifference to the Holy Name.

*krame krame kṛṣṇa nāme citta haya sthira \|*  
*nirantara nāma rase haya ta adhīra \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gradually the Holy Name takes a permanent place in the chanter’s mind, until he becomes fully submerged in the sweetness of the Holy Name.**

*tulasīra sannikaṭe kṛṣṇa līlā sthāne \|*  
*sādhu sannidhāne basi’ sātvata-vidhāne \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vaishnava custom holds that chanting is best in the presence of Tulasi, in a place of Krishna’s pastimes, or in the company of saintly devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

The words used here for Vaishnava custom are *sātvata-vidhāna*, which means the system discovered by the Vaishnavas who have enjoyed worshiping the Lord. At first they chant just half an hour a day, then gradually double this time and then again. Eventually they increase to chanting a lakh of Names per day, eventually increasing to three lakhs a day. Such increases take place naturally as one develops a taste for chanting.

*krame kāla vṛddhi kari sei nāma smare \|*  
*ati śīghra viṣayera chanda ha-ite tare \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gradually, one increases the amount of time spent remembering the Name and then quickly breaks free from the inclination to sense objects.**

## Another way to overcome inattention arising from apathy

*athavā nirjane basi’ smari’ sādhu-rīti \|*  
*indriya pidhāna kari’ nāme kare mati \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Alternatively, one can sit alone and follow how saints do their bhajan. By keeping the senses covered, one can fix the mind on the Holy Name.**

**Комментарий:**

“Keeping the senses covered” can mean either chanting while sitting in an isolated room with the door locked, or with the eyes and head covered with a cloth.

*satvare nāmete niṣṭhā ruci krame haya \|*  
*audāsīnya doṣe tāra krame haya kṣaya \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This quickly leads to a determined commitment, then pleasure in the Name, whereby the offense of apathetic indifference will gradually vanish.**

## The characteristics of indifference arising from lethargy

*jāḍye ye anavadhāna alasera mane \|*  
*tāhe ruci nāhi haya śrī-nāma-grahaṇe \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The inattention arising from lethargy (*****jāḍya*****) refers to the laziness of mind that prevents one from relishing the nectar of the Holy Name.**

*smṛti-kāle punaḥ śīghra virāme prayāsa \|*  
*ei doṣe nāma-rasa nā haya prakāśa \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While remembering the Holy Name, a lazy person quickly gives up trying, and this lack of effort prevents him from getting a taste for the Holy Name.**

*anya kārye vṛthā kāla nā haya yāpana \|*  
*sādhu gaṇa ihā cinti’ smare anukṣaṇa \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saintly devotees are afraid to waste even a moment in other activities and so use every available minute in chanting and remembering.**

**Комментарий:**

This quality is called *avyartha-kālatvam*, which is a characteristic of the devotee who has attained the stage of *bhāva* (See *Bhajana-rahasya*S 1.3.14).

*nāma smare rase maje anya nāhi cāya \|*  
*sei rūpa sādhu saṅge ei dosa yāya \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Meditating on the Name and absorbed in its rasa, they want for nothing. The flaw of inattention disappears when one remains in such association.**

*anveṣiyā sei rūpa sādhu-saṅga kare \|*  
*tad-anukaraṇe citta jāḍya parihare \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Search out devotees of this quality and stay in their company, then emulate their character and give up lethargy.**

**Комментарий:**

It is very difficult to find a pure saintly devotee. One may have to travel far and wide to find suitable devotional association.

*avyartha kālatva dharma sādhura carita \|*  
*dekhile tāhāte ruci ha-ibe niścita \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is in the sadhu’s nature to not misuse even a moment’s time; when an aspirant sees such character, he is sure to be delighted by it.**

*mane habe āhā kabe ihāra samāna \|*  
*smariba gāiba nāma haye bhāgyavān \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He will think, “Ah! When can I be like this great soul? When will I be fortunate enough to chant and remember like he does?”**

*sei ta utsāha āsi alasera mane \|*  
*jāḍya dūra kare kṛṣṇa-nāmera smaraṇe \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Such thoughts bring enthusiasm into the mind of the lethargic aspirant, and inspire him to rid himself of laziness in remembering Krishna’s names.**

*mane habe āja lakṣa nāma ye kariba \|*  
*krame krame tina lakṣa nāma ye smariba \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He will thus begin to resolve, “Today I will chant a lakh of Names, and it won’t be long before I can meditate on three lakhs every day.”**

*mahāgraha habe citte nāmera saṅkhyāya \|*  
*acire yāibe jāḍya sādhura kṛpāya \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He will then have great enthusiasm for chanting his fixed number of Names, and thus, by the grace of the Vaishnavas, he will overcome his lethargy.**

## Overcoming scattered-mindedness (vikcepa)

*vikṣepa ha-ite yei pramāda udaya \|*  
*bahu yatne sei aparādha haya kṣaya \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The kind of inattention that results from scattered-mindedness is an offense that can only be overcome by making great efforts.**

*kanaka kāminī āra jaya parājaya \|*  
*pratiṣṭhāśā śāṭhya-vṛtti tāhāra nilaya \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Its sources lie in desire for money and sex, or from victories and defeats, from the desire for power and prestige, or from profits dishonestly gained.**

*e saba ākṛṣṭi hṛde ha-ile udaya \|*  
*nāmete anavadhāna svabhāvataḥ haya \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When thoughts arising from these sources pull on the mind, one naturally becomes inattentive to the Holy Name.**

## How to overcome such distractedness

*krame krame sei saba cintā parihāre \|*  
*yatibe saubhāgyavān vaiṣṇava ācāre \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One by one, the fortunate aspirant must abandon all these thoughts and make a concerted effort to take up the Vaishnava way of life.**

*prathamete hari-dine bhoga-cintā tyaji’ \|*  
*sādhu saṅge rātra-dina harināma bhaji \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**
