**He should begin by giving up thoughts of sense pleasure on holy days like Ekadasi, spending the entire day and night chanting the Holy Name in the company of devotees.**

*hari-kṣetre hari dāsa hari śāstre laye \|*  
*utsave majibe sukhe parama nirbhaye \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then, in a place associated with Krishna, with Krishna’s servants and Krishna’s scriptures, he should fearlessly immerse himself in the blissful Ekadasi celebrations.**

**Комментарий:**

“Places associated with Krishna” means Nabadwip, Vrindavan, Jagannath Puri, etc. “Krishna’s servants” refers to pure devotees in the line of Rupa Goswami. “Krishna’s scriptures” are the Upanishads, the Gita, Bhagavatam and any other books discussing the Vaishnava siddhantas.

*krame bhakti-kāla mana karibe vardhana \|*  
*hari-kathā mahotsave majāiyā mana \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gradually, he will spend more and more time in acts of devotion, absorbing the mind in a festival of joy, discussing topics related to Krishna.**

*śreṣṭha rasa krame citte ha-ibe udaya \|*  
*jaḍera nikṛṣṭa rasa chāḍibe niścaya \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the superior taste of Krishna devotion will arise and the aspirant will give up the inferior taste of material pleasures.**

*mahājana mukhe hari-saṅgīta śravaṇe \|*  
*mugdha habe manaḥ karṇa rasa āsvādane \|\|40\|\|*  
*nikṛṣṭa viṣaya-spṛhā ha-ibe vigata \|*  
*nāma gāne citta sthira habe avirata \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On listening to the songs of Krishna written by the Mahajans, one’s ears and mind will become enchanted by the nectarean taste, the inferior tastes of material sense enjoyment will then be dislodged and the mind fixed in the singing of the Holy Name.**

*ata eva bahu yatne e pramāda tyaje \|*  
*sthira citte nāma rase cira dina maje \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one should take great care to give up the fault of inattention and absorb himself with singular fixity in the nectar of the Holy Name.**

## Eagerness

*saṅkalpita nāma saṅkhyā pūrṇa karibāre \|*  
*nā haya ayatna nāme dekhi bāre bāre \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I always take care not to neglect completing the number of Names to be chanted according to my vow. I see to this regularly, again and again.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who have a tendency to inattention due to scattered-mindedness generally attempt to complete their fixed number as quickly as possible. As one chants, one should repeatedly pay attention that such carelessness does not arise.

*sa-tarka ha-iyā kari nāma saṅkīrtana \|*  
*pramāda chāḍiyā kari nāmera bhajana \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I chant the Holy Names with great attention; free from the offense of inattentiveness, I engage in Nam bhajan.**

*saṅkhyādhika spṛhā ekāgra-mānase \|*  
*nirantara kari’ nāma tava kṛpā-bale \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the ambition to simply increase my numbers, with attention I constantly chant the Holy Name on the strength of Your mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

One should take care to improve the quality of his chanting rather than trying to increase the quantity.

*ei kṛpā kara prabhu nāmete pramāda \|*  
*nā bādhe āmāra citte nāma rasāsvāda \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Please be merciful to me, O Lord, that the offense of inattention in chanting never blocks my ability to relish the divine flavors of the Holy Name.**

## The secret to success

*ekāgra mānase nirjanete svalpa kṣaṇa \|*  
*nāma smṛti abhyāsa karibe bhakta jana \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee should spend a little time alone in a quiet place and practice concentrating deeply on the Holy Name.**

*ata eva spaṣṭa nāma bhāva lagna mane \|*  
*sadā haya e prārthanā tomāra caraṇe \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore I pray constantly at Your lotus feet to be able to always utter each Name distinctly and to experience emotion when chanting.**

*āpana yatnete keha nāhi pāre \|*  
*tomāra prasāda vinā e bhava-saṁsāre \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By one’s own efforts, no one in this world can overcome distractedness. Such a victory can only come by Your merciful blessings.**

**Комментарий:**

It is impossible for any jiva to single-handedly overcome inattentiveness by his own personal efforts. By Your mercy, however, O Lord, it can be accomplished with ease. Therefore it is absolutely necessary to beg for Your grace with great humility, for this is the only means of gaining release from this offense.

## The necessity for eagerness and attention; sincere effort in chanting is the only way to avoid this offense.

*yatna kari kṛpā māgi vyākula antare \|*  
*tumi kṛpāmaya kṛpā kara ataḥpare \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I take great care to beg for Your mercy, my heart overcome with emotion, knowing that You are all-merciful and will always be generous with Your blessings.**

*tava kṛpā lābhe yadi nā kari yatana \|*  
*tabe āmi bhāgya hīna he śacīnandana \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If I make no effort to attain Your mercy, then I will remain ever unfortunate, O Sachinandan!**

**Комментарий:**

Those who attempt to take up chanting the Holy Name on the strength of their own individual intelligence and material wealth will never attain success. Lord Krishna’s mercy is the basis of all perfection. Thus one who does not make every effort to obtain the Lord’s mercy is indeed a very unfortunate soul.

To conclude this chapter, it is worth remembering Mahaprabhu’s instructions to all humanity about chanting and meditating on the Holy Name with full attention. These are found in the *Caitanya-bhāgavata*, Madhya 23.75-78:

*āpane sabāre prabhu kare upadeśe*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma mahā-mantra śunaha hariṣe*  
*hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare*  
*hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare*  
*prabhu kahe kahilām ei mahā-mantra*  
*ihā giye japa sadā kariyā nirbandha*  
*ihā haite sarva-siddhi haibe sabāra*  
*sarva-kṣaṇa bala ithe vidhi nāhi āra*

The Lord spoke the following instruction to all His students: “Listen joyfully to the great mantra composed of Krishna’s names:

***hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare***  
***hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare***

The Lord continued, “I have spoken the Maha Mantra. Now go ahead and chant it, being sure to take a vow to repeat it a fixed number of times every day. All perfections will come to you from chanting the Holy Name. Chant always; there is no other rule to follow. (Cbh 2.23.75-78)

The word *nirbandha* in verse 77 needs elaboration. Though the word itself has various meanings, in this context, it refers to the contract one makes to chant a specific number of Maha Mantras per day. The sadhaka should chant the Maha Mantra of sixteen names and thirty-two syllables once on each bead of a japa mala of 108 tulasi beads. Four rounds on the beads is called a *grantha*. By gradually increasing his chanting, the sadhaka should aspire to fix his chanting at sixteen *granthas* or one lakh of Holy Names. If chanting is then even further increased to three lakhs of names, the devotee will be immersed in the Lord’s Holy Name for every moment of the day. All previous acharyas and Mahajans followed this instruction of Lord Chaitanya and attained perfection. It is still possible today for anyone to attain perfection by chanting in this way. Everyone from the liberated soul to the salvationist and the materialistic person are eligible to chant the Holy Name of the Lord. Different moods of Krishna-prema are visible in the liberated souls, both that of meeting the Lord and that of suffering the pangs of separation from the Lord. In either of these moods, the nectar of the Holy Name is fully relishable.

*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi alaṅkāra yāra \|*  
*hari-dāsa-pada-yuga bharasā tāhāra \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, who takes pleasure in serving the servant of Hari Das, though devoid of qualification, recites this** ***Harināma-cintāmaṇi*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the twelfth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “Chanting Inattentively.”

## 13 - Remaining attached to “I and Mine”

*śrute’pi nāma māhātmye*  
*yaḥ prīti rahito naraḥ \|*  
*ahaṁ mamādi paramo*  
*nāmni so’py aparādha kṛt \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who has heard the glories of the Lord’s holy name but develops no appreciation for it and continues to be absorbed in thinking of himself and his possessions is also an offender to the Holy Name.**

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā jīvana*  
*sītādvaita jaya jaya gaura-bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to Sita Devi and Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to all of Gauranga’s devotees!**

*preme gada gada haridāsa mahāśaya \|*  
*śeṣa nāma aparādha prabhu pade kaya \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Stammering with ecstatic love, Hari Das went on to explain to the Lord the last of the offenses to the Holy Name:**

*śuna prabhu ei aparādha sarvādhama \|*  
*ei doṣe nāma prema nā haya udgama \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This Namaparadh is the last and worst of them all, for its presence stops Krishna prema from developing.**

**Комментарий:**

Even though they may be initiated, most materially attached persons fall down from the spiritual path because they continue to identify with the material body and remain attached to their possessions. Those who are fixed in bodily attachments, who think “I am a Brahmin, a Vaishnava or a king,” who are possessive towards house and home, children and grandchildren, or wealth and followers, never even truly begin the spiritual path. One can only free oneself of this terrible offense by taking shelter of the Holy Name according to the six-fold process of surrender.

## Surrender is absolutely necessary in chanting the Holy Name

*anya naya aparādha kariyā varjana \|*  
*nāmete śaraṇāpanna ha-ibe sajjana \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After giving up the other nine offenses, the aspirant should surrender fully to Holy Name.**

*ṣaḍ-vidha śaraṇāgati sarva śāstre kaya \|*  
*vistārita balite āmāra sādhya naya \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the scriptures discuss the six kinds of surrender, of which it is impossible for me to speak in great detail.**

## The process of surrender

*saṁkṣepe caraṇe tava kari nivedana \|*  
*ānukūlye saṅkalpa prātikūlya visarjana \|\|6\|\|*  
*kṛṣṇe rakṣā-kārī buddhi pālaka bhāvana \|*  
*nije dīna buddhi āra ātma-nivedana \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will describe the six-fold process of surrender to you in brief: accepting all that is favorable to devotion, rejecting what is unfavorable, depending on Krishna alone for protection and maintainence, feeling humble about one’s own condition, and fully surrendering oneself.**

**Комментарий:**

*Ānukūlye saṅkalpa,* or the determination to accept conditions favorable to devotional service, means to make the promise that in the course of one’s daily in activities, one will only do things that are conducive to devotion, and nothing more. The opposite, i.e., swearing off any activities that are unfavorable to devotional service, is known as *prātikūlya-vivarjanam.*

*e jīvana nā rahile nā haya bhajana \|*  
*jīvana rakṣāya mātra viṣaya grahaṇa \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If I do not stay alive, how will I be able to engage in serving the Lord? Even so, I will accept only as much as I need to keep body and soul together.**

*bhakti anukūla ye viṣaya anukṣaṇa \|*  
*tāhe rocamāna vṛttye jīvana yāpana \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting whatever sense objects are in harmony with the goal of devotion is the proper way to lead one’s life and is pleasing to Krishna.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse describes the *rocamānā vṛtti*, or an agreeable way of living one’s life. It means an attitude that is favorable to attaining pleasure in connection with Krishna.

*bhakti pratikūla ye viṣaye yabe haya \|*  
*tāhāte aruci tāhā varjibe niścaya \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If ever the same sense objects become detrimental to devotional culture, and are thus unpleasurable, they should be abandoned without hesitation.**

*kṛṣṇa vinā rakṣā-kartā nāhi keha āra \|*  
*kṛṣṇa se pālaka mātra jānibe āmāra \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is no one other than Krishna who is truly able to protect me, and I know that He alone and no one else maintains me.**

*āmi dīna akiñcana sakalera chāra \|*  
*adhama durgata kichu nāhika āmāra \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am the most fallen and useless living being in creation; and as the lowest and most unfortunate creature, I possess nothing.**

*kṛṣṇera saṁsāre āmi āchi cira dāsa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa icchā mata kriyā āmāra prayāsa \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am living my life within this world as Krishna’s eternal servant and I make every effort to act only in accordance with His desire.**

*āmi kartā āmi dātā āmi pālayitā \|*  
*āmāra e deha geha santāna vanitā \|\|14\|\|*  
*āmi vipra āmi śūdra āmi pitā pati \|*  
*āmi rājā āmi prajā santānera gati \|\|15\|\|*  
*ei saba buddhi chāḍi kṛṣṇe kari mati \|*  
*kṛṣṇa kartā kṛṣṇa icchā mātra balavatī \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus any idea that I am the doer, the giver or the protector, or that this body, home, wife and children are mine, that I am a Brahmin, a Shudra, father or husband, that I am a king, or a subject, or the refuge of my offspring, is all to be given up and Krishna made the one and only center of my life. Krishna is the real master and His will alone is imperative.**

*kṛṣṇera ye haya icchā tāhāi kariba \|*  
*nija icchā anusāre kichu nā cintiba \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am ready to act only to fulfill Lord Krishna’s desire and will not even think about acting according to my own whims.**

*kṛṣṇa icchā mate haya āmāra saṁsāra \|*  
*kṛṣṇa icchā mate āmi ha-i bhava-pāra \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By Krishna’s desire I am living a worldly life, and if He so wishes, I will cross the material ocean.**

*duḥkhe thāki sukhe thāki āmi kṛṣṇa dāsa \|*  
*kṛṣṇecchāya sarva jīve dayāra prakāśa \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In times of both joy and sorrow, I shall always remain Krishna’s servant and because it is His desire, I will show compassion all creatures.**

*mama bhoga karma-bhoga kṛṣṇa icchā mata \|*  
*āmāra vairāgya kṛṣṇa icchā anugata \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I suffer or enjoy the results of my past actions according to Krishna’s will, and if I become indifferent to the world, that too will be when He desires.**

**Комментарий:**

Even one’s suffering and enjoying past good and bad deeds, as well as the ultimate indifference to this world and renunciation all take place according to Krishna’s will.

## Full self-surrender only takes place after one has taken shelter of Krishna

*sarala bhāvete yabe ei bhāva haya \|*  
*ātma nivedana tāre bali mahāśaya \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one sincerely adopts the attitude I have just described, then that is called full self surrender or** ***ātma-nivedana*****.**

## What happens to one who chants without taking shelter

*ṣaḍ-vidha śaraṇāgati nāhika yāhāra \|*  
*se adhama ahaṁ mama buddhi doṣe chāra \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One unable to accept the six limbs of surrender is a prisoner of false ego who always thinks in terms of “me” and “mine.”**

*se bale āmi ta kartā saṁsāra āmāra \|*  
*nija karma phala bhoga sukha duḥkha āra \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He declares, “I am the master here. This wife and family are mine.I enjoy the fruits of my work, whether they are bitter or sweet.**

*āmāra rakṣaka āmi, āmi ta pālaka \|*  
*āmāra vanitā bhrātā bālikā bālaka \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I am my own protector and I can take care of my needs and those of my wife, brothers, sons and daughters.**

*āmi ta arjana kari āmāra ceṣṭāya \|*  
*sarva kāryya siddha haya sarva śobhā pāya \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I earn my living by the sweat of my own brow and deserve credit for the success I have achieved by my endeavor.”**

*ahaṁ mama buddhi krame bahirmukha jana \|*  
*nija jñāna bale bahu karaye mānana \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the egotism that intoxicates one who has turned his back on Krishna, and who consistently overestimates his own abilities and intelligence.**

*sei jñāna bale śilpa vijñāna vistāre \|*  
*īśvarera īśitā nā māne duṣṭācāre \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the strength of their knowledge, they develop science and technology, but deny that all is in the hands of God, and so commit wicked deeds.**

**Комментарий:**

Non-devotees think that by the force of their own intelligence they can make progress through science and industry and thereby increase their own happiness. In fact, everything takes place by Krishna’s desire, but such people never consider this possibility.

*śrī-nāma-māhātmya śuni viśvāsa nā kare \|*  
*loka vyavahāre kabhu kṛṣṇa-nāmoccāre \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though they may hear the glories of the Name, they do not believe in it.**

**Комментарий:**

They may take Krishna’s name, but do so purely out of social considerations.

*kṛṣṇa nāma kare tabu nāhi pāya prīti \|*  
*dharma dhvajī śaṭha jana jīvane e rīti \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They chant, but experience no love for Krishna; such are the ways of cheats who wrap themselves in the flag of religion.**

*helāya uccāre nāma kichu puṇya haya \|*  
*prīti phala nāhi phale sarva śāstre kaya \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants the Holy Name carelessly may still make spiritual gains, but all scriptures agree that he can never attain love for Krishna.**

## What is the root of this offense?

*māyā baddha haite ei aparādha haya \|*  
*ihāte niṣkṛti lābha kaṭhina niścaya \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The soul caught in the bonds of illusion commits this offense, which is certainly most difficult to overcome.**

*śuddha bhakti phale yāṅra virakti ha-ila \|*  
*saṁsāra chāḍiyā sei nāmāśraya nila \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who attains dispassion as a result of pure devotional practice may give up household life and take full shelter of the Holy Name.**

## How to become free from this offense

*niṣkiñcana bhāve bhaje śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇa \|*  
*viṣaya chāḍiyā kare nāma saṅkīrtana \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Living without possessions, the renouncer worships Krishna’s lotus feet; giving up all sense objects he chants the Holy Name constantly.**

*sei sādhu jane anveṣiyā tāṅra saṅga \|*  
*karibe sevibe chāḍi viṣaya taraṅga \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He searches out holy persons and associates with them; he serves them, free from the turmoil of a life of sense gratification.**

*krame krame nāme mati ha-ibe sañcāra \|*  
*ahaṁtā mamatā yābe māyā habe pāra \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gradually, the renunciate’s attraction for the Holy Name builds; his egotism and possessiveness dissipate and he becomes free of Maya.**

*nāmera māhātmya śuni ahaṁ mama bhāva \|*  
*chāḍiyā śaraṇāgati bhaktera svabhāva \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On hearing the glories of the Holy Name, it is natural for a devotee to give up all false ego and possessiveness, and to take to sharanagati.**

*nāmera śaraṇāgata yei mahājana \|*  
*kṛṣṇa nāma kare pāya prema mahā dhana \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great soul who has taken shelter of the Holy Names and chants them constantly attains the wonderful treasure of prema.**

## The characteristics of a person free from the ten Namaparadhs

*ata eva sādhu nindā yatane chāḍiyā \|*  
*para tattva viṣṇu śuddha manete jāniyā \|\|38\|\|*  
*nāma guru nāma śāstra sarvottama jāni \|*  
*viśuddha cinmaya nāma hṛdayete māni \|\|39\|\|*  
*pāpa spṛhā pāpa bīja tyajiyā yatane \|*  
*pracāriyā śuddha nāma śraddhānvita jane \|\|40\|\|*  
*anya śubha karma haite la-iyā virāma \|*  
*smare ye śaraṇāgata apramāde nāma \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who diligently gives up all blasphemy of Vaishnava sadhus, who adopts the pure understanding that Vishnu is the supreme truth, who accepts that the topmost guru and scriptures are those that preach the Name, who believes in his heart that the Holy Name is completely pure and spiritual, who attentively discards all sinful motivation, the seed of sin, who preaches the pure Holy Names only to those who possess faith, who entirely gives up all other pious activities, who meditates on the Holy Name without being distracted, taking full shelter...**

**Комментарий:**

It is not enough to simply give up committing the ten offenses. Each one of these offenses implies a particular positive injunction. The prohibitions given in the teachings of the ten aparadhs are a way of instructing us in these to positively implement the specific saintly qualities that render these aparadhs impossible. These verses summarize these positive injunctions.

## Bhava is attained quickly when one chants without offenses

*sei dhanya tri-jagate sei bhāgyavān \|*  
*kṛṣṇa kṛpā yogya sei guṇera nidāna \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**...is the most fortunate soul in the three worlds, the most blessed, the reservoir of all virtue and most worthy of receiving Krishna’s mercy.**

*ati alpa dine tāṅra śrī-nāma-granaṇe \|*  
*bhāvodaya haya āra pāya prema dhane \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By chanting in this fashion, such a devotee experiences the awakening of feeling for Krishna, which leads to love, all in a very short time.**

## The path of progress

*evam-bhūta janera sādhana daśā prāya \|*  
*ati svalpa dine yāya kṛṣṇera icchāya \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Krishna wishes, such a devotee quickly transcends any remnance of being merely on the stage of spiritual practice.**

*bhāva daśā haite haite prema daśā haya \|*  
*prema daśā sarva siddhi sarva śāstra kaya \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Passing the stage of spiritual emotion, he attains the stage of ecstatic love, at which point he attains the highest perfection, so say all the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

If one follows Hari Das Thakur’s instructions, one quickly transcends the stage of practice.

*tumi baliyācha nāma yei mahājana \|*  
*la-ibe niraparādhe pābe prema dhana \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, You have Yourself stated that anyone who chants without offenses is a great personality who attains the treasure of love for Krishna.**

## The injunction expressed negatively

*aparādha nāhi chāḍi’ nāma yadi laya \|*  
*sahasra sādhane tāra bhakti nāhi haya \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the other hand, one who continues chanting without giving up offenses may take up a thousand different practices without attaining true devotion.**

*jñāne mukti karme bhukti jñānī karmī jane \|*  
*sudurlabhā kṛṣṇa bhakti nirmala sādhane \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The karmi attains worldly happiness through works, the jnani liberation through knowledge, while the rare prize of Krishna devotion is attained only through impeccable practice.**

*bhukti mukti śukti sama bhakti muktā phala \|*  
*jīvera mahimā bhakti prāpti sunirmala \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Enjoyment and liberation are like a pearl shell,bhakti is the pearl. The glory of the jiva comes on attaining the most holy goal of devotion.**

*sādhane naipuṇya yoge atyalpa sādhane \|*  
*bhakti latā prema phala dena bhakta jane \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Devotees who practice devotion with expertise, need only apply a little effort before seeing the fruits of Krishna prema grow on their devotional creeper.**

## Expertise in bhajan

*daśa aparādha chāḍi nāmera grahaṇa \|*  
*ihāi naipuṇya haya sādhana bhajana \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting the Holy Name without committing the ten offenses is called expertise in devotional practice and worship of the Holy Name.**

## The gravity of offenses to the Holy Name

*ata eva bhakti lābhe yadi lobha haya \|*  
*daśa aparādha chāḍi kari nāmāśraya \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, because I have some yearning to attain pure devotion, I vow to give up the ten offenses and take full shelter of the Holy Name.**

*eka eka aparādha satarka ha-iyā \|*  
*yatanete chāḍi citte vilāpa kariyā \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Remaining ever vigilant against each of the ten offenses, I carefully expunge them from my mind, praying earnestly.**

*nāmera caraṇe kari dṛḍha nivedana \|*  
*nāma kṛpā hale aparādha vidhvaṁsana \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I make this sincere request at the lotus feet of the Holy Name, for if the Holy Name is merciful, all the aparadhs will be destroyed.**

*anya śubha karme nāma aparādha kṣaya \|*  
*kona prāyaścitta yogekabhu nāhi haya \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No other auspicious activity can possibly obliterate these offenses; no other penance or discipline can achieve this end.**

## How to stop Namaparadh

*aviśrānta nāme nāma aparādha yāya \|*  
*tāhe aparādha kabhu sthāna nāhi pāya \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Only tireless chanting dissolves past offenses to the Holy Name, and only such chanting prevents them from reappearing again.**

**Комментарий:**

Tireless chanting means that at any time other than that used for eating and sleeping, or other activities needed for bodily maintenance, one should chant with feeling (*kākuti* – “intense contrition”). This will lead to the destruction of the ten offenses. No other auspicious activity or penance is effective in this regard.

*divā-rātra nāma laya anutāpa kare \|*  
*tabe aparādha yāya nāma phala dhare \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should chant day and night with remorse for past offenses; this will assure their effects will be destroyed and the Name will take effect.**

*aparādha gate śuddha nāmera udaya \|*  
*śuddha nāma bhāva maya āra prema maya \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With the elimination of the offenses, the pure Name rises in the heart. The Pure Name is full of loving emotion and then full ecstatic love.**

*daśa aparādha yena hṛdaye nā paśe \|*  
*kṛpā kara mahāprabhu maji nāma rase \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, I humbly pray that the ten offenses never touch my heart. Please grant that I remain submerged in the pleasure of the Holy Name.**

*e bhaktivinoda haridāsa kṛpā bale \|*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi gāya kutūhale \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By Hari Das Thakur’s mercy, Bhaktivinoda, joyfully recites this** ***Harināma-cintāmaṇi*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the thirteenth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “Remaining attached to’I and Mine’”

## 14 - Offenses in serving the Deity

*jaya gaura-gadādhara jāhnavā jīvana*  
*jaya sītāpati śrīvāsādi-bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu, the life of Jahnava Devi!**  
**All glories to the husband of Sita, Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories Srivas Pandit and all the devotees!**

## Mahaprabhu names Hari Das Thakur the acharya of doctrines related to the Holy Name

*mahāprabhu bale śuna bhakta haridāsa \|*  
*nāma aparādha tattva karile prakāśa \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Chaitanya said, “My dear devotee Hari Das, you have clarified the matter of offenses to the Holy Name to Me.**

*ihāte kalira jīva labhibe maṅgala \|*  
*nāma-tattve tumi hao ācārya prabala \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Your explanations will greatly benefit the conditioned souls of Kali. In this matter, you are indeed a great spiritual master and acharya.**

**Комментарий:**

In Mahaprabhu’s incarnation, Srila Hari Das Thakur is the Namacharya, or foremost spiritual authority on chanting the holy names of Krishna. He practiced everything he preached. He not only taught the jivas the glories of Krishna’s name and how to distinguish between the Pure Name, the Semblance of the Name, and offensive chanting of the Holy Name, but did so by his own example.

*tava mukhe nāma-tattva karite śravaṇa \|*  
*āmāra ullāsa baḍa śuna mahājana \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You are a great authority and so it has given me great delight, to hear from you about the Holy Name; I much relished it. Now hear this:**

*ācāre ācārya tumi pracāre paṇḍita \|*  
*tomāra carita nāma-ratne vibhūṣita \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You are exemplary in your conduct and, in your preaching, profound; Your personality is bedecked with the priceless gems of the Lord’s names.**

*rāmānanda śikhāila more rasa-tattva \|*  
*tumi śikhāile more nāmera mahattva \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Ramananda Raya taught Me the sublime science of transcendental mellows, and now you have revealed to Me the extent of the Holy Name’s glories.**

*ebe bala sevā aparādha ki prakāra \|*  
*śuniyā ghucibe jīvera citta andhakāra \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Now please tell me something about the offenses made in serving the Deity, which will be of great benefit in eradicating the jivas’ ignorance.”**

*haridāsa bale se sevaka jana jāne \|*  
*mi nāmāśraye thāki jāniba kemane \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das Thakur replied: This is a subject known to those who serve Deities; I spend all my time under the shelter of the Name. How can I speak of it?**

*tabu tava ājñā āmi laṅghibāre nāri \|*  
*yāhā balāibe tāhā baliba vistāri \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even so, You have given me an order, and I cannot disregard it. I will speak, but whatever I say will be only what You make me say.**

## Varieties of sevaparadh

*sevā aparādha haya ananta prakāra \|*  
*śrī-mūrti sambandhe saba śāstrera vicāra \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are innumerable** ***sevā aparādhas*****, offenses arising in serving the Deity; These have been described in all scriptures related to Deity worship.**

*kona śāstre dvātriṁśat aparādha gaṇi \|*  
*kona śāstre pañcāśat gañe guṇa-maṇi \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some of these texts name thirty-two such offenses, whereas others, O Virtuous Lord, name fifty.**

## The fourfold classification of sevaparadh

*sei aparādha caturvidhādi prakāre \|*  
*vibhāga karena budha gaṇa śāstre dvāre \|\|12\|\|*  
*śrī-mūrti-sevaka niṣṭha kataguli tāra \|*  
*śrī mūrti sthāpaka niṣṭha aparādha āra \|\|13\|\|*  
*śrī-mūrti darśaka niṣṭha āra katipaya \|*  
*sarva niṣṭha aparādha katividha haya \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Learned devotees identify four categories of sevaparadh, in accordance with the scriptural injunctions, namely:**  
**(1) those committed by persons directly engaged in Deity worship;**  
**(2) those committed in the course of installing the Deities;**  
**(3) those committed by persons come to the temple for darshan; and**  
**(4) those that generally apply in all cases.**

**Комментарий:**

The sevaparadhs, by definition, are always related to Deity worship. Some of these offenses are committed by people engaged in serving the Deity, others are committed by those who install the Deity. People who go to see the Deity in the temple are prone to committing other offenses, while other offenses are committed by people in general. This distinction is easy to comprehend.

## The thirty-two offenses to the Deity

*pādukā sahita yāya īśvara mandire \|*  
*yāne caḍi’ yāya tathā svacchanda śarīre \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Entering the Lord’s temple with one’s shoes on, or going there in a vehicle when one is physically capable;**

*utsave nā seve āra pragati nā kare \|*  
*ucchiṣṭha aśauca dehe vandana ācare \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**not observing the festival days; not bowing to the Deity; offering worship to the Deity in an unclean or contaminated state;**

*eka haste praṇāma sammukhe pradakṣiṇa \|*  
*devāgre prasāre pada haya vīrāsīna \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**offering obeisances on one hand; circumambulating before the Deity; sitting in front of the Deity with one’s feet spread or the soles showing;**

*devāgre śayana āra bhakṣaṇa karaya \|*  
*mithyā kathā ucca bhāṣā jalpanā nicaya \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**sleeping or reclining in the presence of the Deity; eating before the Lord; speaking lies, talking loudly or gossiping with others before the Deity;**

*nigrahānugraha yuddha abhakti rodana \|*  
*krūra bhāṣā para nindā kambalāvaraṇa \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**punishing or blessing others; fighting; non-devotional crying aloud; saying cruel things; criticizing others; covering oneself with a blanket;**

*para stuti, aślīlatā, vāyu-vimokṣaṇa \|*  
*śakti sattve gauṇa upacārera yojana \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**praising others; using obscene language or passing air before the Deity; making substandard offerings to the Lord despite having better means;**

*devānivedita dravya bhakṣaṇe svīkāra \|*  
*kālodita phalādira anarpaṇa āra \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**eating or drinking food that has not been offered to the Deity; not offering fruits and vegetables as they come into season;**

**Комментарий:**

It is an offense for anyone to eat food or drink anything that has not been offered to the Deity.

*anya bhukta avaśiṣṭa khādya nivedana \|*  
*deva prati pṛṣṭha kari sammukhe āsana \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**offering the Deity contaminated food or food first tasted by someone else; sitting with one’s back to the Lord’s deity form;**

**Комментарий:**

It is also an offense to offer the Deity anything that has already been consumed in part by someone else, human or animal.

*devāgre anyera abhivādana pūjana \|*  
*guru prati mauna nija stotra ālocana \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**greeting or worshiping someone else before the Deity; being silent to the spiritual master or glorifying oneself before the the Deity;**

**Комментарий:**

One should not greet anyone before the Deity, with the exception of the spiritual master.

*devatā nindana ei dvātriṁśa prakāra \|*  
*sevā aparādha mahā purāṇe pracāra \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**slandering the gods before the Deity. These are the thirty-two kinds of offenses committed while serving the Lord, according to the Puranas.**

## Other offenses listed by different scriptures

*anyatra āchaye aparādha anyatama \|*  
*saṁkṣepe baliba prabhu tava icchā mata \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Other scriptural sources have listed additional offenses: I will recount them in brief, as it pleases You, O Lord.**

*rājānna bhojana āra andhakāra ghare \|*  
*praveśiyā deva mūrti saṁsparśana kare \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is offensive to eat the food of materialistic persons like kings, or to enter the temple and touch the deity of Hari in the darkness.**

*avidhi pūrvaka hari mūrti saṁsparśaṇa \|*  
*vinā vādye mandirera dvāra udghāṭana \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to approach the Lord’s deity form without the proper rituals, such as opening the altar doors without ringing an instrument like a bell;**

*sārameya dṛṣṭa khādya deve samarpaṇa \|*  
*arcana samaye mauna bhaṅga akāraṇe \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to offer food to the Lord that has been seen by a dog; to break the silence for no good reason during Deity worship;**

*bahirdeśe gamanādi pūjāra samaye \|*  
*gandha mālya nāhi diyā dhūpana karaye \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to leave the altar during Deity worship in order to go to the toilet; to offer incense without also offering perfumes and garlands to the Deities;**

*anarha-puṣpete kṛṣṇa pūjādi karaṇa \|*  
*adhauta vadane kṛṣṇa pūjā ārambhana \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to worship Lord Krishna with unofferable flowers; to begin worshiping Lord Krishna without having cleaned one’s teeth,**

*strī saṅga kariyā kimbā rajaḥsvalā nārī \|*  
*dīpa, śaba sparśiyā, ayogya vastra pari’ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**after having sexual relations, or after touching a woman during her menses, after touching a flame, or a dead body, or while wearing improper clothing,**

*śaba heri’, adhovāyu kariyā mokṣaṇa \|*  
*krodha kari’ śmaśānete kariyā gamana \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**or after seeing a dead body. One should not pass air while worshiping, nor worship while angry, or after going to a cremation ground,**

*ajīrṇa udare āra kusumbha paināka \|*  
*sevana kariyā āra tāmbula guvāka \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**before one’s meal has been fully digested,after eating certain foods, such as safflower or asafoetida,or while chewing betel and pan.**

*taila mākhi kari hari-śrī-mūrti-sparśana \|*  
*eraṇḍa-patrastha puṣpe karaya arcana \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is also an offense to touch the Deity after smearing oil on one’s body. to offer flowers that have been kept in an unclean pot;**

*āsurika kāle pūje pīṭhe bhūme basi \|*  
*snapana samaye mūrti vāma haste sparśi’ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to worship at a demoniac hour, or while sitting on a stool or the bare floor; to touch the Lord with the left hand while bathing Him;**

*vāsi vā yācita phule devatā arcana \|*  
*pūjā kāle garva ukti ayathā ṣṭhīvana \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to offer old or already offered fruits or flowers; to boast or spit at the time of worshiping the Lord;**

*tiryak puṇḍra dhare āra adhauta caraṇe \|*  
*mandire praveśa kare pūjāra kāraṇe \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to worship while wearing horizontal (i.e. three-lined) tilak; to enter the temple for worship without having washed one’s feet;**

*avaiṣṇava pakva kare deve nivedana \|*  
*avaiṣṇave dekhāiyā karaye pūjana \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to offer the Deity food cooked by a non-Vaishnava; to do puja while a non-Vaishnava is watching;**

*viśvaksene nā pūjiyā kāpāli dekhiyā \|*  
*hari pūje nakha jale śrī mūrti smariyā \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**to not worship Vishvaksenabefore puja; while seeing a Shiva worshiper; to bathe the Deity with water touched by the fingernails;**

*gharmāmbu-saṁspṛṣṭa jale karaye arcana \|*  
*kṛṣṇera śapatha kare nirmālya laṅghana \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**or with water into which sweat has fallen; to make oaths in Krishna’s name or to refuse the Deity’s prasad.**

*ei saba kāryye haya sevā aparādha \|*  
*sevākārī janera yāhāte bhakti bādha \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One incurs offenses in serving the Lord in all such acts, and as such they will interfere with the development of devotion.**

## One should take care to avoid the sevaparadhs to which one is prone

*śrī-mūrti sambandhe yāra bhajana pūjana \|*  
*sevā aparādha teṅha karun varjjana \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who engages in the worships of the Deity or chants His names should carefully avoid offenses incurred during His service.**

*vaiṣṇava sarvadā nāma sevā aparādha \|*  
*varjiyā śrī-kṛṣṇa-sevā karuna āsvāda \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A Vaishnava always avoids offenses both in chanting and in Deity worship, and by so doing, comes to taste the nectar of serving the Lord Krishna.**

*ei saba aparādha madhye yāṅra yāhā \|*  
*sambandhe paḍibe tāṅra varjjanīya tāhā \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Each devotee should be aware of which offenses he has a tendency to commit and take care to avoid them.**

## Offenses to the Holy Name must be avoided by every Vaishnava

*kintu nāma aparādha sakala vaiṣṇava \|*  
*sarva kāla tyaji’ labhe bhaktira vaibhava \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But, all Vaishnavas must avoid all the offenses to the Holy Name at all times; only then will they experience the glories of pure devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

All Vaishnavas should be on guard against the ten offenses to the Holy Name. The sevaparadhs are not as universally applicable because they only arise in certain specific situations. Nevertheless, all Vaishnavas should know about these offenses so that they may take care to avoid them also.

## Those on the path of bh va-sev \[91\] need not consider the sevaparadhs to the same degree

*śrī-mūrti virahe yini nirjjanete basi’ \|*  
*bhajana-kāraṇe bhāva mārge aharniśi \|\|46\|\|*  
*nāma aparādha sadā varjjanīya tāṅra \|*  
*nāma aparādha daśa sarva kleśādhāra \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A person who lives in a secluded place, in separation from the Deity, who day and night chants the Holy Name and serves the Lord mentally, must diligently avoid the ten offenses against the Holy Name, for these ten offenses to the Holy Name are the source of all suffering.**

*nāma aparādha gate bhāva sevā haya \|*  
*ata eva aparādha tāhe nāhi raya \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When these offenses have been eliminated, one can engage in** ***bhāva-sevā*****.  The other offenses do not occur in that stage.**

**Комментарий:**

On the *bhāva-mārga*, the primary activity is mental service to the Lord. In this kind of service, there is little occasion for committing the *sevāparādhas*. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu gave specific instructions to Raghunath Das Goswami about the service to the Govardhan Shila.

Lord Chaitanya said, “This stone is the transcendental form of Lord Krishna. Worship it with great enthusiasm. Worship this stone in the mode of pure goodness and you will quickly attain ecstatic love for Krishna. All you need are a jug of water and a few Tulasi flowers. When performed in complete purity, this is called worship in the mode of goodness. The Tulasi manjaris should be small and soft and have two leaves still attached on either side. Offer eight such manjaris with faith.”

After instructing Raghunath in this way, the Lord placed the Govardhana Shila in his hand. Raghunath Das joyfully began to worship the Shila. Svarupa Damodar gave him two pieces of cloth, each about six inches long, a small wooden platform, and a jug in which to bring water; Raghunath Das used these articles to worship the Govardhan Shila, which he saw as directly being Krishna, the son of Nanda Maharaj. The amount of bliss that he enjoyed simply by offering water and Tulasi to the Lord in this way could not be achieved even by worshiping the Deity with the sixteen kinds of offering. Even so, Svarupa Damodar one day said to Raghunath Das: “Offer \[your Govardhan Shila\] eight cowries worth of *khājā* and *sandeśa* sweets.” (Antya 6.294-300, 302-304)

## Bhava-seva is the duty of those who meditate on the Holy Name

*śrī nāma smaraṇe bhāva sevāra udaya \|*  
*tomāra kṛpāya prabhu jīve bhāgyodaya \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As a devotee meditates on the Name, His desire for mental service arises. O Lord, Your mercy brings such good fortune to the individual living entity.**

*bhaktira sādhana yata āchaya prakāra \|*  
*se saba carame deya nāme prema sāra \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is a great variety in the manner of devotional practice, but all ultimately all lead to the essence of prema found in the Name.**

*ata eva nāma laya nāma rase maje \|*  
*anya ye prakāra saba tāhā nāhi bhaje \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should therefore chant the Holy Name, diving into Its sweet nectar, and not place exclusive faith in any other method of worshiping the Lord.**

*haridāsa ājñā bale akiñcana jana \|*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi karilā kīrtana \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On Hari Das Thakur’s order, this helpless soul Bhaktivinoda loudly sings this** ***Harināma-cintāmaṇi*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Here ends the thirteenth chapter of *Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi*, “Offenses in serving the Deity”.

## 15 - Bhajana Pranali

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jaya nityānanda \|*  
*jaya sītānātha jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Gadadhar and Gauranga!**  
**All glories to Nityananda Prabhu!**  
**All glories to the husband of Sita Devi, Advaita Prabhu!**  
**All glories to all of Gauranga’s devotees!**

*saba chāḍi hari-nāma je kare bhajana \|*  
*jaya jaya bhāgyavān sei mahājana \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who renounce everything to commit themselves fully to chanting are great devotees and truly fortunate. May they be forever victorious!**

*prabhu bale hari-dāsa tumi bhakti-bale \|*  
*peyecha sakala jñāna e jagatī-tale \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, “My dear Hari Das, on the strength of your devotion, you have become conversant with all knowledge on this earth.**

*sarva-veda nāce dekhi tomāra jihvāya \|*  
*sakala siddhānta dekhi tomāra kathāya \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I see that all the Vedic revelations dance upon your tongue and that your speech is replete with correct understanding.**

**Комментарий:**

Mahaprabhu says to Hari Das: "You have already spoken to me about the Supreme Person, the jiva, Maya, the pure name of the Lord, Namabhasa and Namaparadh, all in accordance with the Vedic conclusions. Therefore, it is perfectly correct to say that the Vedas dance gleefully upon your tongue.” Now that He has heard all the above from Hari Das, Mahaprabhu will inquire into *rasa*, which is the ultimate prize achieved through the knowledge and practice of the Holy Name.

## An inquiry into rasa

*ebe spaṣṭa bala nāma-rasa ki prakāra \|*  
*ki rūpe labhibe jīva tāhe adhikāra \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Now please clearly instruct Me in the rasa of the Holy Name: How can a jiva become qualified to experience it?”**

*haridāsa mahā-preme kare nivedana \|*  
*tomāra preraṇā-bale kariba varṇana \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Das then very lovingly made his submission to the Lord— I shall describe all these things, confident that You will inspire me.**

## Rasa-tattva

*śuddha-tattva para-tattva yei vastu siddha \|*  
*rasa nāme sarva-vede tāhāi prasiddha \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That substance we have previously called the supreme and purest truth is also celebrated in all the Vedas by the name “rasa” or nectar.**

**Комментарий:**

The aesthetic experience ordinarily described as *rasa* in Sanskrit works of rhetoric is in fact mundane. It is not the true rasa, but its perverted reflection. Matter is made up of twenty-four elements, beyond which lies the spiritual nature of pure being. This transcendental existence is the true rasa. Those who take satisfaction in the self transcend the material existence, but even so are unable to recognize the wonderful varieties that exist in transcendence; they are thus said to be dried up, or “devoid of rasa.” The eternal rasa is found in the transcendental variegatedness of the spiritual world.

*sei se akhaṇḍa rasa para-brahma tattva \|*  
*ananta ānanda-dhāma carama mahattva \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This one and undivided rasa principle is known as the Supreme Brahman, the abode of limitless bliss and the ultimate understanding of God’s glory.**

*śakti śaktimān rūpe viśeṣa tāhāya \|*  
*bheda nāi bheda sama darśanete bhāya \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This single undivided truth manifests as Shakti and Shaktiman, which though not separate truths, appear to be so through the action of** ***viśeṣa*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Hari Das here begins to explain how rasa comes to be experienced. The Supreme Brahman, the indivisible substance in pure being, is naturally manifested in the forms of Shakti and Shaktiman, as energy and energetic. The Supreme Energetic is impossible to perceive or understand \[whereas His energies are visible\], but there is no substantial distinction between the two. Every kind of apparent distinction within the One Truth is caused by the principle known as *viśeṣa*.The Energetic appears eternally as the completely independent Supreme Person. His Shakti reveals His powers. This Shakti manifests in three kinds of phenomena: Chit, Maya and Jiva.

*śaktimān sudurlakṣya śakti prakāśinī \|*  
*trividha śaktira kriyā viśva-vikāśinī \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Shaktiman cannot be perceived except through His Shakti, which is divided into the three functions that manifest all the universes.**

## The Lord’s chit potency reveals the Supreme Substance

*cic-chakti-svarūpe prakāśaye vastu rūpa \|*  
*vastu nāma vastu dhāma tat-kriyā svarūpa \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord’s chit potency reveals the form of the Supreme Substance, His name, His abode, His pastimes and His true identity.**

*kṛṣṇa se parama vastu śyāma tāra rūpa \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-dhāma golokādi līlāra svarūpa \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That Supreme Substance is Krishna. His form is of exquisite dark complexion; His abodes and the sites of His pastimes are Goloka and the other Vaikuntha worlds.**

*nāma dhāma rūpa guṇa līlā ādi yata \|*  
*sakala-i akhaṇḍādvaya jñāna antargata \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Krishna’s name, abode, form, qualities and pastimes and so on are all included within the indivisible, nondual consciousness.**

*vicitratā yata saba parā śakti karma \|*  
*kṛṣṇa dharmī parā śakti kṛṣṇa nitya karma \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever varieties are found there are the work of Krishna’s higher potency, He is the master and supreme actor; the higher potency is His eternal work.**

*dharma-dharmī bheda nāi akhaṇḍa advaye \|*  
*vicitra viśeṣa mātra sac-cin-nilaye \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One and indivisible, there is no true difference between Creator and creation; yet He is characterized by transcendental variety in the spiritual world.**

**Комментарий:**

Krishna is the person (*dharmī*) who is characterized by certain functions (or *dharmas*). His supreme or internal potency is this dharma, or function. There are three kinds of difference, internal, external, and functional. None of these distinctions apply in the case of the Supreme Person and His functions. Nevertheless, the principle known as *viśeṣa* creates the appearance of difference or distinctions, which are manifest in the spiritual world.

## The nature of Krishna’s Maya Shakti

*sei śakti-chāyā eka māyā saṁjñā pāya \|*  
*bahiraṅga viśva sṛje kṛṣṇera icchāya \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The shadow of the spiritual potency is given the name Maya, “illusion.” She has created this material world at Lord Krishna’s behest.**

**Комментарий:**

Maya is only the shadow of Krishna’s supreme, internal potency. Because she is simply the shadow of this energy, Maya is also called the “external energy” of the Lord. She creates this material universe, which is also known as “the abode of the Goddess” (*devī-dhāma*) under the direction of the Lord.

## The Jiva Shakti

*bhedābheda-mayī jīva-śakti jīva-gaṇe \|*  
*tāṭasthye prakāśe kṛṣṇa sevāra kāraṇe \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Both one and different from Krishna is Jiva Shakti, the individual souls. Krishna manifests them as His marginal potency so they can serve Him.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord then manifests the individual souls or jivas, who are the marginal portion of His supreme potency. The jivas are also a particular aspect of Lord Krishna’s Shakti and are therefore meant to serve Him.

## Jivas find themselves in two different situations

*nitya-baddha nitya-mukta jīva dvi-prakāra \|*  
*nitya mukte nitya kṛṣṇa sevā adhikāra \|\|18\|*  
*nitya baddha māyā guṇe karaye saṁsāra \|*  
*bahirmukha antarmukha bhede dvi-prakāra \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are two types of jiva—eternally conditioned or eternally liberated. The eternally liberated jiva is always fit to render service to Krishna, but the conditioned soul is tied up in Maya’s ropes, taking repeated births. Conditioned souls are of two kinds, either inclined inwardly or outwardly.**

**Комментарий:**

Some of the eternally conditioned souls are inwardly inclined, in other words, they make an attempt to find God. The rest are all externally inclined, meaning that their interests lie in everything but Krishna.

*antarmukha sādhu-saṅge kṛṣṇa-nāma pāya \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma-prabhāvete kṛṣṇa-dhāme yāya \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The inwardly inclined search out saintly association and find the Name, then through the power of Krishna’s name attain His supreme abode.**

**Комментарий:**

The most fortunate among the inward-facing jivas come into the association of devotees and take initiation in the Holy Name. Those who are not so fortunate take up the paths of ritual or philosophical speculation, either worshiping numerous gods and goddesses or aspiring to merge into the impersonal Brahman.

## Rasa is the true nature of the Name

*nāma ta akhaṇḍa rasa kalikā tāhāra \|*  
*kṛṣṇa ādi saṁjñā-rūpe viśvete pracāra \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Holy Name is the one unbroken rasa; its budding flower is propagated within this universe in the form of the Lord’s epithets like “Krishna."**

**Комментарий:**

Being of the spiritual nature, or pure goodness, the Supreme Substance is unbroken nectar, or rasa. By His mercy, it appears in the material world in the form of names like Krishna, like a flowerbud inside which the nectarean flower is waiting to fully manifest or blossom.

## Rasa is the essence of the Lord’s form

*svalpa sphuṭa kalikā se rūpa manohara \|*  
*śrī-goloke vṛndāvane śrī-śyāmasundara \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When this bud first starts to open, one sees the Lord’s enchanting form, the beautifully blackish Shyamasundar in Goloka Vrindavan.**

**Комментарий:**

As one enters into a direct experience of the Holy Name, one begins to get a realization of Krishna’s different transcendental forms, all of which are enchanting.

## Rasa is the essence of the Lord’s qualities

*saurabhita kalikā se catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa \|*  
*prakāśe nāmera tattva jānena nipuṇa \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the opening bud starts to release its fragrance, the Holy Name reveals the Lord’s sixty-four qualities; learned experts know this truth.**

**Комментарий:**

As the flowering bud opens further, one can smell its fragrance. Similarly, as the Holy Name reveals itself to the chanter, he or she becomes aware of Krishna’s sixty-four qualities.

## Rasa is the essence of the Lord’s lila

*pūrṇa prasphuṭita nāma kusuma sundara \|*  
*aṣṭa-kāla nitya-līlā prakṛtira para \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the bud of the Name has blossomed completely as a beautiful flower, it is Krishna’s eternal cycle of daily pastimes, completely beyond matter.**

**Комментарий:**

When the flower of the Holy Name is fully bloomed, Krishna’s eternal cycle of daily pastimes, the *aṣṭa-kālīya-līlā*, even though beyond the material nature, appears in this world.

## The nature of devotion

*jīve nāma kṛpodaye svarūpa hlādinī \|*  
*saṁvitera sāra-yutā bhakti-svarūpiṇī \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Holy Name’s mercy to the jiva rises, the Lord’s pleasure potency combines with the essence of His** ***saṁvit śakti*** **in the form of pure devotion... When the individual spirit soul attains the Lord’s mercy, the hladini shakti and samvit shakti combine to become devotional service personified.**

## The workings of bhakti

*āvirbhūta haye nāme prasphuṭita kari \|*  
*rasera sāmagrī prakāśaye sarveśvarī \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**... and descends, making the Holy Name blossom fully. Then, this supreme goddess reveals all ingredients needed to experience rasa fully.**

**Комментарий:**

The potency that rules all becomes manifest and reveals all the ingredients of rasa through the medium of Krishna’s holy name.

*viśuddha cinmaya jīva labhiyā svarūpa \|*  
*sei rase praveśe ei aparūpa \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Completely purified, the transcendental jiva attains his spiritual identity, and enters into that divine rasa. This is wonderful indeed.**

**Комментарий:**

By the power of devotion, the individual soul attains his own original spiritual form. He then enters the rasa-tattva as revealed by this devotional potency.

## The ingredients of rasa: Props

*rasera vibhāva sei tattva ālambana \|*  
*tad-āśraya bhakta, tad-viṣaya kṛṣṇa-dhana \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The media through which rasa is experienced include the props of love, manifest as the vessel of love, the devotee, and the object of love, Krishna.**

**Комментарий:**

The fundamental ingredient mood of rasa is the *sthāyi*-*bhāva,* which also goes by the name of *rati*. This rati becomes rasa when combined with four other elements also known as *bhāva*s. These five elements taken together are called the ingredients of rasa or the media through which rasa is experienced. They are the vi*bhāvas* (the causes of emotion), *anubhāvas* (signs or indicators of emotion through look or gesture), *sāttvika*-*bhāvas* (ecstatic symptoms), and *vyabhicāri*- or *sañcāri*-*bhāvas* (transitory emotions). The *v*i*bhāvas* are divided into two categories: the *ālambanas* (the props or foundations of love) and *uddīpanas* (igniters). The *ālambanas* are of two kinds: *viṣaya* (the object of love) and *āśraya* (the vessel of love or lover). The lover will always be a devotee of Lord Krishna, who is the eternal object of love. The *uddīpanas* are those things that stimulate the feelings of love, such as Krishna’s form and qualities. When the *ālambana* and *uddīpana vibhāvas* act on each other, the result is the appearance of various deliberate acts that are symptomatic of love (*anubhāva*). As these become intensified, they produce the spontaneous ecstasies known as *sāttvikas*. At the same time, the various transitory emotions or *sañcāri-bhāva* also appear.

*nāma kare avirata bhakta mahāśaya \|*  
*kṛpā kari rūpa-guṇa-līlāra udaya \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To the great devotee who chants the Holy Name constantly, the Holy Name mercifully reveals the Lord’s form, qualities and pastimes.**

## The causes of rasa: igniters

*uddīpana kṛṣṇa-rūpa guṇādika yata \|*  
*ālambana uddīpana vibhāve saṁyuta \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ignitersof rasa include Krishna’s form and all His qualities. Both the personal props and igniters are the eternal causes of rasa.**

## From emotional causes to effects

*vibhāva sampūrṇa haile anubhāva haya \|*  
*premera vikāra saba śuddha prema-maya \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When one experiences the causes of rasa fully, one starts to feel its effects. These include all prema’s transformations, all of which are immersed in love.**

## The dominant mood becomes rasa when the causes produce both spontaneous and deliberate effects on the devotee

*sañcārī sāttvika krame udita ha-ile \|*  
*sthāyībhāva rasa haya sarva-śāstra bale \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When deliberate acts of love and spontaneous emotional reactions are added to the dominant mood, it becomes rasa; so say all the scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

Here the functioning of *rasa* may be compared to the working of a juice-extracting machine. *Sthāyi*-*bhāva*, which is also known as *rati* or the permanent emotion, is the honey. Once the machine starts moving by the combined energy of the four *bhāvas*, the permanent emotion (*sthāyi-bhāva*) is converted into *rasa*. In this metaphor, the lover, i.e., the devotee of Lord Krishna, is the taster of the nectar of rasa.

## The process for experiencing rasa

*sei rasa sarva-sāra siddhi-sāra jāni \|*  
*parama puruṣa artha sarva-śāstre māni \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I know that this rasa to be the essence of all creation, the highest achievement; I accept the verdict of the scriptures: it is the ultimate goal of human life.**

**Комментарий:**

Of all *rasa*s, the *rasa*s of Vraja are the highest. They are the highest goal of life for the individual spirit souls. The four traditional goals of life are piety, economic development, sense gratification, and impersonal liberation. These four goals of life, piety, economic development, sense gratification, and impersonal liberation eventually lead to *rasa*, which is the highest goal of life. A person who is completely liberated is eligible to taste the *rasa*s.

*bhakty-unmukha jīva śuddha-gurura kṛpāya \|*  
*śrī-yugala brahma-nāma saubhāgyete pāya \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the mercy of a pure-devotee guru, a soul eager for devotional service has the good fortune to be given the spiritual names of the Divine Couple.**

**Комментарий:**

Of all introspective souls, those who have turned toward the practice of devotional service are the best. It is only as the result of many, many past pious deeds that anyone takes to the path of devotional service. When faith awakens in such a person, he naturally comes into contact with a pure devotee spiritual master (*śuddha*-*sādhu*-*guru*). Then by this spiritual master’s mercy, he is given the Maha Mantra, which contains the names of the Divine Couple.

*tulasī-mālāya nāma saṅkhyā kari’ smare \|*  
*athavā kīrtana kare parama ādare \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then remembers a prescribed number of Names on a tulasi mala, or else he chants the Names aloud with great love and respect.**

**Комментарий:**

Even though someone has faith, he may at first encounter obstacles in the shape of materialistic engagements. The guru entrusts the disciple with a spiritual practice so that he can overcome such obstacles and find strength in chanting. This means worshiping the Lord by either chanting aloud or silently remembering the Holy Name on a rosary of tulasi beads. This process of worship is the secret to all success. One should therefore first spend a little time in a secluded place chanting alone with single-minded concentration. Gradually one increases the number of Holy Names he chants until he achieves constancy in chanting, which is inevitably accompanied by the falling away of obstacles like sense desire.

There are two broad streams of devotional practice: one has the tendency to engage in Deity worship (archan), while the other is in the tendency to engage in chanting (kirtan) and meditation (smaran). Though both of these inclinations are worthwhile, the activities of chanting and meditation are prevalent amongst the most fully committed Vaishnavas in the renounced orders. Many of these great souls meditate on one portion of their daily number of Holy Names on their japa beads and chant the rest aloud. The advantage of chanting aloud is that one simultaneously performs three kinds of devotion: chanting, hearing, and meditation or remembering.

*eka grantha saṅkhyā kari ārambhibe nāma \|*  
*krame tina lakṣa smari pūre manaskāma \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should begin by chanting one** ***grantha*** **(four rounds) of Holy Names, then gradually increase to three lakhs, whereby all desires will be fulfilled.**

*saṅkhyā madhye kichu nāma karibe kīrtana \|*  
*tāte sarvendriya sphūrti ānanda-nartana \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Of the fixed number of Names one chants, a portion should be sung aloud. All the senses will become joyful and one will begin to dance in ecstasy.**

*nāma nava-vidha aṅga karaya āśraya \|*  
*tathāpi kīrtana smṛti sarva-śreṣṭha haya \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even though chanting the Holy Name plays a part in all the nine kinds of bhakti, directly chanting and remembering the Holy Name are the best practices of all.**

## According to one’s individual characteristics, one follows either the path of Deity worship or the path of hearing and chanting

*arcana-mārgete gāḍhatara ruci yāṅra \|*  
*śravaṇa-kīrtana-siddhi tāhāte tāṅhāra \|\|39\|\|*  
*nāme aikāntikī rati ha-ibe yāṅhāra \|*  
*śravaṇa-kīrtana-smṛti kevala tāṅhāra \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who have a decided preference for the path of Deity worship will attain the same perfection as from hearing and chanting. One who has single-minded affection for the Holy Name, only engages in hearing, chanting and remembering.**

## The progression from hearing to chanting to remembering the Holy Name

*sevā nati dāsya sakhya ātma-nivedana \|*  
*sahaje nāmera saṅge haya pravartana \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Service, obeisances, service attitude, friendship and self-surrender all proceed naturally out of the chanting of the Holy Name.**

*nāma-nāmī eka tattva viśvāsa kariyā \|*  
*daśa aparādha chāḍi nirjane basiyā \|\|42\|\|*  
*ati svalpa dine nāma ha-iyā sadaya \|*  
*śrī-śyāmasundara-rūpe hayena udaya \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One who sits down somewhere alone to chant with deep faith in the oneness of the Name and the Named receives the mercy of the Holy Name in a very short time and the Lord appears to him in His form as Shyamasundar.**

**Комментарий:**

Three kinds of people—the materialist, the ritualist and the philosopher— are considered “outwardly directed” (*bahirmukha*), because all of them are engaged in activities based on a false understanding of their true self-interest. The materialist directs his efforts to achieving gratification of this body’s senses. The ritualist aims at sense pleasure in a future lifetime, while the philosopher seeks liberation from all worldly pain. One who overcomes these three positions is said to be “inner-directed” (*antarmukha*). The inner-directed devotees can be subdivided according to three degrees of advancement—neophyte, intermediate, and advanced. The neophyte rejects all demigod worship and serves only Krishna in the deity form, despite still being affected by material motivations. Nevertheless, he is unclear about his own true identity, or that of Krishna and other devotees. Though simple and naive, however, he is not offensive. One can summarize him as being self-centred in his motivation. Therefore, although the neophyte is not a pure Vaishnavas, he is still considered to be “almost a Vaishnava” (*vaiṣṇava-prāya*). On the other hand, the intermediate inward-looking person is a pure Vaishnava, and so of course is the fully-committed advanced introspective person. The most advanced Vaishnava is completely indifferent to material things. No one can be considered truly “inward-looking” unless he is convinced that the Name and the Named are identical. All inward-lookers have exclusive faith in Krishna and are so qualified to chant His holy name.

*yabe nāma-rūpe aikya hayata sādhane \|*  
*nāma laite rūpa āise citte sarva-kṣaṇe \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord’s name and form become one in a devotee’s practice, he then sees the Lord’s form in the heart constantly as he chants.**

*tāra kichu dine rūpe guṇa kari yoga \|*  
*śrī-nāma smaraṇe guṇa karaya sambhoga \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After some time, realization of the Lord’s virtues is added and the devotee also relishes Krishna’s qualities while chanting the Name.**

**Комментарий:**

The step-by-step advancement in chanting takes place as follows. The neophyte inward-looker must in the beginning try to achieve constancy in chanting and remembering the Holy Name by rejecting the ten offenses. This chanting and remembering should be done while pronouncing the Holy Name clearly and distinctly. When one’s chanting is clear, steady, and blissful, he should meditate on the Lord’s Shyamasundar form. Engaging the hands by counting the Names on one’s beads, one should again and again direct the mind and tongue to the name of Krishna itself. Then, with his spiritual eye, the practitioner will be able to clearly visualize the Lord’s form, which is the meaning of the Holy Name. Alternatively, one may sit in front of the Lord’s deity form and drink in the beautiful sight of the Lord with the eyes while simultaneously chanting and meditating on the Holy Name.

Even after reaching the stage where the Holy Name and the form of the Lord have become one, the aspirant must practice remembering the Lord’s transcendental qualities. When one has come to experience the unity of the Lord’s name, form and qualities, then one can turn one’s attention to meditating on the Lord’s lilas in “still-picture” form (*mantra-dhyāna-mayī līlā*), gradually merging this meditation into that of the name, form and qualities. At this point in his practice, the devotee starts to experience rasa. This experience of rasa becomes complete when he has mastered the still-picture form of meditation on the Lord’s pastimes and turned to the “moving-picture” form, meditating on the Lord’s pastimes in the eternal world as they change over the periods of the day (*svārasikī aṣṭa-kālīyā līlā*).

At the beginning of this cultivation of devotion, the devotee is a mere neophyte. If he finds advanced association, he quickly attains the intermediate stage and finally becomes advanced himself. In the neophyte stage, one will chant the mere semblance of the Holy Name (Namabhasa), but once one’s contaminations are eradicated, one quickly becomes capable of chanting the pure Holy Name and eligible to serve the pure Vaishnavas.

## The unity of the Lord’s name, form and qualities

*svalpa-dine nāma rūpa guṇa eka haya \|*  
*nāma laite sarva-kṣaṇe tinera udaya \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In a very short time, the Lord’s name, form and qualities are seen as one, and as the devotee chants the Name, all three manifest themselves to him.**

## Worship – “still-picture” meditation

*mantra dhyāna mayī ei nāma upāsanā \|*  
*prāthamika dhārā jāni’ kare vibhāvanā \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This stage of worshiping the Name is called “still-picture meditation”; one contemplates, knowing it to be the preliminary wave of Krishna’s lila.**

*smṛti-kāle yoga-pīṭhe kalpa-druma-tale \|*  
*gopa-gopī-vṛte kṛṣṇe dekhe kutūhale \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While meditating, the devotee visualizes the Yoga Pith under a desire tree where Krishna is joyfully surrounded by cowherds and milkmaids.**

*sāttvika-vikāra saba haya prasphuṭita \|*  
*bhajana ānande bhakta haya pulakita \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While meditating in this way, the devotee experiences ecstatic symptoms and his body trembles all over in the joys of bhajan.**

*krame yabe nāma sva-saurabhe praphullita \|*  
*aṣṭa-kāla kṛṣṇa-līlā ha-ibe udita \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Name eventually blossoms to its most fragrant fullness, one is able to visualize the constantly changing daily activities of the Lord.**

## Worship – “moving-picture” meditation

*svārasikī upāsanā ha-ibe udaya \|*  
*līlocita pīṭhe kṛṣṇe darśana karaya \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Meditation on these pastimes is known as** ***svārasikī*** **or “flowing” worship; it shows the devotee Krishna in each place according to His pastimes.**

*saṅge saṅge guru-kṛpā siddha-svarūpete \|*  
*līlāya praveśe bhakta sakhīra saṅgete \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the guru’s blessings, the practitioner immediately enters these pastimes in an eternal spiritual form, accompanying one of the sakhis.**

*mahābhāva svarūpiṇī vṛṣabhānu-sutā \|*  
*tāṅra anugata bhakti sadā prema-yutā \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vrishabhanu’s daughter, Radha, embodies the greatest love** ***mahābhāva*****; devotion that follows Her mood and direction is always filled with loving ecstasy.**

**Комментарий:**

Though there are five main rasas or spiritual mellows—shanta (neutrality), dasya (servitorship), sakhya (friendship), vatsalya (parenthood), and shringar or madhurya (conjugal)—the last of these is the highest. Devotees eligible to enter this mood are recipients of Sri Krishna Chaitanya’s special mercy. Lord Krishna has many yutheshwaris or female group leaders who serve Him in the conjugal mood, but Srimati Radharani must be supplicated by all. She is the direct manifestation of Lord Krishna’s svarupa shakti or spiritual potency, and all the other Vraja gopis are simply Her expansions. It is therefore the goal of every rasika devotee to seek entry into Her yutha or group. Service to Lord Krishna in Vraja is impossible without first taking shelter of the Vraja gopis. Thus it is necessary to enter Srimati Radharani’s camp and serve there under Lalita Devi’s direction.

*sakhī ājñā mate kare yugala-sevana \|*  
*mahā-preme magna haya se rasika jana \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The rasika devotee then becomes absorbed in the highest prema, serving the Divine Couple under the sakhis’ directions.**

## When the subtle body is overcome, one attains concrete perfection (vastu-siddhi)

*sādhana-bhajana-siddhi lāgālāgi tāya \|*  
*liṅga-bhaṅge vastu-siddhi tomāra kṛpāya \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At this stage, one’s practice of bhajan borders success. When, by Your grace, all subtle identifications fall away, one attains concrete perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

As the devotee follows this method of cultivating rasa, the gap between his practice and perfection in bhajan narrows. Within a very short time, he realizes his spiritual identity, which is known as svarupa siddhi*.* Krishna’s will to elevate the practitioner naturally follows the mercy of the yutheshwari. Once that comes about, the illusory subtle body that was the result of turning away from Krishna is easily destroyed and the jiva gains entrance into Vraja in her original spiritual form. This is called vastu siddhi*.*

## What follows is beyond description; it is only accessible to direct perception

*ihāra adhika āra vākya nāhi cale \|*  
*tad uttara anubhava labhi kṛpā-bale \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**More than this, words are no longer effective; what lies beyond can only be experienced by the grace of Krishna.**

**Комментарий:**

Up to this point, things can be effectively explained by words. Beyond it, the emotional state of devotion cannot be effectively described. Krishna’s mercy alone makes it possible to experience.

*ei ta ujjvala rasa parama sādhana \|*  
*ihāte niścaya mile kṛṣṇa prema dhana \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the practice that leads to the realization of ujjvala rasa, through which the highest wealth of Krishna prema is attained.**

**Комментарий:**

The madhura rasa is also known as ujjvala (“illuminated, brilliant”) rasa, because in the spiritual world it is the most brilliant state. We can understand this rasa by hearing and chanting about Krishna’s pastimes in this world.

## The cultivation of eleven characteristics

*sādhite ujjvala-rasa āche bhāva ekādaśa*  
*sambandha vayasa nāma rūpa \|*  
*yūtha veśa ājñā-vāsa sevā parākāṣṭhāśvāsa*  
*pālya-dāsī ei aparūpa \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Needed to cultivate the most brilliant love for Krishna are eleven characteristics: relationship, age, name, physical appearance, membership in a group, specific dress, a mission, an address, a specific service, the aspiration for an ultimate goal, and the inner identification as a protected maidservant.**

**Комментарий:**

Ramananda Ray said:

*ata eva gopī bhāva kari aṅgīkāra \|*  
*rātri dina cinte rādhā kṛṣṇera vihāra \|\|*  
*siddha dehe cinti kare tāhāṅñi sevana \|*  
*sakhī bhāve pāya rādhā kṛṣṇera caraṇa \|\|*  
*gopī ānugatya vinā aiśvarya jñāne \|*  
*bhajileha nāhi pāya vrajendra nandane \|\|*

**One cannot obtain Krishna in Goloka Vrindavan simply by serving him according to regulative principles. Therefore one should accept the mood of the gopis and meditate on the pastimes of Sri Radha and Krishna both night and day. One should meditate on one’s eternal spiritual body and use it to serve Radha and Krishna; by so doing one will attain the direct association of their lotus feet. If one does not follow in the footsteps of the gopis and remains absorbed in the consciousness of the Lord’s majesty, he cannot attain the service of the lotus feet of the son of Nanda Maharaj, even though he may be engaged in devotional service. (CC 2.8.227-9)**

One whose natural tendency is to cultivate the conjugal rasa should definitely adopt the female form and attitude of a gopi in Vrindavan. No living entity can enter the conjugal mood of Vraja in a masculine mood or body. Only when one has adopted the identity of a gopi can she truly worship Krishna. This identity is composed of eleven aspects. Thus only one who has adopted these eleven attitudes can be said to have taken a gopi identity. They are:

1\. *sambandha* (a specific relationship with Radharani in Vrindavan),

2\. *vayas* (age, such as 12 years, 6 months, 10 days),

3\. *nāma* (a name, such as Kamala Manjari),

4\. *rūpa* (bodily appearance, usually expressed in terms of bodily hue, such as the color of lightning),

5\. *yūtha-praveśa* (entry into a particular group under the command of one of Radharani’s intimate friends, such as Lalita),

6\. *veśa* (a dress of a particular style and color, such as a blue cloth covered in stars),

7\. *ājñā* (a specific instruction),

8\. *vāsa-sthāna* (a place of residence, such as Svananda-sukhada Kunj),

9\. *sevā* (a specific service, such as providing the Divine Couple with camphor),

10\. *parākāṣṭhā* (the ambition to attain a particular blessing, such as being directly asked to do something by Sri Rupa Manjari),

11\. *pālya-dāsī-bhāva* (the mood of a completely dependent maidservant, under the tutelage of one of Radharani’s sakhis).

Whatever one’s identity in this world, one should internally adopt a spiritual identity in these eleven aspects and worship Radha and Krishna directly in that form.

## Five stages in the cultivation of the gopi identity\[106\]

*ei ekādaśa bhāva sampūrṇa sādhane \|*  
*pañca daśā lakṣya haya sādhaka jīvane \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The complete realization of these eleven aspects of one’s spiritual identity develops through five recognizable stages in the life of a practitioner.**

*śravaṇa varaṇa āra smaraṇa āpana \|*  
*sampatti e pañca-vidha daśāya gaṇana \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These five stages are the following: (1) hearing, (2) acceptance, (3) remembering, (4) appropriation and (5) full possession.**

**Комментарий:**

As one progresses in the cultivation of one’s spiritual identity, five stages are progressively crossed. These are *śravaṇa-daśā* “the state of hearing*,*” *varaṇa-daśā* “the state of accepting*,*”*, smaraṇa-daśā* “the state of remembering,” *āpana-daśā* “the state of appropriation, i.e., making it one’s own*,*” *sampatti-daśā* “the state of full possession.”

*sei gopī bhāvāmṛte jāṅra lobha haya \|*  
*veda dharma loka tyaji se kṛṣṇe bhajaya \|\|*  
*vraja lokera kona bhāva lañā yei bhaje \|*  
*bhāva yogya deha pāñā kṛṣṇa pāya vraje \|\|*

**One who has a strong desire to taste the nectar of the gopīs’ loving mood abandons all consideration of the religious principles of the Veda. He cares not for public opinion, but simply worships Krishna... Whoever worships Krishna in any one of the moods of the residents of Vraja will attain a suitable spiritual body to serve Him there.” (CC 2.8.219, 221)**

With these words, Ramananda Raya taught that any practitioner who wishes to cultivate the ujjvala rasa absolutely must take a gopi body. When one hears these pastimes of Lord Krishna and becomes attracted to this particular mood, he must approach a genuine spiritual master to learn the above-named eleven aspects of his spiritual identity. Hearing the spiritual master delineate this identity in theory is called *śravaṇa-daśā* (the stage of hearing). When the disciple eagerly accepts this identification, that is called *varaṇa-daśā* (the stage of accepting). When she cultivates this mood and identity by meditating on rasa in all its aspects, then she is situated on the stage of remembering (*smaraṇa-daśā*). When she has fully assimilated that identity, which has now been made fully clear, she has reached the state called *āpana-daśā* or *prāpti-daśā*. Finally, when she becomes entirely separated from this earthly existence and eternally fixed in her longed-for spiritual form, she has attained *sampatti-daśā,* the stage of full possession.

## The first of these: the hearing stage

*nijāpekṣā śreṣṭha śuddha-bhāvuka ye jana \|*  
*bhāva-mārge gurudeva sei mahājana \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The spiritual master on this path of cultivating the spiritual identy is a great ecstatic, more advanced than oneself, and pure in his mood.**

*tāṅhāra śrī-mukhe bhāva-tattvera śravaṇa \|*  
*ha-ile śravaṇa-daśā haya prakaṭana \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On hearing from his divine lips the theoretical principles of this path, the aspirant enters the stage of hearing or** ***śravaṇa-daśā*****.**

## The principles of bhava marg

*bhāva-tattva dvi-prakāra karaha vicāra \|*  
*nija ekādaśa bhāva kṛṣṇa-līlā āra \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The theory of this bhava marg has two components— one, the eleven aspects of the practitioner’s identity, the other, Krishna’s lila.**

## Advancing to the stage of acceptance

*rādhā-kṛṣṇa aṣṭa-kāla yei līlā kare \|*  
*tāhāra śravaṇe lobha haya ataḥpare \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the aspiring devotee hears about Radha and Krishna’s ashta kaliya lila*****,*** **he starts to feel an intense desire one to join Them in Their activities.**

*lobha ha-ile guru-pade jijñāsā udaya \|*  
*kemane pāiba līlā kaha mahāśaya \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Possessed by this desire (*****lobha*****), he asks the spiritual master, “O great soul, what must I do to enter these pastimes?”**

*gurudeva kṛpā kari karibe varṇana \|*  
*līlā-tattve ekādaśa bhāva-saṅghaṭana \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The spiritual master then mercifully describes to his disciple the eleven aspects of identity and how they relate to the Lord’s lila.**

*prasanna ha-iyā prabhu karibe ādeśa \|*
